GoodRx Help Guide

GoodRx’s own help documentation, gathered into one document

2026-08-02

GoodRx Help Guide

This guide gathers GoodRx’s own help documentation, as it stood on 2 August 2026.

How this was gathered. GoodRx’s rendered help pages refuse automated reading; its own listing answered. All 251 articles arrived carrying their full text.

Two warnings, and they matter more here than in most volumes.

First, this is not clinical advice. GoodRx is a discount and price-comparison service. What follows explains how coupons, memberships and pharmacy participation work. It says nothing about whether a medicine is right for you, how to take it, or what it interacts with. For any of that, ask a pharmacist or the prescriber.

Second, prices and terms change constantly. Coupon values, which pharmacies participate, and what a membership includes are all moving targets, and this guide is a snapshot taken on the date above. Check the live service before relying on a price.

This document holds 44 categories, 251 articles and about 95,472 words.

About Companion

GoodRx Companion Terms of Service

GoodRx Companion Terms of Service

Effective Date: Jul 16, 2026

Welcome to GoodRx Companion, a service provided by GoodRx, Inc. (“GoodRx”). Please read these Terms of Service (these “Terms”) carefully and in their entirety. Along with the GoodRx Terms of Use and Privacy Policy applicable to your use of GoodRx’s website, the GoodRx Companion-specific website, goodrx.com/Companion (the “Site”), anRd the GoodRx mobile application (the “App”), which are incorporated herein by reference (collectively, the “Terms of Use”), these Terms set forth the legally binding terms and conditions that govern your use of the Services (as defined below). You acknowledge that by using or accessing the Services, you are accepting and consenting to be bound by these Terms. In the event of any conflict between the Terms of Use and these Terms as it relates to the Services, these Terms shall take precedence. If you do not agree with all of the provisions of these Terms, you may not access or use the Services. 

IMPORTANT: PLEASE REVIEW THE ARBITRATION AGREEMENT SET FORTH BELOW CAREFULLY, AS IT WILL REQUIRE YOU TO RESOLVE DISPUTES WITH GOODRX ON AN INDIVIDUAL BASIS THROUGH FINAL AND BINDING ARBITRATION. BY ENTERING THIS AGREEMENT, YOU EXPRESSLY ACKNOWLEDGE THAT YOU HAVE READ AND UNDERSTAND ALL OF THE TERMS OF THIS AGREEMENT AND HAVE TAKEN TIME TO CONSIDER THE CONSEQUENCES OF THIS IMPORTANT DECISION.

About GoodRx CompanionGoodRx Companion is NOT health insurance. GoodRx Companion is not intended as a substitute for insurance, and does not meet any creditable coverage requirements under any state law. GoodRx Companion cannot be combined with federal or state-funded programs such as Medicare or Medicaid.

GoodRx offers discounts on prescriptions through GoodRx’s website, the Site, the App, and discount cards issued to customers (“Cards”) accessing such website or App. GoodRx Companion is a premium program that provides members (“Members” and your status as a Member is referred to as your “Membership”) even bigger discounts on prescriptions, online provider visits, and other services through the Site, the App, and Cards, and by email messages, which you may elect to receive or opt out of receiving, sent to the email address associated with your account (collectively, the “Services”).  GoodRx Companion is currently not available in Washington and discounted online provider visits are not available to Companion Members in Oklahoma at this time.  

The list of eligible medications available at discounted or promotional pricing (including any “free” medications) is subject to change at any time without notice.  Discounted pricing for healthcare provider visits is currently unavailable to Members in Oklahoma. Pricing and availability of certain non-insurance benefits (including dental, vision, laboratory services, and other offerings) may vary by state and are subject to applicable regulatory requirements. 

GoodRx Companion provides discounts at participating pharmacies, but GoodRx does not pay pharmacies on your behalf. You are obligated to pay for all services provided. You may obtain a current list of participating Companion pharmacies here or by calling 844-595-5222.  GoodRx Companion provides an open formulary.

GoodRx Companion also offers access to discounted healthcare provider services to Members, except for those residing in Oklahoma. The telehealth services are provided by independent, physician-owned medical groups with a network of U.S.-licensed healthcare providers (“Providers”) who provide clinical telehealth services (“Telehealth Services”) at a discounted rate to Members. GoodRx Companion only provides discounts from participating Providers. The range of discounts will vary depending on the type of Telehealth Service received. GoodRx does not provide medical advice or care, nor does it employ or pay the Providers that provide Telehealth Services. GoodRx Companion provides an online communication platform for the Providers and their patients to connect through the use of asynchronous and synchronous communication technologies. The Providers, and not GoodRx, are responsible for the quality and appropriateness of the care they render to you. You are responsible for all fees due for the Telehealth Services, including any fees charged by the healthcare organization(s) or Provider(s). The list of Providers, which may be updated from time to time, but no less frequently than every six (6) months, can be found here or you may call 1-415-237-1378. Providers provide telehealth services commensurate with their scope of practice. Once you are a Member, you will have access to telemedicine visits for supported services without any waiting period. The list of telemedicine services, which may be updated from time to time and may vary based on your location, can be found here. You agree that your use of the Site, App, or Services for Telehealth Services is further subject to the (a) Consent to Telehealth Services and (b) Notice of Privacy Practices of the independent medical group providing Telehealth Services.

GoodRx Companion is provided by GoodRx, Inc., located at 2701 Olympic Blvd, West Building Suite 200, Santa Monica, CA 90404. Please reach out to 1-855-487-0694 for assistance with questions, an up-to-date list of participating pharmacies or providers, or to request specific contact information for your department of insurance or service of process information for a specific state.

For Rhode Island residents

Free Trial; Billing. GoodRx Companion offers a seven (7)-day free trial for eligible users. Following the free trial period, your Membership will automatically renew and you will be billed $14.99 per month, or $119.88 annually, to the payment method on file unless and until you cancel. You may cancel your Membership at any time by visiting your account settings and selecting “Cancel Membership” at least one (1) day prior to your next billing date to avoid future charges. 

Your Membership

In order to participate in the Services, you must first register for an account with GoodRx (your “Account”) in accordance with GoodRx’s Terms of Use. You must then complete the registration process necessary to become a Member by following the instructions set forth in the GoodRx Companion application and providing certain information to GoodRx, such as a payment method (“Payment Method”), which you may edit through the Site under “My Account” and then “Settings”, and your name, email address, date of birth, mailing address, pharmacy, prescriptions, and any other information as requested by GoodRx (“Registration Data”).  You agree that you will maintain and update this information as required in order to keep it current, complete, and accurate. At this time, your membership benefits will only work with a prescription under your name, and any prescriptions must match the name and date of birth of the account holder. 

You also grant us the right to disclose to third parties certain Registration Data about you for the purpose of administering the program. GoodRx’s collection and use of personally identifiable information in connection with the Services is governed by GoodRx’s Privacy Policy, which is incorporated into these Terms by reference.

You agree to fully, accurately, and truthfully create your GoodRx Account when providing the Registration Data. Your GoodRx Account and credentials are personal to you, and you are solely responsible for maintaining the confidentiality of your GoodRx Account or credentials, and for all activities that occur under such GoodRx Account or credentials. You agree to prohibit anyone else from using your GoodRx Account or credentials and agree to immediately notify GoodRx of any actual or suspected unauthorized use of your GoodRx Account or credentials or other security concerns of which you become aware. You must be at least 18 years of age to become a Member. Free trial eligibility is determined by GoodRx at its sole discretion. We reserve the right to revoke the free trial and put your account on hold in the event that we determine that you are ineligible for a free trial offer. We may use information such as device ID, method of payment, or an account email address used with an existing or recent GoodRx membership to determine eligibility. For combinations with other offers, restrictions may apply. To view more details about your plan, visit our Site and after logging in, click the “Accounts” link to view your Account Settings page and click the Membership tab. You may also open the App, log in, tap on the “Account” tab, then tap “Membership.” 

GoodRx may change the price of your Membership from time to time, and will communicate any price changes to you via the email associated with your Membership in advance of any price increase, and if applicable, how to accept those changes. Price changes will take effect at the start of the next Membership Cycle following the date of the price change. Subject to applicable law, you accept the new price by continuing to use the GoodRx Services after the price change takes effect. If you do not agree with a price change, you have the right to reject the change by unsubscribing from the Services prior to the price change going into effect.

Use of the Services

Access to Information

Information about your Prescriptions. Most prescriptions purchased in the United States, including prescriptions filled through the use of discount coupons, loyalty cards, or insurance copays, result in the pharmacy reporting patient data back to the company that provides the benefit (“Claims Data”). GoodRx uses and stores this Claims Data linked to the information you provide when using the Services (including, without limitation, your name, email address, prescription names, and your address) to provide you with coupons and discounts on prescriptions through your Account.  

If you cancel your Membership, you may request that we delete your Claims Data by calling 1-855-487-0694 or by submitting a request via goodrx.com/requestdeletion. 

 Eligible Users

The Services are offered and available to users who are 18 years of age or older. By using the Services, you represent and warrant that you are of legal age to form a binding contract with GoodRx and meet all of the foregoing eligibility requirements. The Services are offered and available to users who reside in the United States. If you do not meet all of these requirements, you must not access or use the Services.

Information about your Telemedicine Visits 

Information regarding your telemedicine visit is provided to us, stored, and added to your Account. Through the tools in your Account you can also access messages regarding your visit, as well as medical history and other details pertaining to your telemedicine visit when you log in to GoodRx Care, . 

Restrictions on Use

You agree not to use the Services in a way that is, in GoodRx’s sole discretion, harmful to others (including minors) in any way or violates any applicable law, regulation, obligation, or other similar restrictions imposed by a government entity. You also agree not to violate any community requirements periodically posted by GoodRx, remove any copyright, trademark, or other proprietary notices from any portion of the Services (including any discount coupons or other Member-only content sent to you), cause or launch any programs or scripts for the purpose of scraping, indexing, surveying, or otherwise data mining any portion of the Services, or attempt to gain unauthorized access to, or impair any aspect of the Services, or its related systems or networks (or to other computer systems or networks connected to, or used together with the Services), whether through password mining or any other means. You shall promptly notify GoodRx if you learn of any security breach related to the Services.

You agree that any prescriptions or pharmaceutical products that you acquire or obtain through the Site or Services will be solely for your (or your family member’s or dependent’s, as applicable to whom the prescription is written for) personal use. GoodRx may cancel, suspend, or terminate your access to the Site and the Services at any time, and for any reason, including any violation of these Terms.

 

Dispute Resolution

By agreeing to these Terms, you agree that you are required to resolve any claim that you may have against GoodRx on an individual basis in arbitration, as set forth in this Arbitration Agreement. This will preclude you from bringing any class, collective, or representative action against GoodRx (except as noted below under “Batch Arbitration”), and also preclude you from participating in or recovering relief under any current or future class, collective, consolidated, or representative action brought against GoodRx by someone else.

 Agreement to Binding Arbitration Between You and GoodRx

You and GoodRx agree that any dispute, claim or controversy arising out of or relating to (a) these Terms or the existence, breach, termination, enforcement, interpretation or validity thereof, or (b) your access to or use of the Services at any time, whether before or after the date you agreed to the Terms, will be settled by binding arbitration between you and GoodRx, and not in a court of law. Both parties further acknowledge and agree to waive any right to a trial by jury. For the avoidance of doubt, the scope of arbitration includes federal and state statutory and common law claims, including under the consumer protection laws and principles.

Class Action and Collective Relief Waiver

EXCEPT AS SET FORTH BELOW PURSUANT TO THE SECTION ENTITLED “BATCH ARBITRATION,” YOU AND GOODRX ACKNOWLEDGE AND AGREE THAT, TO THE MAXIMUM EXTENT ALLOWED BY LAW, THERE SHALL BE NO RIGHT OR AUTHORITY FOR ANY DISPUTE TO BE ARBITRATED OR LITIGATED ON A CLASS, JOINT, COLLECTIVE OR CONSOLIDATED BASIS, OR IN A PURPORTED REPRESENTATIVE CAPACITY ON BEHALF OF THE GENERAL PUBLIC (SUCH AS CLAIMS AS A PRIVATE ATTORNEY GENERAL OR FOR PUBLIC INJUNCTIVE RELIEF), UNLESS BOTH YOU AND GOODRX OTHERWISE AGREE IN WRITING. THE ARBITRATOR MAY NOT PRESIDE OVER ANY FORM OF ANY CLASS, JOINT, COLLECTIVE OR REPRESENTATIVE PROCEEDING. THE ARBITRATOR MAY AWARD RELIEF (INCLUDING ANY DECLARATORY OR INJUNCTIVE RELIEF) ONLY IN FAVOR OF THE INDIVIDUAL PARTY SEEKING RELIEF AND ONLY TO THE EXTENT NECESSARY TO RESOLVE AN INDIVIDUAL PARTY’S CLAIM. THE ARBITRATOR MAY NOT AWARD RELIEF FOR OR AGAINST ANYONE WHO IS NOT A PARTY TO THE PROCEEDING.

This Class Action and Collective Relief Waiver are an essential part of this “Dispute Resolution” section, and if it is deemed invalid or unenforceable with respect to a particular claim or dispute, neither you nor GoodRx may arbitrate such claim or dispute. Notwithstanding the foregoing, if a court or arbitrator determines that this is not enforceable as to a particular claim or request for relief, and all appeals from that decision have been exhausted (or the decision is otherwise final), then the parties agree that that particular claim or request for relief may proceed in court but shall be severed and stayed pending arbitration of the remaining claims. This provision does not prevent you or GoodRx from participating in a class-wide settlement of claims.

Notwithstanding the foregoing, you and GoodRx each retain the right to (i) elect to have any claims resolved in small claims court on an individual basis for disputes and actions within the scope of such court’s jurisdiction; (ii) bring an action in state or federal court to protect its intellectual property rights (“intellectual property rights” means patents, copyrights, moral rights, trademarks, and trade secrets and other confidential or proprietary information, but not privacy or publicity rights); and (iii) seek a declaratory judgment, injunction, or other equitable relief in a court of competent jurisdiction regarding whether a party’s claims are time-barred or may be brought in small claims court. Seeking such relief shall not waive a party’s right to arbitration under this agreement, and any filed arbitrations related to any action filed pursuant to this paragraph shall automatically be stayed pending the outcome of such action.  

Mandatory Informal Dispute Resolution

If a dispute arises, GoodRx is committed to working with you to reach a reasonable resolution. For any issue or dispute that arises between you and GoodRx, both parties acknowledge and agree that they will first make a good faith effort to resolve it informally before initiating any formal dispute resolution proceeding in arbitration or otherwise. This includes first sending a written description of the dispute to the other party. For any dispute you initiate, you agree to send the written description of the dispute along with the email address associated with your account to legal@goodrx.com with the subject line “User Dispute.” The notification shall include the nature of the dispute, the product or service to which your dispute pertains (a free GoodRx account, a GoodRx Companion Membership, a GoodRx Care telehealth visit, etc.), the date(s) of any disputed charges, the relief sought, and the party’s contact information (phone, address, email). 

You and GoodRx then agree to negotiate in good faith about the dispute through email and telephonic means. If the dispute is not resolved satisfactorily through this informal process within sixty (60) days after receipt of the written description of the dispute, you and GoodRx agree to the further dispute resolution provisions below.

Please note that the aforementioned informal dispute resolution process is a prerequisite and condition precedent to commencing any formal dispute resolution proceeding. The parties agree that any relevant limitations period and filing fee or other deadlines will be tolled while the parties engage in this informal dispute resolution process. Failure to engage in this process could result in the award of fees against you in arbitration.

 Arbitration Rules and Governing Law

In the event the parties are unable to resolve the dispute via the “Mandatory Dispute Resolution Process” as set forth above, the parties may proceed with arbitration. If you are an individual, the arbitration will be administered by the American Arbitration Association (“AAA”) in accordance with the AAA’s Consumer Arbitration Rules and the Supplementary Procedures for Consumer Related Disputes then in effect, except as modified by this Arbitration Agreement. If you are a business or legal entity other than an individual, then the arbitration will be administered by AAA in accordance with the AAA’s Commercial Arbitration Rules then in effect (and together with the AAA’s Consumer Arbitration Rules and the Supplementary Procedures for Consumer Related Disputes, the “AAA Rules”), except as modified by this Arbitration Agreement. The AAA Rules are available at www.adr.org.

The parties agree that the arbitrator (“Arbitrator”), and not any federal, state, or local court or agency, shall have exclusive authority to resolve any disputes relating to the interpretation, applicability, enforceability or formation of this Arbitration Agreement, including any claim that all or any part of this Arbitration Agreement is void or voidable. The Arbitrator shall also be responsible for determining all threshold arbitrability issues, including issues relating to whether the Terms are unconscionable or illusory and any defense to arbitration, including waiver, delay, laches, or estoppel.

Notwithstanding any choice of law or other provision in the Terms, the parties agree and acknowledge that this Arbitration Agreement evidences a transaction involving interstate commerce and that the Federal Arbitration Act, 9 U.S.C. § 1 et seq. (“FAA”), will govern its interpretation and enforcement and proceedings pursuant thereto. It is the intent of the parties that the FAA and AAA Rules shall preempt all state laws to the fullest extent permitted by law. If the FAA and AAA Rules are found to not apply to any issue that arises under this Arbitration Agreement or the enforcement thereof, then that issue shall be resolved under the laws of the state of California.

Process

If you desire to initiate arbitration, you must provide GoodRx with a written Demand for Arbitration as specified in the AAA Rules. (The AAA provides an applicable form Demand for both Consumer and Commercial disputes at www.adr.org). Additionally, your Demand for Arbitration shall set forth and allege sufficient facts in support of a legally-recognized cause of action, along with the amount of recovery sought in connection with each associated cause of action. In the event the Demand for Arbitration fails to adequately plead a cause of action or associated recovery as set forth herein, the Demand for Arbitration and/or cause of action shall be subject to dismissal by the Arbitrator. The Arbitrator will be either (1) a retired judge or (2) an attorney specifically licensed to practice law in the state of California and will be selected by the parties from either (i) the AAA’s roster of consumer arbitrators for disputes with individuals, or (ii) the AAA’s roster of commercial arbitrators for businesses and other legal entities. If the parties are unable to agree upon an Arbitrator within fourteen (14) days of delivery of the Demand for Arbitration, then the AAA will appoint the Arbitrator in accordance with the AAA Rules.

Batch Arbitration

To increase the efficiency of administration and resolution of arbitrations, in the event 100 or more similar arbitration demands (those asserting the same or substantially similar facts, and seeking the same or substantially similar relief) presented by or with the assistance or coordination of the same law firm(s) or organization(s) are submitted to AAA against GoodRx within reasonably close proximity in time, the arbitration provider shall (i) administer the arbitration demands in batches of 100 demands per batch (to the extent there are fewer than 100 arbitration demands left over after the batching described above, a final batch will consist of the remaining demands); (ii) designate one Arbitrator for each batch; and (iii) provide for a single fee due per side for any filing, administrative, or Arbitrator fees per batch. Arbitrator selection for each batch shall be conducted to the greatest extent possible in accordance with the applicable AAA Rules for such selection, and the Arbitrator will determine the location where the proceedings will be conducted. You agree to cooperate in good faith with GoodRx and the arbitration provider to implement such a “batch approach” or other similar approach to provide for an efficient resolution of claims, including the payment of single filing and administrative fees for batches of claims. This “Batch Arbitration” provision shall in no way be interpreted as authorizing class arbitration of any kind. GoodRx does not agree or consent to class arbitration, private attorney general arbitration, or arbitration involving joint or consolidated claims under any circumstances, except as set forth in this section.

 

Location and Procedure

Unless you and GoodRx otherwise agree, the arbitration will be conducted in the county where you reside.  You agree to attend the arbitration hearing in-person, even if you are represented by counsel.  If the claim does not exceed $10,000, then the arbitration will be conducted solely on the basis of documents you and GoodRx submit to the Arbitrator, unless you request a hearing or the Arbitrator determines that a hearing is necessary. If the claim exceeds $10,000, your right to a hearing will be determined by the applicable AAA Rules. Subject to the applicable AAA Rules, the Arbitrator will have the discretion to direct a reasonable exchange of information by the parties, consistent with the expedited nature of the arbitration.

Arbitrator’s Decision

The Arbitrator will render an award within the time frame specified in the applicable AAA Rules. Judgment on the arbitration award may be entered in any court having competent jurisdiction to do so. The Arbitrator may award declaratory or injunctive relief only in favor of the claimant and only to the extent necessary to provide relief warranted by the claimant’s individual claim. An Arbitrator’s decision shall be final and binding on all parties. If a party prevails in arbitration, that party will be entitled to an award of attorneys’ fees and expenses, to the extent provided under applicable law.  For disputes involving individuals (as opposed to businesses or other legal entities) under this Arbitration Agreement, GoodRx will not seek, and hereby waives all rights GoodRx may have under applicable law to recover attorneys’ fees and expenses if GoodRx prevails in arbitration. For disputes involving business or other legal entities under this Arbitration Agreement, GoodRx shall be entitled to recover its attorneys’ fees and expenses if GoodRx prevails in arbitration.

 

Fees

It is your responsibility to pay your portion of any AAA filing, administrative, and arbitrator fees as set forth in the applicable AAA Rules. To the extent the filing fee for the arbitration exceeds the cost of filing a lawsuit, the Arbitrator may require GoodRx to pay the portion of that fee that exceeds the cost of filing suit.  However, if the Arbitrator finds that either the substance of your claim or the relief sought in your Demand for Arbitration was frivolous or was brought for an improper purpose (as measured by the standards set forth in Federal Rule of Civil Procedure 11(b)), then the Arbitrator may determine that you are solely responsible for the entirety of the AAA filing, administrative, and Arbitrator fees.

You are responsible for your own attorneys’ fees and any remaining AAA filing, administrative and arbitrator fees unless the AAA Rules and/or applicable law provides otherwise.  

Severability and Survival

If any portion of this Arbitration Agreement is found to be unenforceable or unlawful for any reason, (1) the unenforceable or unlawful provision shall be severed from these Terms; (2) severance of the unenforceable or unlawful provision shall have no impact whatsoever on the remainder of the Arbitration Agreement or the parties’ ability to compel arbitration of any remaining claims on an individual basis pursuant to the Arbitration Agreement; and (3) to the extent that any claims must therefore proceed on a class, collective, consolidated, or representative basis, such claims must be litigated in a civil court of competent jurisdiction and not in arbitration, and the parties agree that litigation of those claims shall be stayed pending the outcome of any individual claims in arbitration.

Cancellation of Membership 

Your Companion membership starts on the date that you sign up for a membership and provide us with your payment method. You will be charged for your membership starting on the date of your membership, unless you have a free trial period (as specified during your sign-up for the membership). 

After your free trial period ends (if any), your Companion monthly membership will automatically renew and continue for a one (1) month period, or a one (1) year period, (each, a “Membership Cycle”), unless and until you cancel your membership or GoodRx terminates it. You can cancel your monthly or annual Companion membership at any time. To avoid charges associated with the next Membership Cycle, cancel at least one (1) day prior to your Membership Cycle renewal date. Note that canceling your annual Companion plan will not result in a refund of any prepaid fees except as otherwise stated herein.  For example, for a monthly membership cycle, if your Companion monthly membership starts on May 12, and your trial period ends on May 18, your Companion membership will automatically renew for an additional Membership Cycle on May 19 unless you cancel prior to May 19, or GoodRx terminates your Membership.  If your Companion annual membership starts on May 12, your membership will automatically renew on the following May 12 unless you canceled prior to the May 12 renewal date.  You will be charged $14.99 each month for a monthly Membership, and $119.88 for an annual membership. You will be charged to the credit card number you provided.  

 

Except as provided below, membership fees are non-refundable. If you are a resident of Texas, Florida, Illinois, Ohio, Tennessee, South Carolina, Indiana, Missouri, Colorado, Louisiana, Maryland, Nevada, Mississippi, Kansas, Oklahoma, Oregon, Arkansas, Iowa, Utah, Nebraska, Connecticut, New Hampshire, Delaware, West Virginia, Montana, South Dakota, Rhode Island, Alaska, North Dakota, or Vermont, you may cancel your Membership within thirty (30) days after your initial enrollment and receive a full refund of the membership fees you paid, provided that your cancellation request is submitted within such thirty (30)-day period. Following the applicable 30-day refund period, or if you reside outside the states listed above, membership fees are non-refundable. 

If you cancel after the applicable refund period, your cancellation will become effective as described in the App and these Terms, and you will continue to have access to your Membership benefits until the effective date of cancellation, but no prorated or other refund will be provided for any membership fees already paid.  If you cancel your Membership during a free trial or are otherwise eligible for a refund of your Membership fees, then your access to the Membership benefits will terminate on the date you cancel.  

Amounts paid for medications, prescriptions, treatments, labs, and/or telehealth visits are not refundable. You may find your Membership Cycle renewal date or cancel or modify your membership by visiting the “Membership” tab in your “Accounts” while logged in to your GoodRx account either online or in the GoodRx app. You can also update your billing details on your membership page. If you need to update your billing details associated with your account at any time, you may do so from your membership page. For additional support with cancellation, you can email . 

You may also cancel your membership via mail. Your request must include the full name, Companion Member ID and email address connected with your Companion account. Please mail this request to: GoodRx, Inc., Attn: Patient Advocacy-Companion Cancellation, 2701 Olympic Blvd., West Building Suite 200, Santa Monica, CA 90404

  3. Select Companion Membership   
  4. Click Cancel my Companion Membership

    • Note that GoodRx Companion is not available in Washington state

  6. Start your companion benefits

I had a free GoodRx account and earned rewards, and now have a Companion membership. Will my rewards carry over?

You can still redeem existing rewards points for digital gift cards. Companion members do not earn new Rewards points at this time.

Is GoodRx Rewards available for Companion members?

Unfortunately, Companion members do not earn Rewards points at this time. You can still redeem existing rewards points for digital gift cards.

What is GoodRx Companion?

GoodRx Companion is a discount plan that offers discounts on prescriptions and healthcare provider services. 

 

  • Discounts on medications with 200+ that are FREE and hundreds more under $10*
  • Discounts on common dental, vision, lab, and imaging services
  • $19 online care visits, 7 days a week  

GoodRx Companion is NOT health insurance but can be used instead of health insurance.

  • Eligible free medications and discounted prices are subject to change at any time.

  4. Click Cancel my Companion Membership

About Pay Online

Are all prescriptions eligible for Pay Online?

Not all medications are eligible for online purchase. Availability depends on factors such as the medication class, storage or handling requirements, and other restrictions.

Can the coupon be used for non-eComm drugs? (Pay Online)

Yes, your GoodRx eComm proof of payment will work like a standard GoodRx card. If you use your eComm card but have not paid online in advance, the traditional GoodRx price will be returned to the pharmacy for payment at the pharmacy.

Can the price change after I pre-pay?

Your pre-paid price is generally secured. In some cases, you may be asked to pay an additional amount at the pharmacy. This can happen if there are state and local taxes which must be applied, or if your prescription on file at the pharmacy does not exactly match the prescription details entered online (such as the quantity, dosage, or medication).

Do I already need a prescription to use Pay Online?

Yes. This service is for pre-paying for an existing prescription. You must already have a valid prescription at the pharmacy you have chosen. If you need a new prescription, you can easily get many prescriptions through GoodRx Care’s telehealth service or your healthcare provider before ordering.

Do I need a new GoodRx card to use the eComm service?

Your previous GoodRx cards work with all eComm purchases. However, If you are using a physical card or older GoodRx card, the pharmacy will need to update your profile with the new information.

How do I enter my prescription information? (Pay Online)

You must enter the drug name, form, dosage, and quantity exactly as it is written on your prescription bottle or prescription. Any differences may lead to a price increase at pickup due to the medication being more expensive than previously priced.

How long do I have to pick up my prescription? What happens if I don’t pick up my prescription?

You have 6 days from the time you pre-pay to pick up your prescription. If you do not pick it up within this window, the hold on your credit card will expire, and your pre-payment will automatically cancel.

My pharmacy also offers pay online for my prescriptions. Does this work with GoodRx Pay Online service?

No. Your pharmacy’s online payment system and GoodRx’s Pay Online service are separate. Payments through one system cannot be transferred or combined with the other.

The pharmacist is asking for a different price for my pre-paid order. What should I do?

Politely remind the pharmacist that this order has been pre-paid via our service. Ask them to look at your prepaid coupon and manually enter the specific BIN, PCN, Group, and Member ID. If the price is still different it may be due to local or state taxes, or a difference between your prescription at the pharmacy and what information was entered online.

What happens if I accidentally pay for the same prescription more than once?

Only 1 payment will be charged at prescription pick up. The other charge will expire within 6 days and automatically cancel.

What happens to my credit card when I pre-pay?

When you pay online, a temporary hold is placed on your credit card for the amount shown. Your card is only officially charged once the pharmacy processes the prescription. If your order is canceled or expires, the hold will simply expire (typically within 6 days).

What happens to my prescription if I don’t pick it up within 6 days?

Your prescription will still be available for pick up as long as the pharmacy has not returned it to stock. If your prescription is still available for pick up at the pharmacy then you will either need to pay at the counter or pre-pay online through GoodRx again.

What if I need to cancel my order?

Orders not picked up within the 6-day window are automatically canceled, and the hold on the credit card expires. If you need to cancel sooner, please contact GoodRx Support at +1 (855) 426-4465 and we can manually cancel the order.

What if the pharmacist can’t find my order? (Pay Online)

First you will want to confirm they have your prescription on file. If they do, politely explain that the prepaid coupon is what links their system to your payment. If issues persist, please call GoodRx Support at +1 (855) 426-4465.

What is a prepaid coupon and how do I use it?

A prepaid coupon is generated as your proof of payment once you place your order online. You must present the coupon to the pharmacist and ask them to enter the details which will reduce the balance to $0 so you don’t owe anything at the time of pickup (some exceptions may apply, see below). After paying online, it is best to contact your pharmacy to process your refill and update the coupon information.

What is GoodRx Pay Online?

The pay online feature gives you the option to pay for your prescription on GoodRx’s website or app and receive a prepaid coupon to present at the pharmacy, meaning no payment is due at pickup.

Where can I find the status of my prescription?

This is a pre-payment service only, we do not have access to the status of your prescription. Please contact the pharmacy directly for pickup details and additional information.

Why does my credit card show a different amount than what I see in my order details?

GoodRx puts a hold on your credit card for the cost of your prescription before discounts are applied, so you may see a higher pending charge. Once your transaction is complete your credit card statement will reflect the final price you paid with any discounts applied.

Why is my credit card transaction still pending even though I’ve already picked up my prescription?

Once you pick up your prescription, we submit the final transaction for settlement, but it can take a few days for the status to change from pending to posted. Different credit card issuers and banks have varying speeds for processing and posting transactions.

Will Pay Online automatically move my prescription to the correct pharmacy?

No. You will need to initiate a transfer through GoodRx, call the pharmacy, or your healthcare provider to transfer the prescription to the pharmacy where you pre-paid online. Image below shows how you can initiate a transfer through GoodRx. You will find this button below a coupon when you search for your drug.

GoodRx Gold offers members the choice of two different plan types (Individual plan & Family plan) and two different billing cycles: Monthly & Annual. See the table below to see which plan is right for you

Billing FAQs

 

What happens if I cancel my Annual plan`before my membership term has ended?
  • Your Gold membership benefits will be available to you for the remainder of your membership term. You will not receive a prorated refund.

 

I’ve already paid for a month of Gold membership at the monthly rate. Will the amount that I’ve paid to date be prorated when I switch to an Annual billing plan?
  • Yes, any amount that you’ve paid toward your membership to date will be prorated to your account when you switch to an Annual billing plan.

 

How many times can I switch between monthly and annual billing cycles?
  • There’s no limit to how many times you can switch between monthly billing and annual billing cycles.

Sign up for a 30-day Gold free trial today, and learn more about Gold membership and billing by visiting the  GoodRx Terms of Use and Gold Terms of Service.

How do I add or remove members on my family plan?

Once you’ve finished registering for a GoodRx Gold Family Plan, you can add up to five additional members—family, friends or pets—on one subscription. Add or remove members at any time by following the steps below:

  • Adding/Removing members online

  • First name
  • Last name
  • Birthdate
Removing Members

  1. Select the member you wish to remove

 

  1. Scroll down and select ‘Remove Member’, then read the removal notice and select’Remove’

  • Choose Member type (Partner/Spouse, Dependent / Other, Pet)
  • First name
  • Last name
  • Birthdate

 

Adding Members

  1. Under ‘Profile’, select ‘Plan Information’

How to Sign Up for GoodRx Gold

Signing up for a GoodRx Gold membership is easy! New members who have not previously created a Gold account can try GoodRx Gold free for 30 days.  Follow the steps below to sign up for Gold through the GoodRx mobile app or website. 

  • Sign up on the GoodRx mobile app
  • Sign up on GoodRx.com

 

Sign up for Gold on the GoodRx Mobile App (iOS or Android)

  1. Download the GoodRx mobile app for free (if you haven’t already)

  2. Open the app

  3. Tap the ‘Upgrade’ button

  4. Select your Gold plan type and hit ‘Next’

  5. Enter your first and last name, email, and date of birth and hit ‘Next’

    • Check the box to confirm that you don’t live in Washington state

  6. Add your payment information and hit ‘Next’

  7. Enter the 6-digit verification code sent to your email address in order to complete the Gold sign up process and start your 30-day free trial 

 

Sign up for Gold on the GoodRx.com

  1. Go to GoodRx.com/gold

  2. Click ‘Start your 30-day free trial’

  3. (If not logged in to an existing free GoodRx account) Enter your first and last name, email, and date of birth and click ‘Join for free’

    • Check the box to confirm that you don’t live in Washington state

  4. Select your Gold plan type and hit ‘Next’

  5. Add your payment information and hit ‘Next’

  6. Enter the 6-digit verification code sent to your email address in order to complete the Gold sign up process and start your 30-day free trial

What is the GoodRx Gold cancellation policy?

GoodRx Gold offers a 30-day free trial membership which can be canceled at any time. 

At the end of the free trial period, Gold Members will be charged their first membership fee according to their plan type (Individual/Family) and billing cycle (Monthly/Annual).

 

You can cancel your membership by logging into your account and following the instructions outlined here. There are no fees or penalties to cancel your GoodRx Gold account. 

 

***** Annual plan subscribers must cancel within 2 weeks of an annual membership billing charge in order to be eligible to have the charge refunded to the card on file.

How do I cancel a Gold Home Delivery order?

Care Direct General FAQs

Can I change the date on my next order?

Yes! You can adjust your delivery date up to 72 hours before your medication is scheduled to ship. Just log in to GoodRx.com, find your upcoming shipment, and choose a new date if needed.

Can I get a copy of my medical records or prescription?

Yes, please reach out to our telehealth partner, Wheel, at support@wheel.com.

Can I transfer my Care Direct prescription to another pharmacy instead of having it shipped?

Yes, you may transfer a prescription to your preferred pharmacy.  Please contact our support team or 855-526-3318.  GoodRx is not responsible for any fees you may incur to have your prescription filled by your preferred pharmacy, which will remain your sole obligation.

Do I already need a prescription to use Care Direct?

No! When you sign up for GoodRx Care Direct, our service takes care of everything—from your telehealth consultation to the prescription process. Once you submit your medical history and symptoms, a licensed clinician will review your information and, if appropriate, issue a prescription. Your prescription is then sent directly to our pharmacy partner, so you never have to worry about obtaining one on your own.

How do I cancel my GoodRx Care Direct subscription?

You can cancel your GoodRx Care Direct subscription at any time by following these steps:

  1. Log in to your GoodRx account at GoodRx.com.
  2. Click the account or menu icon in the upper right corner.
  3. Select Dashboard.
  4. Choose your ED subscription.
  5. Click Cancel plan.

If you have any questions or need additional assistance, feel free to reach out to our support team at or 855-526-3318.

How do I cancel or reschedule my Telehealth appointment?

Our telehealth visits are asynchronous and remain open for 10-14 days, depending on the specific condition, so there’s no traditional “appointment” to cancel or reschedule. Once you’ve started your consultation on GoodRx.com, you can message your clinician any time within that window. If you have any concerns, please reach out to our support team.

How do I complete my Telehealth appointment?

In some cases, your clinician may have additional questions before making a determination as to your eligibility for a prescription. To view and respond to messages, simply go to GoodRx.com/care, click on Get Started, and log in. Once logged in, click on Messages to open the communication thread with your clinician.

How do I contact my healthcare provider?

You can easily reach your healthcare provider through your GoodRx treatment subscription dashboard. Here’s how:

  1. Log in to your GoodRx account at GoodRx.com
  2. Click the account or menu icon in the upper right corner.
  3. Select Dashboard.
  4. Choose your treatment subscription.
  5. Select “Messages” 

This feature lets you ask non-urgent questions, discuss symptoms, or request follow-up details about your prescription. If you need to adjust your medication or it’s been a while since your last consultation, you may be prompted to complete a new telehealth visit for a more in-depth review.

As a reminder, messaging is not in real time and there may be a delay in response time from a clinician—if you have a medical emergency, please call 911 or go to the nearest emergency department. If you need any help navigating your account or scheduling a visit, our support team is here for you!

How do I renew my prescription?

You’ll receive an email 7 days before your prescription expires. You’ll also see a prompt when you log-in at GoodRx.com. Simply complete a telehealth visit with our clinician team. After reviewing your treatment progress, if a renewal is appropriate, a new prescription will be issued and your medication will continue to be shipped automatically.

How do I request a refill for my prescription?

When it’s time to refill a recent prescription, you’ll receive a prompt when you log-in at GoodRx.com. To proceed, you will complete a telehealth visit by answering brief health questions and messaging a clinician. A licensed provider will then review your treatment progress to ensure a renewal is appropriate. Once approved, your prescription can be sent directly to a local pharmacy for pickup.

How do I update my address?

Log in to GoodRx.com and go to your account settings. Update your shipping address there and save your changes. We’ll use your new address for your next shipment.

How do I update my payment information?

Simply log in to GoodRx.com, head over to your account dashboard, and click on Update Payment. Enter your new billing details, and we’ll use them for future charges.

How does shipping work?

We automatically ship your medication when it’s time for your next fill. Just make sure your shipping address is current in your Account Settings on GoodRx.com, and we’ll handle the rest. You’ll receive updates about your order’s progress along the way in your dashboard, and receive emails when your order is shipped and when it is delivered.

How does this work?

At GoodRx, we make it easy to get the treatment you need. First, sign up at GoodRx.com and complete a brief intake about your health. Then, select your preferred treatment and your preferred subscription plan cycle.  Next, a licensed clinician reviews your information during an asynchronous telehealth visit that stays active for 10 days, allowing you to respond to any follow-up questions at your own pace. Once your prescription is approved, it is sent to our pharmacy partner, and your medication is shipped directly to your door.  Your designated payment method will be automatically charged at the regular intervals based on your subscription plan. This automatic billing will continue until you cancel your subscription so you never have to miss a fill.  

Read more about how Care Direct works in the Care Direct Terms of Use.  GoodRx Care Direct is NOT insurance.

How long do I have to respond to messages from my clinician?

Depending on which condition you’re being treated for, you’ll have 10-14 days to respond to questions from your clinician.

My treatment is not working, what should I do?

If you feel that your treatment isn’t effective, your health is our top priority. Log in at GoodRx.com and use the messaging feature on your dashboard to contact your clinician. You might also be prompted to schedule a follow-up telehealth visit so that your progress can be reviewed. Based on this review, your clinician may recommend adjustments to your treatment plan.

Website Troubleshooting

If you experience connectivity issues while on GoodRx.com, start by refreshing your browser. Ensure your internet connection is stable and consider clearing your browser’s cache and cookies, as this can often resolve common issues. If the problem persists, please contact our customer support team for further assistance at or 855-526-3318.

What is your refund policy?

If you have questions about your order or run into any issues, our customer support team is here to help. Contact the Patient Advocacy team for questions regarding order issues or run into any issues. Support is available at 855-268-2822, Monday–Sunday, 6 AM–9 PM Central Time. Services and the cost of medication are generally non-refundable.

What is your return policy?

Unfortunately, once your medication has shipped, we can’t accept returns because it’s prescribed specifically for you. If you have questions about your order or run into any issues, our customer support team is here to help.

What should I do if I am experiencing side effects?

If you are having a medical emergency, you should immediately seek medical attention at an emergency room or facility equipped to provide urgent care. You can contact emergency services by dialing 911.

If you are not having a medical emergency, please contact your healthcare team by logging in to GoodRx.com and selecting ‘Ask your healthcare team’ on the dashboard.

When is my next delivery?

To view your upcoming delivery date, simply log in to your GoodRx.com account and check your dashboard. All your subscription details, including your next scheduled shipment, are displayed there.

When will I be charged?

  • Initial Charge: You’ll be charged only after your clinician approves your prescription and your first fill is shipped.
  • Future Charges: After that, you’ll be billed each time your next fill is scheduled to ship—so you’re only paying when you’re actively receiving your medication.

Where is GoodRx Care Direct Available?

GoodRx Care Direct offerings are available in all states, however, additional state restrictions may apply for specific condition treatments, see condition-specific FAQs for details.

Where is my order?

For the latest tracking information, log in to your GoodRx.com account and visit your dashboard. Once your order has shipped, you’ll see a tracking number so you can monitor its progress.

Centro de Ayuda

Consumer Health Data Privacy Notice

**Last Updated: September 12, 2025 Effective Date: March 31, 2024

**

Washington Residents: This Consumer Health Data Privacy Notice is incorporated into GoodRx’s (“GoodRx,” “we,” “us,” or “our”) general privacy policy found here and other related policies that link to this notice, and applies to personal data defined as “consumer health data” (“CHD”) under Washington law.

 

Consumer Health Data We Collect

The CHD we collect depends on how you interact with us, the services you use, and the choices you make. Because CHD is defined very broadly, many of the categories of data we collect are or could be considered CHD.  

Such data may also be categorized as follows:

  • Information about your health-related conditions, symptoms, status, diagnoses, testing, or treatments (including surgeries, procedures, medications, or other interventions). For example, we may collect such information through surveys or other communication with you for research studies and improving product accessibility. We may also collect information about your use of a coupon provided by GoodRx (if the corresponding pharmacy benefit manager or applicable pharmacy discloses information about the transaction to us).
  • Precise location information that could reasonably indicate your attempt to acquire or receive health services or supplies. For example, we may collect GPS location data when you request us to locate the nearest pharmacy to you.
  • Information that could identify your attempt to seek health care services or information, including services that allow you to assess, measure, improve, or learn about your or another person’s health. For example, your interactions with us could involve queries or reveal information about you concerning prescriptions, or other health-related topics. 
  • Information about your prescriptions, such as your provider and pharmacy information, to facilitate fulfillment of your prescription, or sign up for a pharmaceutical manufacturer’s copay card program.

 

  • Other information that may be used to infer or derive data related to the above or other health information.  

 

Sources of Consumer Health Data

CHD is sourced directly from you, from your interactions with our products and services (including inferences we make from your use of GoodRx Services and from your devices), and from third parties and service providers.

 

Why We Collect and Use Consumer Health Data

We collect and use CHD as reasonably necessary to provide you with the products you have requested or authorized. This may include delivering and operating the products and their features, personalization or contextualization of certain product features, communicating with you, ensuring the secure and reliable operation of the products and the systems that support them, troubleshooting and improving the products, advertising and marketing of our Services and other products or services we believe you may find interesting, as well as advertising and marketing served by third parties on our websites and mobile applications or on third-party platforms, and other essential business operations that support the provision of the products (such as analyzing our performance, meeting our legal obligations, developing our workforce, and conducting research and development). 

We may use CHD for other purposes for which we give you choices and/or obtain your consent as required by law – for example, for advertising or marketing purposes. Please review your choices in the applicable privacy policy and the “How to Exercise Your Rights” section below for more details on the controls and choices you may have.   

 

Our Sharing of Consumer Health Data

We may share each of the categories of CHD described above for the purposes described in the relevant privacy policy. In particular, we may share CHD with your consent or as reasonably necessary to complete any transaction or provide any product you have requested or authorized, as described in the previous section above.  

For example, if you make a purchase, we will share information about the transaction as necessary to process the payment, including to protect against fraud. And we may disclose data when we believe that doing so is necessary to comply with applicable law or respond to valid legal process.

 

Third Parties with Which We Share Consumer Health Data

As necessary for the purposes described above, we share CHD with the following categories of third parties: 

  • Affiliates and subsidiaries. We may share information with our subsidiaries and affiliated companies for business and operational purposes. GoodRx subsidiaries and affiliates include:
    • GoodRx Care
    • RxNxt
    • RxSaver
    • Scriptcycle

 

  • Service providers and third parties. Vendors or agents (“processors”) working on our behalf may access CHD for the purposes described above. For example, companies we’ve hired to provide customer service support or assist in protecting and securing our systems and services may need access to data to provide those functions.  
  • For corporate transactions. We may disclose CHD as part of a corporate transaction or proceeding such as a merger, financing, acquisition, bankruptcy, dissolution, or a transfer, divestiture, or sale of all or a portion of our business or assets.
  • For legal obligations/safety. We may be required to share CHD for things like compliance with a valid legal process, such as a subpoena, court order, or search warrant, or where there is a lawful request. We may also disclose your information to protect the security of our Services, servers, network systems, and databases.
  • To enforce legal rights. We may also share CHD to protect our customers, employees, or property; in emergency situations; to investigate, prevent, or take other action regarding suspected illegal activity, fraud, or other wrongdoing; and to enforce our rights under our terms of service and policies, in court or elsewhere.

 

See the sharing section of the relevant privacy policy for detailed explanations of the categories above.

 

How to Exercise Your Rights and Set Your Data Preferences

Washington law provides certain rights to Consumers, including rights to access, delete, or withdraw consent relating to such data, subject to certain exceptions. You can request to exercise such rights as described in the relevant privacy policy.  If you would like to exercise any of your rights, including a request to review and request changes to your CHD, please submit:

  • GoodRx: The “Data Access Request” form (for your right to access), fill out and submit the “Data Deletion Request” form (for your right to delete), or withdraw consent from your account settings or as otherwise instructed when providing consent.
  • Scriptcycle: The “Data Access Request” form (for your right to access), fill out and submit the “Data Deletion Request” form (for your right to delete), or withdraw consent from your account settings or as otherwise instructed when providing consent.
  • RxSaver: The “Data Access Request” form (for your right to access), fill out and submit the “Data Deletion Request” form (for your right to delete), or withdraw consent from your account settings or as otherwise instructed when providing consent.
  • RxNxt: Email Data Access Requests to  datarequest@rxnxt.com (for your right to access), email Data Deletion Requests to with the subject link ATTN: Customer Service Department—Data Deletion (for your right to delete), or withdraw consent from your account settings or as otherwise instructed when providing consent.

 

For questions, please contact us at privacy@goodrx.com. If your request to exercise a right under applicable law is denied, you may appeal that decision by contacting us at datarequest@goodrx.com. If your appeal is unsuccessful, you can raise a concern or lodge a complaint with your state Attorney General.

 

Updates to This Consumer Health Data Privacy Policy

We may update this Consumer Health Data Privacy Policy from time to time. We may modify this Privacy Policy at any time. When we do so, we will post the revised privacy policy on our website. Please check the revision date at the top of this page to determine if the policy has been modified since you last reviewed it.

How do I cancel my Gold membership?

You can cancel your account at any time by following these quick steps: 

  • Canceling your account in the app
  • Canceling your account online

Canceling Your Gold Account in the GoodRx Mobile App

  1. Log in to the GoodRx Mobile App and tap the Account tab.  
  2. Next, click Gold Membership  
  3. Click Cancel my GoodRx Gold membership  
  4. Confirm that you want to cancel your Gold membership by clicking Continue cancellation   

   

Canceling Your Gold Account on the GoodRx Gold Website

  1. Log in to your Gold account at goodrx.com/gold                                       
  2.  Click on the profile icon in the upper right corner   
  3. Click the Account option                                     
  4. Select GoldMembership   
  5. Click Cancel my Gold Membership  
  6. If you’re sure you want to cancel your Gold membership, click ‘Yes, cancel anyway’  

Done!

Subscription Cancellation Notice

Cancellation

In addition to the phone and mail options outlined in the applicable subscription Terms and Conditions, subscription members (including Gold and Companion users) may submit cancellation requests via email to support@goodrx.com with the subject line “Subscription Cancellation Request.” Cancellation requests must include the full name of the member, the associated subscription (e.g., Gold or Companion), subscription Member ID and email address connected with the member’s subscription account.

Data Deletion

Data Retention and Deletion

Effective Date: August 16, 2023

GoodRx, including its subsidiaries and affiliates (together, “GoodRx”), retain consumer personal information for certain periods of time. The chart below (the “Retention Schedule”) describes how long we retain personal information, and for what purpose. Any terms that are used in this Retention Schedule that are not defined here have the meanings given to them in our privacy policies. Except as described below, we will retain your personal information while you remain active (as measured by relevant actions or engagement with us, such as logging into, accessing or engaged with our Services) so that you can effectively use our services.

Unless an exception applies (described below), we will then delete your personal information in the following circumstances: (a) no later than ninety (90) days after you request that we delete your personal information using our deletion request form; (b) within two (2) years, for personal information used for Advertising and Marketing and Reviews, and (c) once your engagement with us becomes inactive, we delete your personal information following seven (7) years after the Effective Date of this Retention Schedule or seven (7) years after your account is deemed inactive, whichever is later.

Exceptions may apply where we are required to retain certain personal information by law (e.g., subpoena, warrant, regulatory or legal order, etc.), and/or as necessary for the establishment, exercise or defense of legal claims. In addition, from time to time, GoodRx may impose a different retention schedule on specific information; if that occurs, GoodRx will advise you of that retention schedule through specific notice. Please note, deletion under this Retention Schedule includes removing information such that it is not maintained in retrievable form and cannot be retrieved in the normal course of business as permitted under applicable law.

Data Security: Miscellaneous

Cookies FAQ

1.What are cookies? 

Cookies are small text files that store information on web browsers. Other technologies, such as tags, pixels, SDKs, and similar technologies to track online activity, may store data stored on your browser or device and also serve similar purposes. For simplicity, we refer to all these technologies collectively as ‘Cookies’.

We use cookies if you use GoodRx, including through our website or applications. Cookies enable GoodRx to offer its products to you and to understand the information that we receive about you, including use of other websites and applications, whether or not you are registered or logged in.

This FAQ explains how we use cookies and the choices you have. The Privacy Policy will apply to our processing of the data that we collect via cookies and more.

2. Why do we use cookies?

Cookies help us provide, protect, and improve GoodRx products, such as, but not limited to, personalizing content, tailoring and measuring analytics, advertising, and providing a better experience. There are two types of cookies: session cookies and persistent cookies. A session cookie lasts while your browser is open and is automatically deleted when you close your browser. A persistent cookie lasts until you or your browser deletes the cookies, or they expire.

The cookies that we use and how we use them may change over time as we improve and update our products and services. We typically use them for the following purposes:

  1. Authentication
    1. We may use cookies to verify your account and determine when you’re logged in so that we can make it easier for you to access our services and show you the appropriate experience and features.
  2.  Security, site and product integrity
    1. We may use cookies to help keep your account and data safe and secure. We also use it to combat activity that violates our policies and otherwise obstructs our ability to provide our services.
  3.  Advertising
    1. We may use cookies to help us show advertisements and to make recommendations for our products and services. 
  4. Measurement
    1. We may use cookies to help us serve and measure advertisements across different browsers and devices that may be used by you.
  5.  Site features and services
    1. We may use cookies to enable the functionality that helps us provide GoodRx services. We also use cookies to help provide you with content that is relevant to you.
  6.  Performance
    1. We use cookies to provide you with the best experience possible on our platform
  7.  Analytics and research
    1. We use cookies to better understand how you use our products and services for insights, including how we can improve. 
  8.  Third-party websites and applications
    1. Third-party websites and/or applications may share information with us from cookies set on their websites and domains, regardless of whether you have a GoodRx account or are logged in. These companies may also combine information they collect from your interaction with the GoodRx Services with information they collect from other sources. We may not have access to information these companies collect using cookies. 

For example, we may use Microsoft Advertising’s Universal Event Tracking (UET) feature and you can find more information on how Microsoft handles personal data in their Microsoft Privacy Statement. Another example is we may use Google Analytics to better understand your interactions with our online properties and improve our service based on user behavior. 

  1. How can you control your information?

We use cookies for a variety of purposes, such as to help personalize and improve content and services, provide a safer experience, and show you relevant information about GoodRx products. You can control how your data is used by GoodRx to show you advertisements and more by using the tools described below. Since cookies operate on the device and browser level, if you wish to disable or decline all cookies then you will need to do so on each device you use to access GoodRx. Device or browser level settings are solely in your control, including deletion of cookies on your device.

  1. On the GoodRx website
    1. You can use the “Cookie Preferences” linked in the footer of the applicable GoodRx webpage to control how we use cookies for targeted advertising and/or cross-context behavioral advertising. 

 

  1. Browser and Device cookie controls
    1. In addition, your browser or device may offer settings that allow you to choose whether browser cookies are set and to delete them. 
    2. Controls vary by browser, and manufacturers may change both the settings they make available and how they work at any time. 
    3. You will need to turn on or activate any browser settings on each device on which you wish to configure cookies.

Delivery Order Issues

I received the wrong medication, now what?

If you received the wrong medication, please call us at 1-855-846-4665. We are available from 7AM - 8PM Central Time, 7 days a week.

I want a refund. How do I do that?

Need a refund? No problem, we’re here to help. Call us at 1-855-846-4665. We are available from 7AM - 8PM Central Time, 7 days a week.

My order was delivered damaged/defective, what do I do?

GoodRx will replace one order for a non-controlled substance ONLY.  Controlled substances are not eligible for replacements.

Please call us at 1-855-846-4665. We are available from 7AM - 8PM Central Time, 7 days a week.

What if I have questions about my order that aren’t listed here?

If you have any questions, we’re always here to help! Give us a call at 1-855-846-4665.

Our Patient Advocacy team is ready to answer your questions and can help you place an order. We are available from 7AM - 8PM Central Time, 7 days a week.

Earning Points

Do I need to show a coupon every time I fill a prescription to earn points?

Once you are enrolled in GoodRx Rewards, you can earn points when you search for and use a new GoodRx coupon at the pharmacy*.

 

Here’s how it works: 

  • Before searching for a new coupon, make sure you are logged in to your GoodRx account on the GoodRx app. 
  • Search for your medication and locate a coupon. 
  • Show that coupon to the pharmacist and ensure that they enter the information exactly as shown. 
  • Within 48 hours, we’ll automatically add points to your GoodRx Rewards balance.
  • If your medication is eligible for refills, you’ll earn points automatically every time the pharmacist processes your refill.

*Exclusions apply. Only the primary member on a Gold Family Plan may earn points on eligible prescriptions. Check GoodRx.com or our mobile app to see if a prescription qualifies for GoodRx Rewards points.

How are points calculated?

Once you have enrolled in GoodRx Rewards, you’ll earn points for taking eligible actions in the GoodRx app. 

For a list of all eligible actions and points value, click here.

How do I earn points?

You can register for a GoodRx account and enroll in rewards by visiting the rewards tab in the GoodRx app. It’s as easy as that!

Using a GoodRx Coupon for a New Prescription 

Once you are enrolled in GoodRx Rewards, you’ll earn points when you search for and use a GoodRx coupon at the pharmacy*.

Here’s how it works: 

  • Before searching for a new coupon, make sure you are logged in to your GoodRx account on the GoodRx app. 
  • Search for your medication and locate a coupon. 
  • Show that coupon to the pharmacist and ensure that they enter the information exactly as shown. 
  • Within 48 hours, we’ll automatically add points to your GoodRx Rewards balance.
  • If your medication is eligible for refills, you’ll earn points automatically every time the pharmacist processes your refill.

 

Using a GoodRx Coupon at the Pharmacy for an Existing Prescription Refill

After you have enrolled in GoodRx Rewards, you can earn points when you refill a prescription using a new GoodRx coupon*. 

Here’s how it works: 

  • When you are ready to refill a prescription, you’ll need to use a new coupon. 
  • Before searching for a new coupon, make sure you are logged in to your GoodRx account on the GoodRx app. 
  • Search for the medication that you will be refilling and locate a coupon. 
  • Show that coupon to the pharmacist and ensure that they enter the information exactly as shown. 
  • Within 48 hours, we’ll automatically add points to your GoodRx Rewards balance.

**Exclusions apply. Only the primary member on a Gold Family Plan may earn points on eligible prescriptions. Check GoodRx.com or our mobile app to see if a prescription qualifies for GoodRx Rewards points. Unfortunately, Companion members do not earn Rewards points at this time. Companion users can still redeem existing rewards points for digital gift cards.*

Is there a maximum number of points I can earn?

You will be capped at earning 25,000 points on a monthly basis.

I’ve been using GoodRx coupons on my prescriptions. How do I earn points for my existing prescriptions?

Once you are enrolled in GoodRx Rewards, you can earn points when you search for and use a new GoodRx coupon at the pharmacy for your refill*.

 

Here’s how it works: 

  • When you are ready to refill a prescription (or fill a new prescription), you’ll need to use a new coupon. 
  • Before searching for a new coupon, make sure you are logged in to your GoodRx account on the GoodRx app. 
  • Search for the medication that you will be refilling and locate a coupon. 
  • Show that coupon to the pharmacist and ensure that they enter the information exactly as shown. 
  • Within 48 hours, we’ll automatically add points to your GoodRx Rewards balance.

*Exclusions apply. Only the primary member on a Gold Family Plan may earn points on eligible prescriptions. Check GoodRx.com or our mobile app to see if a prescription qualifies for GoodRx Rewards points. Unfortunately, Companion members do not earn Rewards points at this time. Companion users can still redeem existing rewards points for digital gift cards.

I’ve successfully enrolled in GoodRx Rewards. How do I earn points?

Congratulations! You are on your way to earning points. 

Now that you are enrolled, you will earn points when you use a new GoodRx coupon at the pharmacy for your medications*. 

Here’s how it works: 

  • Before searching for a new coupon, make sure you are logged in to your GoodRx account on the GoodRx app. 
  • Search for your medication and locate a coupon. 
  • Show that coupon to the pharmacist and ensure that they enter the information exactly as shown. 
  • Within 48 hours, we’ll automatically add points to your GoodRx Rewards balance.

**Exclusions apply. Only the primary member on a Gold Family Plan may earn points on eligible prescriptions. Check GoodRx.com or our mobile app to see if a prescription qualifies for GoodRx Rewards points. Unfortunately, Companion members do not earn Rewards points at this time. Companion users can still redeem existing rewards points for digital gift cards.*

When will the points be added to my account?

You can expect to see the points you’ve earned added to your account within 24-48 hours of picking up your medication.

Where can I view the points I have earned?

You can always access your point balance in the Rewards tab of the GoodRx app.

Erectile Dysfunction

Can I change my dosage on my medication? (Erectile Dysfunction)

Yes, subject to clinician approval. If you’d like to change your dosage, send a message to our clinician team via the secure messaging feature on GoodRx.com. You will then get prompted to complete a new telehealth visit. If your dosage change is approved, your prescription will be updated accordingly.

Where is GoodRx Care Direct available? (Erectile Dysfunction)

GoodRx Care Direct for ED is available in all U.S. states, except North Dakota and South Carolina.
