Here’s how you can get in touch:

  • Phone Support: Give us a call at +1 (855) 268 2822, available 7 days a week between 6 AM - 9 PM Central. 

  • Email: Email us with any questions or concerns at

The prices you list for my Medicare Plan are wrong

First, you’ll want to make sure that you have selected the correct plan and copay stage. If you have the correct plan information selected, it’s possible the price has recently updated.

GoodRx uses Medicare data from a government agency that supplies data to third parties like GoodRx. We receive updates a few times a year, but sometimes our data can fall behind the current information for your plan. GoodRx matches the information on Medicare.gov as best as we can, but prices can change throughout the year.

If you have questions or concerns about your plan’s coverage or co-pay costs, contact your Medicare provider directly. You can also visit Medicare.gov to find more information about your current plan and the estimated cost of your prescriptions.

While Medicare prices are estimates, GoodRx coupon prices are as accurate as possible, and pharmacies are obligated to accept these discounts.  While we work to get Medicare prices updated, you can remove your plan from the search to view GoodRx prices for your pharmacy instead.

VCRx is now Powered by GoodRx

Exciting News! VCRx is now powered by GoodRx.Existing VCRx users, VCRx is now powered by GoodRx.  When you use VCRx, you accept the GoodRx Terms of Use and acknowledge the Privacy Policy. Rest assured, you can still use your VCRx card at the same pharmacies.

Getting Started

How do I sign up for GoodRx for HCPs on the app?

GoodRx for healthcare professionals (GoodRx for HCPs) is tailored to serve as a one-stop shop for healthcare professionals to improve affordability, access, and adherence of medications for their patients. It is completely free of charge, and available on both web (desktop and mobile) and app (iOS and Android).

This article talks about how to sign up for GoodRx for HCPs experience on the app.

Want to learn more about GoodRx for HCPs? See here.

Want to learn how to sign up for GoodRx for HCPs on the web? See here.

How do I get started?If you don’t have the GoodRx app downloaded:

  1. Download the GoodRx app on your mobile phone (available on both iOS and Android).
  2. Tap “I’m a healthcare professional” and then tap “Continue” on the next screen.
  3. Sign up for a free GoodRx account by entering your email address or phone number, and verifying it via the 6-digit verification code to your mail or messages. You can also sign up using your Google account.

What if I already have a GoodRx account? Feel free to sign in using the same account. You can easily switch between HCP and patient experiences (see here).

  1. Complete your HCP profile by adding your role, name, primary practice state, specialty and NPI (for prescriber roles) to gain full access to all features within the GoodRx for HCPs experience.

If you are already using the GoodRx app:

  1. Open the GoodRx app on your iOS or Android device, and navigate to the “Account” tab in the bottom right.
  2. If you already have an account, tap “Update your profile as an HCP”.
    1. Otherwise, tap “Set up your profile as an HCP”. Enter your email address or phone number, and verify it via the 6-digit verification code to your mail or messages. You can also sign up using your Google account.
  3. Complete your HCP profile by adding your role, name, primary practice state, specialty and NPI (for prescriber roles) to gain full access to all features within the GoodRx for HCPs experience.

Once registration is complete, go to the home tab.

  • On the home tab, tap on any drug name or search for a drug to get to its drug page. Explore the various available savings options for that drug. Try sharing a coupon with yourself.
  • Discover new savings for drugs related to your specialty on the home tab itself. Order a free savings kit from Resources tab, if you don’t already have one in your office.

Learn more about what you can do here.

How do I switch between HCP experience and patient experience?

We understand that you could sometimes wish to use GoodRx as a patient yourself as well. You can seamlessly switch between the two experiences.

  • Tap on the ‘Account’ tab at the bottom right.
  • To switch from the HCP experience to the patient one, select ‘Go to GoodRx for patients’.
  • To switch from the patient experience to the HCP one, select ‘Go to GoodRx for HCPs’.

How do I sign up for GoodRx for HCPs on the web?

GoodRx for healthcare professionals (GoodRx for HCPs) is tailored to serve as a one-stop shop for healthcare professionals to improve affordability, access, and adherence of medications for their patients. It is completely free of charge, and available on both web (desktop and mobile) and app (iOS and Android).

This article talks about how to sign up for GoodRx for HCPs experience on the web.

Want to learn how to sign up for GoodRx for HCPs on the app? See here.

How do I get started?

  1. Navigate to www.goodrx.com/hcp/join on your laptop, desktop, or mobile phone.
  2. Sign up for a free GoodRx account by entering your email address or phone number, and verifying it via the 6-digit verification code sent to your mail or messages. You can also sign up using your Google account.

What if I already have a GoodRx account? Feel free to sign in using the same account. You can easily switch between HCP and patient experiences (see here).

  1. Complete your HCP profile by adding your role, name, primary practice state, specialty and NPI (for prescriber roles) to gain full access to all features within the GoodRx for HCPs experience.

Once registration is complete, you will be directed to the home page.

  • On the home page, click on any drug name or search for a drug to get to its drug page. Explore the various available savings options for that drug. Try sharing a coupon with yourself.
  • Discover new savings for drugs related to your specialty on the home page itself. Order a free savings kit, if you don’t already have one in your office.

Learn more about what you can do here.

How do I switch between HCP experience and patient experience?

We understand that you could sometimes wish to use GoodRx as a patient yourself as well. You can seamlessly switch between the two experiences.

  • To go from the HCP experience to the patient one, select ‘Switch to GoodRx for patients’ in the top right corner of your window.
  • To go from the patient experience to the HCP one, select ‘Switch to GoodRx for HCPs’ in the top right corner of your window.

What is GoodRx for healthcare professionals?

GoodRx for healthcare professionals (GoodRx for HCPs) is tailored to serve as a one-stop shop for healthcare professionals to improve affordability, access, and adherence of medications for their patients. It is completely free of charge, and available on both web (desktop and mobile) and app (iOS and Android).

Want to get started? Learn how you can sign up for GoodRx for HCPs on the web or app.

Who can use GoodRx for HCPs?

GoodRx for HCPs is available to all healthcare professionals including:

  • Physicians
  • Dentists
  • Physician associates
  • Nurse practitioners
  • Pharmacists
  • Pharmacy technicians
  • Veterinarians
  • Nurses
  • Medical assistants
  • Social workers
  • Office staff members

How can it help me as a healthcare professional?1. GoodRx for HCPs helps you help your patients afford and access the medication you are prescribing

  • Search for a drug you are looking to prescribe or use one of the bookmarks for quicker access.
  • Adjust the drug configuration, if needed.
  • Get streamlined access to all savings options for your patient (insured or uninsured):
    • GoodRx coupons, showing prices across pharmacies in your patient’s zip code
    • Manufacturer savings programs
    • Patient assistance programs
    • Home delivery
  • Directly share the savings to your patient via print, text, or email – without sharing your personal contact information.

2. GoodRx for HCPs helps you discover affordable alternative drugs and new savings options for drugs relevant to you

  • Quickly compare pricing across brand/generic equivalents, other drugs in the same class, or other drugs that treat relevant conditions.
  • Discover manufacturer savings programs and GoodRx exclusive discounts for brand-name drugs in your specialty.
  • Print savings cheat sheets to have a handy reference for drug pricing in a particular topic area.

3. GoodRx for HCPs helps streamline access to tools and resources

  • Order a free GoodRx savings kit for your office so that your patients can walk away with a physical savings card in hand.
  • See how much your patients have saved using GoodRx discounts on generic and brand-name drugs.
  • Create your practice and invite your team to share bookmarks and see patient savings insights across the practice.
  • Get your questions answered about how GoodRx works and who it is for.

GoodRx Discount Cards

How can I request a discount card?

Getting a discount card is easy… and free! This free card entitles you to discounted prices of up to 80% at most U.S. pharmacies. All you have to do to request a card is head over to the GoodRx Discount Card page.

Keep in mind, however, that better discounts may be found by looking up drug prices and printing coupons on our website or mobile app. Click here to learn more about the difference between the GoodRx coupons and discount card.

We’ll show you how to request your card below:

Step 1Go to the Discount Card page

You can get to the GoodRx Discount Card page by clicking here,  or navigate to the GoodRx website, open the menu via the top-right icon, and select “Get a prescription savings card” . We’ve highlighted the location of this menu in the image below.

Step 2Tell us where to send your card

On the GoodRx Discount Card page, enter the requested information into the correct fields. Then, click the box “I agree to the GoodRx Terms of Service and Privacy Policy” and click “Submit”.

How can I request bulk cards for my patients and clients?

You can request a free Patient Savings Kit or additional card refill packs by clicking here.

That link will take you to the GoodRx for Professionals webpage. Just fill out the easy online order form to request materials for your office!

Not sure of the types of kits we offer?

Patient Savings Kit

The Savings Kit includes:

  • 100 FREE pre-activated GoodRx Discount Cards, giving patients and their families up to 80% off their prescriptions;
  • A countertop stand that explains how to use GoodRx;
  • Information on our powerful iPhone® and Android™ apps;
  • Details on how to find prices, savings tips, coupons, manufacturer discounts and more.

Card Refill Packs

The Refill Packs include a box of 150 cards.

Need help filling out the Kit Request form?

Below are a few quick tips for filling out the form:  

 

Savings Kit or Savings Cards Only

A savings kit includes 100 free pre-activated savings cards and a countertop stand that explains how to use GoodRx. If you are looking to order just the cards, select ‘Savings Cards Only’ in the top right.

The ‘Savings Cards Only’ option comes with a box of 150 free pre-activated savings cards to refill your GoodRx countertop stand so that patients can continue saving money. 

Your Savings Kit section

After selecting which language you would like the cards to be in, or if you would like materials for a veterinary office, you can also select the quantity of savings kits you would like to receive. Click on the drop-down menu to select the number of savings kits. You can also select “Sign me up for auto delivery” to receive refills of cards automatically.

Basic Information

Enter the name of the person you would like to receive the package, i.e. John Smith. You can enter the name and email address of any recipient — whether they are a doctor or not.

Practice or Business Name

If you are an individual requesting bulk cards, you can leave this one blank. 

Mailing Address

Remember to add any suite, floor, building or apartment numbers to ensure the cards are sent to the correct address.

NPI Number

If you do not have an NPI number, you can leave this section blank.

No longer want to receive any mailing from GoodRx?

If you need a break from these mailings, learn how to stop receiving Patient Savings Kits and refill packs.

My discount card(s) and coupon have different Member ID’s. Does this matter?

It’s normal for each GoodRx discount card and each GoodRx coupon to have a different Member ID. The Member ID listed on a GoodRx discount card or coupon is not assigned to any specific person.

Each card and coupon will have separate member IDs and code information. The pharmacist enters this information into the pharmacy’s system in order to apply the appropriate discount. You can use any coupon or card at the pharmacy regardless of the listed Member ID. 

It’s important to note that you will generally save more when you use the most current coupons listed on the GoodRx website and mobile app. We offer cards for those who do not have access to the coupons or for those who prefer to carry a card with them. While the card will still provide you with a discount at the pharmacy, it may not always provide you with the price listed on the website or app. In order to receive the coupon price listed on our site, you must use the current coupon.

Checking our website before you go to the pharmacy and using a new coupon will allow you to receive the best price every time you fill or refill a prescription.

Learn more about the differences between the discount card and coupons here.

What is the difference between GoodRx coupons and discount cards?

GoodRx makes it easy to save money at the pharmacy with two different ways to access our free prescription discounts: 

 

GoodRx Coupons

  •  Use GoodRx.com or the GoodRx mobile app to find the best discounts for your prescription at local pharmacies — then show the matching coupon at the pharmacy to save up to 80% on your purchase. You can send these coupons to yourself via text message or email from the GoodRx mobile app or website if you don’t have a printer. To learn more about how you can use GoodRx coupons without printing, click here.

     

GoodRx Prescription Drug Savings Cards

  • For those who prefer to keep a physical card in their purse or wallet (or cannot access the GoodRx website or mobile app), the GoodRx Prescription Drug Savings Cards can also be presented at the pharmacy for savings of up to 80%. To request a GoodRx Prescription Drug Savings Card sent via mail, fill out the request form here. 

 

Unlike the Prescription Drug Savings Cards, using the GoodRx website or mobile app to search for coupons on your prescriptions allows you to compare prices across multiple pharmacies in your area and take advantage of additional savings options that may be available, depending on your prescription.

GoodRx for Pets: Miscellaneous

What is GoodRx for Pets?

GoodRx is dedicated to helping you save on prescriptions for all your loved ones, including your pets! With GoodRx for Pets, you can save on pet medications, including many human prescriptions commonly prescribed by vets. Additionally, you can now order pet-specific medications delivered right to your door!

 

How do I get started?

  1. Search for the medication on GoodRx.com and select the ‘GoodRx for Pets’ option in the Home Delivery section.

  2. Confirm the prescription details (e.g., supply and quantity) and add it to your cart.

  3. At checkout, create a GoodRx for Pets account, choose a shipping method, enter payment information, and provide your vet’s details.

  4. After completing your order you’ll receive a confirmation email that’ll contain tracking information for your shipment.

How can I check the status of my GoodRx for Pets order?

With your GoodRx for Pets account you can check the status of your order through the ‘Account Management’ tab. 

 

When can I expect to receive my order?

You can expect your order to ship within 24 business hours for OTC items, and 24-72 business hours for Rx items after your vet’s approval. For orders with both, shipping occurs once the Rx is authorized. Delivery typically takes 3-5 business days after shipping.

 

Still have more questions?

We’re here to help.

Call us at 877-503-0295 (M-F, 8 AM - 7 PM ET; Sat, 9 AM - 5 PM ET) or email customercare@goodrxforpets.com for further assistance.

GoodRx Rewards Issues

Do my rewards points expire?

If your account is inactive for 120 days, the points earned will expire. Your account is considered inactive if you have not used coupons or redeemed any rewards within a 120-day time period. You will be notified via the email address associated with your account before your points expire. 

 

As long as you are either using GoodRx coupons or redeeming points, you can save up as many points as you’d like, for as long as you’d like! Points will never be removed unless they expire or are used for a reward!

I had earned points with GoodRx Rewards, but upgraded my account to a Gold membership. Will my points carry over?

Yes! If you’re currently a free GoodRx user enrolled in the GoodRx Rewards program, any existing Rewards points you have in your balance will carry over if you choose to upgrade to a GoodRx Gold membership. Gold members can redeem their Rewards points for gift cards and for discounts on prescriptions.

  • Please Note: Only the pimary member on a Gold Family Plan is eligible to earn Rewards points. For more information, refer to the GoodRx Rewards Terms & Conditions.

I had to return my medication to the pharmacy. Do I lose the points that I’ve earned?

In the event that the medication is returned to the pharmacy before the purchase is processed under your Rewards profile, the points will not be added to your account.

If I close my GoodRx account, can I transfer my points to another user?

Your points are non-transferrable and tied to your GoodRx account. Once you close your account with GoodRx, you will lose all access to any unredeemed points earned.

What if I no longer want to be a part of the program? Can I opt out?

If you would like to close your GoodRx account, you can do so by logging into your account and visiting your account settings page. There you will have the option to delete your GoodRx account. Note, once you delete your account, you will lose access to any saved prescription information and history, and you will forfeit any unredeemed points on your account balance.

GoodRx Rewards Questions

Can I use both GoodRx Rewards and Fetch Rewards at the same time?

Yes! They are two separate programs that process your rewards differently.

  • To earn rewards with Fetch when filling a prescription, you must provide the pharmacy with the GoodRx coupon codes accessed through the Fetch app.
  • With GoodRx Rewards, you’ll earn points when you search for and use a GoodRx coupon at the pharmacy* 

*Exclusions apply. Only the primary member on a Gold Family Plan may earn points on eligible prescriptions. Check GoodRx.com or our mobile app to see if a prescription qualifies for GoodRx Rewards points. Unfortunately, Companion members do not earn Rewards points at this time. Companion users can still redeem existing rewards points for digital gift cards.

Rewards Points Values

1,000 GoodRx Rewards points are currently worth $1 USD. GoodRx users can earn rewards points in a variety of ways: 

  • Action: Points EarnedValue (in USD)

  • Welcome to Rewards: Double-check your info: 750 $0.75
  • Welcome to Rewards: Use your GoodRx coupon: 1,500 $1.50
  • Using a GoodRx coupon for a new prescription Learn More: 1,000 $1.00
  • Using a GoodRx coupon for each prescription refill Learn More: 1,000 $1.00

Home delivery orders are not eligible for Rewards points

 

Welcome to Rewards Activation Challenges

If you’re a newly enrolled GoodRx user, you will have the opportunity to earn your first 2,500 Rewards points through a 3-part series of activation challenges designed to make sure you’re all set for Rewards success!

 

Here’s how it works:

  1. Learn the Basics:  Learn how to start earning and redeeming GoodRx Rewards points
  2. Double-Check Your Info: Set your preferred pharmacy, review your personal information, and set your GoodRx app notifications to make sure your information is accurate and you receive alerts for Rewards activity 
  3. Use your coupon: Finally, find a GoodRx coupon for your prescription to show at your preferred pharmacy next time you go to purchase your medication. 

 

 

 

Using a GoodRx Coupon at the Pharmacy: New Prescription

After you have enrolled in GoodRx Rewards, you can earn 1,000 points each time you fill a prescription using a new GoodRx coupon. Note that schedule 2 drugs are not eligible for rewards. 

 

Here’s how it works: 

  1. When you are ready to fill a new prescription, you’ll need to use a new coupon. 
  2. Before searching for a new coupon, make sure you are logged into your GoodRx account on the GoodRx app. 
  3. Search for the medication that you will be filling and locate a coupon. 
  4. Show that coupon to the pharmacist and ensure that they enter the information exactly as shown. 
  5. Within 48 hours, we’ll automatically add points to your GoodRx Rewards balance.

 

Using a GoodRx Coupon at the Pharmacy: Existing Prescription Refill

After you have enrolled in GoodRx Rewards, you can earn 1,000 points each time you refill a prescription using a new GoodRx coupon. Note that schedule 2 drugs are not eligible for rewards. 

 

Here’s how it works: 

  1. When you are ready to refill a prescription, you’ll need to use a new coupon. 
  2. Before searching for a new coupon, make sure you are logged into your GoodRx account on the GoodRx app. 
  3. Search for the medication that you will be refilling and locate a coupon. 
  4. Show that coupon to the pharmacist and ensure that they enter the information exactly as shown. 
  5. Within 48 hours, we’ll automatically add points to your GoodRx Rewards balance.

Mastercard Rewards Program

GoodRx is excited to offer people a $5 GoodRx Prepaid Mastercard® for new GoodRx users who qualify.

How does it work?

You will receive a one time $5 GoodRx Prepaid Mastercard® (“Rewards Card”) when you use a GoodRx Prescription Savings Card (“Discount Card”) that you received in the mail, or by using a free GoodRx coupon from our GoodRx app.  

The Rewards Card will be emailed to the address associated with your account after you have made the qualifying purchase. You can expect to receive the Rewards Card within 4-6 weeks of the qualified purchase being verified.

I received this special offer. How do I get the $5 reward?

  1. Sign up for a free GoodRx account online, or sign in to your existing GoodRx account.
  2. Use a GoodRx Prescription Savings Card or GoodRx coupon within thirty (30) days of account creation.
  3. Once we verify the qualifying purchase of a prescription, we will email you a $5 Rewards Card.

  • When you use your GoodRx Prescription Drug Savings Card (“Discount Card”) or a free GoodRx coupon to purchase a prescription at a participating pharmacy, you will receive a $5 GoodRx Prepaid Mastercard® or, as applicable, a $5 gift card from select retailers (“Rewards Card”). Please visit goodrx.com for a current list of participating pharmacies.

Redeeming GoodRx Rewards Points

Redeeming Points

In order to redeem Rewards points for either additional discounts on prescriptions or a digital gift card of your choosing , you must have used a GoodRx coupon while logged into your GoodRx account* and have met a minimum redemption threshold of 3,000 points and other requirements pursuant to the GoodRx Rewards Program Terms and Conditions . 

 

*Unfortunately, Companion members do not earn Rewards points at this time. Companion users can still redeem existing rewards points for digital gift cards.

 

To Redeem your Rewards Points:
  1. Open the GoodRx app and select the ’Rewards’ tab in the bottom menu bar.
  2. Under ‘Ways to Redeem’ select either:
    • Extra prescription discounts
    • Gift cards of your choice

 

To Redeem GoodRx Rewards points for an Additional Discount on your prescription:
  1. In the GoodRx Rewards tab, select ‘Extra prescription discounts’  under ‘Ways to redeem’
  2. If you have saved prescriptions in your Medicine cabinet, select the eligible prescription you would like to exchange Rewards points for an additional discount on
  3. Make sure the coupon is for the same pharmacy* where your prescription is on file 
    • To change to another participating pharmacy, click ‘Change’ next to the Pharmacy name under ‘Your Rewards Discount’
  4. Redeem the available $5, $10, or $15 discount on the eligible prescription by tapping ‘Redeem points for discount’. Please note that Gold members are currently unable to redeem rewards points for discounts on their prescriptions. 
  5. Show the GoodRx coupon with the additional Rewards discount to the pharmacist when you purchase your medication at your preferred pharmacy
  • Additional discounts on top of GoodRx coupon prices are subject to availabilty and pharmacy participation. 

 

To Redeem GoodRx Rewards points for a Gift Card of your choice
  1. In the GoodRx Rewards tab, select ‘Gift cards of your choice’  under ’Ways to redeem’
  2. Review the instructions for redeeming Rewards points for a gift card of your choice sent via email
  3. Check your email to select a gift card from one of hundreds of vendors on the Tango platform

How do I get started?

Can I fill more than a 3-month supply with GoodRx Gold Home Delivery?

Government regulations require that your first order for each prescription cannot exceed the quantity that you have been prescribed. 

For refills, you may be able to order up to the amount remaining on your prescription, depending on the specific medication.

Can I pick up my prescription at the pharmacy if I need it sooner?

If you place a Gold Home Delivery order but later decide that you want to pick up your prescription at your local pharmacy, please contact our pharmacy partner, Fuze Health, directly at 650-353-5495 to have your prescription transferred back to your local pharmacy.

GoodRx Gold is accepted at thousands of pharmacies across the country, including CVS, Rite Aid, Costco, VONS, Publix, and Albertsons. See the full list here.

GoodRx Gold is currently not accepted at Rite-Aid, Sam’s Club, Walgreens, or Walmart.

Can I transfer prescriptions from other mail-order pharmacies?

Yes, you can transfer medications from other mail-order pharmacies. When you placing your Gold Home Delivery order, enter the mail order pharmacy’s:

  • Name
  • Phone number

and we’ll handle the rest.

 

Please note that some mail-order pharmacies, such as Hims and Roman, require patients to contact them first before they will approve a prescription transfer.

If this applies to you, please contact the pharmacy that currently has the prescription before placing your Gold Home Delivery order.

Do I have to sign up for GoodRx Gold in order to get mail delivery?

Yes. Gold Home Delivery is only available to GoodRx Gold members using the GoodRx app, or our website.

Signing up for a 30-day free trial of GoodRx Gold is easy and takes less than 5 minutes!

Do I have to transfer my prescription in order to place a Gold Home Delivery order?

Is your prescription from:

  • A doctor’s office, local pharmacy, or mail order pharmacy
  • A GoodRx Care visit

 

My prescription is from a doctor’s office, local pharmacy, or mail order pharmacy

Yes, you must transfer a valid prescription for your Gold Home Delivery order to be successful.

Prescription transfers are not required for Gold Home Delivery refill orders, unless your prescription has no refills remaining.

 

 

My prescription is from a GoodRx Care visit

Unfortunately, prescriptions obtained via GoodRx Care are not eligible for Gold Home Delivery. You may transfer your prescription to a local pharmacy for pick-up or choose another mail delivery service.

How do I sign up for a GoodRx Care visit?

GoodRx Care is a Telehealth service that allows you to consult with licensed medical professionals for various treatments including mental health, sexual health, primary care and more– all from the comfort of your home.

Please note that you must be between the ages of 18 and 65 to use GoodRx Care telehealth services.

 

To get started with a GoodRx Care visit: 

  1. Visit GoodRx Care: Visit the GoodRx Care website and click ‘Get Started’ to begin the process.
  2. Choose your Service: Select the type of care or treatment you need from the available options
  3. Complete a Medical Questionnaire: Answer a few questions about your health to help the provider understand your needs.
  4. Confirm your visit: Complete the visit via text, call, or video with a licensed medical professional. 
  5. Pick up prescription: You can have your prescription sent directly to a local pharmacy

How do transfers work?

You can transfer prescriptions from:

  • A healthcare provider
  • A local pharmacy
  • A mail-order pharmacy

For pharmacy transfers, provide us with the name and phone number of the pharmacy where your prescription is located. For doctor transfers, provide your doctor’s name and phone number. 

We will handle the rest and notify you if there are any issues with transferring your prescription.

I have a Family Plan, can all of us use the benefits?

  • GoodRx Care (Telehealth): Only individual Gold account holders or the primary account holder of a Gold family plan can receive the Gold discount on GoodRx Care visits. Other family members on the plan will not receive the Gold discount for these visits. Please note that GoodRx Care telehealth services are only available for patients between the ages of 18 and 65.  
  • Gold Home Delivery: Free home delivery is available for all members and dependents on a Gold Family Plan. When placing an order, simply select the specific family member the prescription is for during the checkout process.  
  • GoodRx Rewards: Only the primary account holder on a Gold Family Plan is eligible to earn and redeem GoodRx Rewards points. Points cannot be awarded for coupon use associated with other individuals on the plan.  
  • Using Gold Card at the Pharmacy: All dependents on a family plan share the same BIN, PCN, Group, and Member ID. To ensure the correct discount is applied, the pharmacist must enter these exact codes when processing a claim for any member on a Gold family plan.

What is GoodRx Care?

GoodRx Care is a telehealth platform allowing patients to meet virtually with licensed healthcare professionals to treat a wide variety of conditions. Additional services offered include:

  • Prescription services
  • Lab tests
  • Short-term medication refills. 

Telehealth visits for GoodRx Gold Members are $19 per visit and start at $59.99 per visit for non-Gold members. 

To learn more about GoodRx Care or schedule a virtual doctor visit, visit GoodRx.com/care

GoodRx Care Basic Troubleshooting & Support

If you have additional questions or you’re experiencing issues related to GoodRx Care: 

  • Find answers and helpful troubleshooting information on the GoodRx Care Help Center
  • If you require further assistance, send a message to GoodRx Care’s dedicated support team through your GoodRx Care account (via the Message Center).
  • Give us a call at (855)-268-2822 anytime between 7AM - 8PM Central Time, 7 days a week.

How do I use GoodRx at the pharmacy?

Can I reuse my coupon? Do they expire?

GoodRx coupons themselves do not expire; you are able to re-use the coupons as many times as you would like. However, we recommend you always obtain a new coupon from the website before refilling so you are receiving the most up to date price when you pick up your prescription. Because coupon prices are tied to actual cash prices, which fluctuate over time, prices can change from month to month.

As prices fluctuate, we update our prices. The coupons we list have different sources and codes - so you’ll need to be sure you are using the most up-to-date coupon.

To make sure you get current prices, we do recommend printing a new coupon each time you fill or refill a prescription. Then, make sure the pharmacist enters the new coupon codes.

Can I use a GoodRx coupon for my pet? Can you help me find a pet coupon?

Yes! If your pet has been prescribed a human medication (like Latanoprost), most GoodRx coupons will work at local pharmacies or you can get pet medications shipped to your door with GoodRx for Pets*. To find prices and coupons for your pet, simply search for the medication name on our homepage like you would for any prescription. Exceptions to keep in mind: 

  • Discounts don’t apply to compounded medications. 
  • Discounts don’t apply to pet-only medications (e.g., heartworm, flea and tick treatments) but we’ll still help you find them at competitive prices and get them shipped from GoodRx for Pets*

When using a coupon for your pet, the pharmacist will need your vet’s DEA number and your pet’s birth date to process the prescription.

 

If the coupon isn’t working as expected. 

  • If the coupon isn’t processing, make sure the pharmacist has your vet’s DEA number, since vets don’t use NPI numbers like human doctors, and that the birth date entered for your pet matches what the vet provided. 

    • In some cases, the pharmacist may be able to enter a veterinarian’s state prescriber ID instead of the DEA number.  

  • Occasionally certain coupons will also come with restrictions that prevent you from being able to use it for a pet. If you run into a restriction with a coupon, we can help! We have other options available though prices may vary. Give us a call at 855-268-2822 Monday-Sunday between 7am to 8pm CT.

  • GoodRx for Pets is powered by a third-party partner.

Can I use my Health Savings Account (HSA) to purchase prescriptions with a GoodRx discount?

Yes! If you have a plan with a high deductible, you may also have a Health Savings Account (HSA) that you or your employer can pay into.

You can use the funds from your HSA on qualified medical expenses, including prescriptions purchased using a GoodRx discount card.

You can see a full list of qualified medical expenses here.

Do purchases with GoodRx count towards my deductible?

When using GoodRx instead of your insurance, the amount you pay will not be automatically applied toward your deductible.

You may want to contact your insurance company to find out if you can submit receipts for prescriptions purchased using GoodRx. Many insurance plans will allow you to submit receipts for potential reimbursement and/or credit toward your deductible and other policy limits.

GoodRx & GoodRx Gold Restrictions

Both free GoodRx and GoodRx Gold have some restrictions for when and how they can be used. 

  • Controlled substances
  • HSA / FSA
  • Insurance & Medicare

 

Controlled Substances

For GoodRx Gold members, please be aware that for controlled medications, your card will be accepted at the pharmacist’s discretion.

 

HSA / FSA

While you cannot use an HSA/FSA account to purchase a GoodRx Gold or GoodRx Companion membership, you may be able to pay for your prescriptions. Check with your HSA or FSA provider for more information.

 

Insurance & Medicare

You can use a GoodRx discount instead of your prescription insurance or Medicare if the cost is lower. However, GoodRx cannot be combined with your insurance or any federal or state-funded program such as Medicare or Medicaid. GoodRx is not insurance. 

 

If you choose to use a GoodRx coupon or your GoodRx Gold membership, it’s important to ask the pharmacist not to run your prescription through your insurance or Medicare.

 

While you can still use GoodRx discounts even if you’re enrolled in insurance or Medicare, GoodRx cannot be combined with either. For more information, see Using GoodRx While Having Insurance or Medicare

 

 

For more information, see GoodRx Terms of Use and the Gold Terms of Service.

How a drug purchased without Medicare affects limits

GoodRx shows you both levels of Medicare co-pay prices as well as other prices you can get when you pay cash, use a discount coupon, or use some other way besides your Medicare coverage.

When you purchase a drug with Medicare, the cost of that drug counts towards your plan’s limits. This can be a good thing (getting you out of the donut hole or a deductible stage more quickly), or a bad thing (putting you into the donut hole more quickly).

When you purchase a drug without using your Medicare plan, the cost of that drug will not typically count towards your limits. Some plans will allow you to submit receipts for drugs purchased without Medicare for credit towards your limits though, so check with your plan.

Ultimately, the decision whether to use your Medicare coverage is up to you. You’ll want to consider the savings you would receive by not using Medicare, your expected annual drug costs, and if you will spend enough on prescriptions to enter the donut hole or post-donut hole stages. Your Medicare plan can give you more information on your total spending and help guide your decision-making.

How can GoodRx help me save money if I’m already insured?

Your insurance plan’s co-pay may not be the lowest price. Many insurance plans have high deductibles or limited formularies that don’t cover the drugs you need.  

You may be surprised to learn that there are many discounts available that will provide lower prices than your co-pay. Some pharmacies have special generic priced medications for $4 — or even free! — without insurance.

Here are three things to consider when deciding whether to fill your prescription with your insurance or GoodRx:

  1. Is this drug covered by your plan? If so, how much will it cost?

    Insurance companies use “formularies” which list how much they will pay for a specific prescription. Formularies divide drugs into “tiers.” Tier 1 drugs may have a $20 co-pay, Tier 2 may be $40 and so on. For many brand-name drugs, your plan may cover just a percentage of a negotiated price.

    Check your insurance company’s website to see if they provide drug pricing, and then compare your co-pay to the price listed on GoodRx. You may be able to save money by using GoodRx and paying cash for drugs that are not “preferred.”

  2. Have you satisfied your deductibles?

    If you have a high deductible health plan (also known as an HDHP), you’ll be paying out of pocket for most prescriptions and medical services until you hit your deductible. 

    For expensive brand-name drugs, your plan’s price may not be as low as the prices GoodRx can find, especially if the drug isn’t on your plan’s formulary. In these cases, you’ll usually still get a discounted rate, but it may not be as low as the prices GoodRx can find. Check your insurance company’s website for more information.

  3. Can GoodRx beat your co-pay?

    Don’t be surprised if local and mail-order prices are well under your co-pay. The typical insurance plan in America offers a $10 co-pay for preferred drugs (and it goes up from there), while many generic drugs can be found for less than that.

If you use GoodRx in place of your health insurance, just ask the pharmacist to process the transaction using the coupon or discount card instead of your insurance. You can also use your Health Savings Account (HSA) to pay for prescriptions when you use a GoodRx discount.

Remember that the cost of prescriptions purchased using GoodRx discounts will not automatically count toward your deductible. If you have other medical expenses, or if you have several expensive prescriptions, it may make more financial sense to use your insurance to satisfy your deductible.

How can someone with Medicare save money with GoodRx?

Know the price of a drug before you go to the pharmacy

The cost of your prescriptions can change very frequently. It’s best to “know before you go” so that you can better understand your healthcare costs.

Learn about cost-saving alternatives

Once you can easily access prices, there are many ways you can save money on your prescriptions. Is your drug on formulary? Have you priced generic alternatives? Have you looked up the prices of alternative drugs which treat the same condition but cost less? Have you looked at purchasing a higher dosage pill or a greater quantity, which may cost less on a per-dose basis? Have you looked at mail order pharmacy options?

All of these options can be easily compared by looking up a drug on GoodRx.com.

Can you beat your Medicare drug insurance co-pay?

Medicare’s Part D and Advantage programs will bring down your drug costs, but they do not always offer the lowest prices. Many chain pharmacies offer membership clubs with low-cost generics. There are a variety of coupons and discounts available on GoodRx which can provide lower prices than your co-pay.

If your drug is not on your formulary, Medicare will provide limited coverage and you may want to explore the other options provided on GoodRx to bring down the cost of these drugs.

Avoid falling into the Medicare donut hole too soon

GoodRx and GoodRx Gold can be especially helpful if you have concerns about going into the donut hole too soon. You can use your Medicare plan for your more expensive brand drugs, and use GoodRx to purchase your less expensive generic medications. The cost of drugs purchased under GoodRx will not count against your coverage limit - thus keeping you out of the donut hole longer. 

It’s important to note that the amount you pay when using GoodRx does not automatically count towards your deductible or getting you out of the donut hole either.

If you are in the donut hole, and would like to use GoodRx, you may want to contact your insurance company to find out if you can submit receipts for prescriptions purchased using GoodRx. Many plans will allow you to submit receipts for potential reimbursement and/or credit towards your deductible and other policy limits.

 

Interested in learning more about how GoodRx can help you get the most out of your Medicare coverage? View our Medicare Guide here.

How do I Transfer My Prescription to a Different Pharmacy?

You can transfer your prescription to a different pharmacy by either:

  1. Contacting the pharmacy where you wish to transfer your prescription and providing them with your prescription details along with the contact details of the pharmacy where your prescription is currently on file
  2. Initiating a prescription transfer from a GoodRx price page (detailed below) 

 

How to Transfer your Prescription via GoodRx website

 

  1. Go to GoodRx.com

  2. Search for your medication

    • For more accurate pricing, make sure to enter your prescription details exactly as written and update your zip code to reflect your search area.

  3. Underneath any coupon on the pricing page for your prescription, click where it says Prescription not at [Pharmacy name]? Start a transfer to use this coupon **

  4. Follow the prompts to initiate a prescription transfer by selecting your preferred pharmacy location and the pharmacy where your prescription is currently on file

     

  5. When prompted, enter the patient information 

  6. Review your transfer and click Submit when ready

How do I use a GoodRx coupon at the pharmacy?

A GoodRx coupon works just like any coupon you take into the grocery store. At the time of checkout, hand the coupon to your pharmacist and save off the retail price of your prescription.

When you go to the pharmacist to pick up your prescription, let them know that you have a coupon before they scan your medication to begin checkout.

Or, if you’re bringing a paper prescription to the pharmacy, present the pharmacist with your GoodRx coupon along with your prescription. This way, the GoodRx coupon will be automatically applied when you pick up your medication!

I have Medicare. How can GoodRx help me?

GoodRx is here to help you get the most out of your Medicare coverage. From navigating plans, to price comparing your prescriptions, and more. Visit our resource page here.

To keep up with the latest news on Medicare, check out our GoodRx Health Medicare library.

Is GoodRx a pharmacy? Can I place an order with you?

No, GoodRx is not a pharmacy. We cannot accept or fill prescriptions of any kind. GoodRx lists prices and coupons for pharmacies across the United States, but we do not sell or prescribe medications.  

If you are seeking a health/medical service provider, visit GoodRx Care.

My pharmacy said I can’t use GoodRx because I have Medicare–is this true?

You can use most GoodRx discounts instead of your Medicare Part D or Advantage plan if the GoodRx price is less than your co-pay. However, you can’t combine the two. If the pharmacist tries to process the discount with your Medicare information, the discount will not be applied.

It’s important to note that while the vast majority of discounts can be used by Medicare-eligible patients, some discounts that are listed for brand name drugs cannot be.

As long as you are not using your Medicare or an InsideRx Brand Discount, pharmacies are contractually obligated to accept the coupons under most circumstances, so you shouldn’t run into any issues.

If you do have any trouble, please give us a call at (855) 268-2822 (Monday-Sunday, between the hours of 7am and 8pm CT). Our GoodRx Advocates are always happy to help, and can typically resolve most issues while you’re in the pharmacy.

Quantity limits, prior authorizations and step therapy

Many Medicare drug plans place restrictions on certain medications. You can find more information on restrictions for your prescriptions by clicking on the Medicare tab to the left of the prices on any drug page.

Some drugs on Medicare have quantity restrictions, i.e. you can only buy 30 pills in a 30-day period. GoodRx will tell you those quantity limits. If you choose not to use Medicare to make your drug purchase, you will not be limited by these restrictions.

Some drugs on Medicare have prior authorization requirements—basically, your Medicare plan won’t cover your prescription until it is approved. If you choose not to use Medicare to make your drug purchase, you will not be limited by these restrictions.

Many drugs on Medicare have step therapy requirements, which means your Medicare plan requires you to first try certain drugs to treat your medical condition before they will cover another drug for that condition. If you choose not to use Medicare to make your drug purchase, you will not be limited by these restrictions.

Sometimes Medicare plans will make exceptions to these restrictions; you’ll want to check with your plan to be sure.

Understanding pharmacy terminology.

NPI: The National Provider Identifier. NPI is a unique identification number for covered health care providers. 

 

NDC: National Drug Code. Serves as the FDA’s identifier for drugs.

 

DEA: Drug Enforcement Agency. You need to obtain a DEA number when filling your pet’s prescription. This code can be obtained from your veterinarian. 

 

Gold Person Code: The person code is used to individually identify each member on a GoodRx Gold account. All members, regardless of whether you have an Individual or a Family Plan, will have a person code assigned to them (e.g. John Doe = 01, Jane Doe = 02).

The pharmacist must enter the GoodRx Gold card information along with the correct person code for each member in order for the card to work correctly. Learn more here.

What do I do if I’m not eligible for the InsideRx discount?

If you do not qualify for the Inside Rx discount, you can still find other low prices and ways to save on GoodRx! When you click on the green “Get Free Discount” button next to the pharmacy name and price, we will ask you to confirm your eligibility to use the discount.

If you are not eligible, click on the blue link that says, “I don’t qualify. Please show me other options” at the bottom of the eligibility pop-up.

We’ll then send you to the next best available discount that does not require eligibility.

What is the difference between CVS and Target (CVS)?

CVS Pharmacy now has locations in Target retail stores. When you scroll through the list of pharmacies on GoodRx, you will see two separate CVS listings: “CVS Pharmacy” and “Target (CVS).

Pharmacies inside of Target stores are listed as “Target (CVS).”  If you fill your prescription(s) at a CVS pharmacy inside of a Target store, you will need to use the Target (CVS) coupon. Prices listed for Target (CVS) are only valid when filling your prescription at a CVS pharmacy inside of a Target store.

If you are filling your prescription(s) at a CVS Pharmacy that is not inside of a Target store, we recommend using the regular CVS Pharmacy coupon. The prices listed for CVS are the prices you will pay when filling your prescription at a free-standing CVS Pharmacy that is not located inside of a Target.

Eventually all Target (CVS) and CVS prices will be the same for all prescriptions, but for now, make sure you have the correct coupon, as prices may vary.

What is the InsideRx brand drug program? Who can use it?

What is Inside Rx and the Inside Rx Brand Drug Program?

Inside Rx is a prescription savings program powered by Express Scripts. Inside Rx and GoodRx have teamed up to deliver greater savings on brand-name drugs! Eligible patients can save an average of 34% off of the typical retail price for select brand-name medications which could translate into savings of over $1,600 per year.  

What brand-name drugs are covered under this program?

You’ll find Inside Rx discounts on over 100 brand-name prescriptions that treat diabetes (insulins), asthma (inhalers) and other common illnesses. You can check the full list of drugs covered under this program here.

Is there a fee to use Inside Rx?

No. There are no fees, signups or obligations required to receive these valuable discounts. Simply present the appropriate discount at a participating pharmacy to save.

What are the eligibility requirements?

You are Eligible to use the Inside Rx brand discount if you meet all of the following requirements:

  • You are not currently eligible for prescription coverage from any state or federally funded programs, including but not limited to Medicare, Medicaid, VA, DoD, or Tricare.
  • If you are filling a prescription for any of the following medications you must be under 65 years of age: Advair® Diskus®, Advair® HFA, Anoro® Ellipta®, Arnuity® Ellipta®, Breo® Ellipta®, Incruse® Ellipta®, Flovent® Diskus®, or Flovent® HFA.
  • You are not filling the prescription for a pet.
  • You have been prescribed this drug or are a caregiver of somebody taking this drug.
  • You are a resident of the United States or Puerto Rico. 

I’m 65+. Why can’t I use the Inside Rx discount card?

It is our goal to help all Americans with the cost of their medications. While most discounts provided on GoodRx can be used regardless of your age and Medicare status, state and federal laws place restrictions on how brand-name drugs can be discounted.

Federal laws prohibit those eligible for Medicare or government-sponsored health programs like Medicare, Medicaid or TRICARE from using Inside Rx discounts to purchase certain brand-name medications. All persons 65 years of age or older are considered Medicare-eligible, even if you do not currently have a Medicare plan or cannot purchase one.

If you are 65 years of age or older, you will not be able to use the Inside Rx discounts on the following medications: Advair® Diskus®, Advair® HFA, Anoro® Ellipta®, Arnuity® Ellipta®, Breo® Ellipta®, Incruse® Ellipta®, Flovent® Diskus®, Flovent® HFA.

Click here to see what you can do if you’re not eligible.

Can I use an Inside Rx brand discount if I have medicare?

No, you are not eligible to use the Inside Rx brand discounts if you are eligible for Medicare, Medicaid, TRICARE or other government-sponsored insurance plans.   

Can I use an Inside Rx discount if I have commercial insurance?

Yes! You can use Inside Rx discounts instead of your prescription insurance. This includes employer-sponsored health insurance and plans purchased from a health care exchange. However, the discounts cannot be used with your insurance or to lower your co-pay.

  • Plan Deductible: If you choose to use the Inside Rx discount instead of your insurance, the amount you pay will not be automatically applied toward your deductible. You may want to contact your insurance company to find out if you can submit receipts for prescriptions purchased using Inside Rx. Many insurance plans will allow you to submit receipts for potential reimbursement and/or credit towards your deductible and other policy limits.
  • Flexible Savings Account (“FSA”) or Health Savings Account (“HSA”): Depending on the terms of your FSA or HSA plan, they may be used to pay for your prescription when using your Inside Rx discount. You can use the funds from your HSA on qualified medical expenses, including prescriptions purchased using a discount card.  You can see a full list of qualified medical expenses here.

How our pricing works

Assistance with Medicare drug costs

If you’re having trouble paying for your Medicare prescriptions, there are resources available to help. Medicare offers Extra Help, which is a program for low-income seniors. You can learn more at the Official Website of the U.S. Social Security Administration. In addition, many non-profits and drug manufacturers offer patient assistance programs—we’ve described some of them here.

How do I find and use an InsideRx brand discount?

Benefiting from these exclusive discounts is easy; just follow the simple steps below:

1. Search GoodRx and select your discount

Search the GoodRx website for the name of your drug, and select the form, dosage, and quantity that match your prescription.

You should see a list of pharmacies and prices. Next to each price, you will see a green “Get Free Discount” button. If an Inside Rx Brand discount is available for your drug, you will see a notice that says “Exclusive! Restrictions apply” directly underneath this button (see image below).

If you see the “Exclusive! Restrictions Apply” notice, click on the green “Get Free Discount” button next to your preferred pharmacy.

2. Confirm eligibility

After clicking on the “Get Free Discount” button, a window will pop up asking you to confirm your eligibility. Read through all of the requirements.

If you meet all of the requirements and are eligible to use this discount, click on the green “PROCEED TO DISCOUNT” button.

If you are not eligible, click on the blue link that says, “I don’t qualify. Please show me other options” at the bottom of the eligibility pop-up.

3. Print or download the discount

Once you have the discount printed or pulled up on your phone, take it to a participating pharmacy with your prescription.

4. Show your card to your pharmacist

Pull up the discount card from the mobile app, or present the printed card. The pharmacist will enter the discount card codes to find your Inside Rx price.

5. Then pay and enjoy the savings. That’s it!

How do I find and use coupons for over-the-counter medications and medical supplies?

We offer prices and coupons for many popular over-the-counter medications, including: zyrtec, aspirin, vitamins, claritin, nicotine patches, and more. Find prices for your over-the-counter medication by searching the GoodRx website or mobile app for the drug or product name.

We also have discounts for medical supplies and devices like test strips, needles, and meters! You can view a list of medical supplies and devices listed on GoodRx by clicking here. 

 

Before you head to the pharmacy

Please note that using a GoodRx coupon for a non-prescription product works a bit differently than you may be used to. Follow these steps in order to use a GoodRx coupon and save on a non-prescription product:

  1. Get a doctor’s prescription. Though you do not need a prescription from a doctor to buy non-prescription products themselves, you will need a prescription in order to receive the GoodRx discount for the products. (Doctors often do write prescriptions for these products. Just call your doctor’s office and have them call or fax in the prescription to your preferred pharmacy.)
  2. Get a GoodRx coupon. Search the GoodRx website for the item you want to buy. Then print, email, or text yourself the appropriate coupon. Even better – download our free GoodRx mobile app which has coupons built in!
  3. Go to the pharmacy counter, not the cash register. After receiving your prescription, the pharmacist will locate and set this product aside for you. Many of these products are kept behind the pharmacist’s counter; if not, the pharmacist can help you find the right product and apply the coupon. Do NOT take your GoodRx coupon to the regular cashier at the front of the store – they will not be able to apply it to your purchase.

GoodRx discounts will often help you save, but also be sure to look for other store promotions. If you find a lower price for a product without using a GoodRx coupon, choose the lower price option instead. There is no commitment to using GoodRx; we just want you to find the maximum savings so that you can stay healthy.

Please note: Discounts for over-the-counter prescriptions are accepted at the pharmacist’s discretion; unlike regular prescription discounts, pharmacies are not obligated to honor discounts for over-the-counter medications and supplies.

 

What if I don’t see prices or coupons for my drug?

We do our best to list every over-the-counter medication, but there may be some that we are not able to list at this time.  We’re always on the look-out for new prices and coupons, so we recommend that you check back often to see if we were able to add pricing for your medication!

How do I find and use coupons for vaccines?

We offer prices and coupons for many vaccines. You can view a complete list of vaccinations available on GoodRx here. We’ll show you pharmacy cash prices and GoodRx coupons when prices are available.

How do I use a GoodRx coupon for a vaccine/immunization?

  1. You will need a prescription from a doctor. While you do not need a prescription to receive an immunization at your pharmacy, a prescription is required when purchasing the vaccine with a GoodRx coupon. (You can typically call your doctor’s office and have them call or fax in the prescription to your pharmacy.)
  2. Get a GoodRx coupon. Search for the name of the vaccine you need or select the vaccine from this page. Then, print, email or text yourself the appropriate coupon. Even better – download our free mobile app which has coupons built right in.
  3. Purchase the vaccine from your pharmacy. Tell the pharmacy that instead of administering the vaccine, you just want the package.
    • Some pharmacies may charge an additional fee if they administer the vaccine. This cost is sometimes automatically included in the pharmacy system, but is not included in the coupon price. Asking for just the package will help you to get the correct price on the vaccine. Alternatively, you can specify that you’d like them to remove the administration fee prior to processing the coupon.
  4. Have your pharmacist or doctor administer the vaccine. Keep in mind that you may need to pay an administration fee for this.

What should I do if the pharmacy charges me a higher price than listed on the coupon?

First, make sure you have a prescription from your doctor for the specific vaccine the pharmacy is giving you. The GoodRx coupon cannot be processed in the pharmacy’s system without a valid prescription.

It’s also important to note that some pharmacies may charge an additional fee if they administer the vaccine. This cost is not included in the coupon price.

Questions? GoodRx Advocates are here to help. Call 1-855-799-2553 for assistance.

Manufacturer programs

Many manufacturers offer patient assistance programs. It is easiest to find these programs through resources like those listed above where you can search by drug name, but they are also often linked on the manufacturer’s website or the official site for your medication. Here are a few examples:

  • AstraZeneca’s AZ and Me program (includes Crestor, Nexium, Seroquel and Symbicort)
  • GlaxoSmithKline’s Bridges to Access (includes Advair, Flovent, Imitrex and Lamictal)
  • Pfizer RxPathways (includes Celebrex, Effexor XR, Lipitor and Lyrica)

For drug-specific manufacturer coupons and programs, check the Savings Tips section on the left side of the drug pricing page after you search for your medication on GoodRx.

Prescription assistance programs

Need help paying for your prescriptions, or just looking to lower your monthly costs?

There are thousands of programs available from federal and state governments, non-profits, manufacturers and other organizations to help patients get the medications they need. Eligibility for these programs is typically based on income, insurance or Medicare status.

Medicaid

Medicaid coverage eligibility is different from state to state, but most states provide at least some coverage for low-income and disabled people. Starting in 2014, most adults under age 65 with individual incomes up to about $15,000 per year will qualify for Medicaid in every state. 

Find more information and see if you qualify for Medicaid assistance here.

Children’s Health Insurance Program (CHIP)

CHIP provides low-cost health coverage for children in families who earn too much money to qualify for Medicare. There is a CHIP available in every state, though some states have unique names for their programs.

You can get more information on the program in your state by visiting or calling 1-877-543-7669 (1-877-KIDS-NOW).

Community Health Centers

Community health centers are hospitals, clinics and health centers that provide free or low-cost care, typically using a sliding scale based on income. Anyone can use a community health center, whether they have health insurance or not. 

Here are a couple of resources for finding a community health center in your area:

  • US Health Resources and Services Administration
  • PPARX Free Clinic Finder

State Programs

Each state also offers a unique selection of assistance programs in addition to Medicaid, CHIP and community health centers. Search for programs available in your state here.

If you don’t qualify for or wish to participate in these programs, there are other ways to lower the price you pay for prescription drugs.

The 340B Drug Discount Program

If you are uninsured or underinsured, you may be able to reduce costs by receiving care at a facility participating in the 340B Drug Discount Program. If you pay out of pocket for your medical services, you can save up to half of the cost of your medications, regardless of your income or employment status.

Our partner, Community Catalyst, has an excellent guide to 340B programs.

How to find Patient Assistance programs

Directories

The website below provides information and directories of available assistance programs by location, medication, diagnosis, income and other factors:

  • Partnership for Prescription Assistance

Here you will find applications for assistance programs, FAQ sections, toll-free help lines and additional resources to help you find ways to save on your medications.

Tips for reducing the cost of your prescription drugs

Prescription drugs can be very expensive, especially if you don’t have insurance. Fortunately, there are many ways you can reduce the price you pay for the medications you or your family members need.

Generic vs. Brand Brand-name drugs are expensive. Generics are generally much cheaper. If you have been prescribed a brand-name drug, ask your doctor if there is a generic equivalent available that you could be prescribed instead. Generic drugs approved by the FDA often have the same dosages, strengths, forms, quality, performance, and intended uses as their brand-name equivalents. On GoodRx.com, we list both brand name drugs and generic equivalents.

Split a higher dosage pill

Always check with your doctor first to see if splitting higher dosage pills is a safe option for you.

For many drugs, an increase in dosage does not result in a corresponding increase in price. Many consumers ask their doctors to prescribe a higher dosage and then they simply split the pills in half to take the appropriate amount per day.

In example, if you need to take one 250mg tablet per day, your doctor may be able to prescribe you 500mg tablets and you would then take half a tablet (250mg) per day. 

Mail Order vs. Retail Mail-order pharmacies are often cheaper than retail pharmacies. This is particularly true if you have insurance — your co-pays may be lower if you order a 90-day supply through your insurance company.

However, you can also save by paying out of pocket and ordering online from sites like Mailmyprescriptions.com or your local pharmacy’s home delivery service. If you have a GoodRx Gold membership that is linked in the GoodRx mobile app, you have access to GoodRx Gold Mail Delivery!

Using GoodRx Gold Mail Delivery, you can order your prescriptions from the comfort and safety of home via the GoodRx mobile app. If free mail delivery is an option for your medication, you will see Gold Mail Delivery on that drug’s price page in the GoodRx app.

Ask the pharmacist Some pharmacies have price-matching programs, but not all of them advertise it. Many pharmacies will negotiate on prices if you ask.

Discount Coupons These coupons can reduce your cost up to 75% from the walk-in cash price. Most U.S. pharmacies accept these coupons.

Manufacturer Coupons Many brand-name drug manufacturers offer coupons (sometimes called co-pay cards) to offset the high cost of their drugs. GoodRx lists virtually all of these. Learn how to search for brand-name drugs on our site.

Pharmacy Discount Programs Many major pharmacies offer a limited list of discounted generic drugs. In addition, some pharmacies offer additional discounts when you join a free or paid membership program.

GoodRx Gold Our premium membership program GoodRx Gold allows you to find even better discounts and coupons than our regular free search resource. Try GoodRx Gold free for one month.

Types of prices: pharmacy membership prices, cash prices and online retail pharmacy prices

When you search for a prescription on GoodRx, we’ll sort through a number of prices to find you the best deal. As a result, we display pharmacy membership prices, cash prices and online retail pharmacy prices.

Membership prices: What does it mean when the price says,“with membership?”

If you see a “with membership” note underneath the price and a blue button that says “LEARN MORE,” this means the best price for this prescription is through a pharmacy membership program. Pharmacy membership programs are offered by some pharmacies, either for free or for an annual fee. To use these programs, customers must sign up for the membership through the listed pharmacy and use this membership when purchasing the prescription.

Click on the blue “LEARN MORE” button next to the price, and the price row will expand. We’ll let you know what the terms of a membership are and provide a link so you can learn more. You will see a blue button that says “GET MEMBERSHIP”. Clicking this button will take you directly to the pharmacy’s website.

Cash Prices: What does it mean when the price says “No Coupon Necessary?” 

When you see the “No Coupon Necessary” note next to a price on GoodRx, this indicates the price displayed is the pharmacy’s cash price (or retail price). This is the price that the pharmacy would charge for a specific prescription if you were to purchase the medication without using any discounts or insurance. To get this price, you do not need to present a card, a coupon or insurance; just go to the listed pharmacy and purchase your prescription!

GoodRx’s cash prices are based on multiple sources, including published price lists, purchases, claims records and data provided by pharmacies. Please be aware that cash prices are only estimates and can sometimes change very quickly. While we try to be as accurate as possible and make updates based on recent price increases or decreases, we always recommend checking with your pharmacy before filling your prescription.

Online Retail Pharmacies: What does it mean when the price says “Buy Online?”

If you see an orange “BUY ONLINE” button next to a pharmacy’s name and price, then that pharmacy is a mail-order pharmacy.  To receive the listed price, you will need to order this medication directly from the pharmacy’s website. You will not be placing an order through GoodRx; we will simply direct you to the listed pharmacy’s website.

Clicking on the orange “BUY ONLINE” button will open a new browser window, and you will be taken to the listed pharmacy’s website where you can place your order. A valid prescription will be required. If you have questions about your order, please contact the pharmacy directly.

We only list licensed U.S. mail-order pharmacies that are accredited by a nationally recognized professional organization that works to ensure the public’s health and safety.

How do I find the GoodRx coupon price if there is a pharmacy membership price, cash price or online retail pharmacy price?

If you click on the “LEARN MORE,” “NO COUPON NECESSARY” or “BUY ONLINE” button, you will see a tab that says “Prices”. Here we’ll show you the next best available coupon price. Click on the green “GET FREE COUPON” button to print, email or text the coupon to yourself.

What should I do if the pharmacy does not honor a membership or cash price? 

It is important to note, pharmacies are contractually obligated to honor the GoodRx coupons, while cash and pharmacy membership prices are estimates, as marked.

These listed membership prices are not offered through, or in affiliation with, GoodRx. You will not be able to receive a pharmacy membership price by using a GoodRx discount; we’re simply listing the pharmacy’s published prices.

While we try to be as accurate as possible with our cash and membership prices, certain pharmacy generic drug program prices may be higher in certain locations (CA, HI, MN, MT, PA, TN and WI), due to laws within these areas. We list the prices based on the pharmacy’s pricing list, but restrictions can apply, and we always recommend contacting the pharmacy ahead of time to verify the price.

Why did the price for my medication change?

Our mission at GoodRx is to stop consumers from paying too much at the pharmacy for their prescriptions, and we work hard every day to help you save as much as possible. If the price of your prescription recently increased, we sincerely apologize for this inconvenience.

GoodRx prices are based on pharmacy cash prices which fluctuate frequently—daily, weekly or monthly. We update our prices often, and you can count on the prices we list to be as accurate as possible. The good news is that just as prices can increase, they can also decrease.

Our dedicated GoodRx staffers track down pharmacy prices daily, posting the best available price for each prescription and pharmacy on our website and mobile app. While we are able to provide prices for comparison, we do not have control over the prices. 

Prices can increase for a number of reasons.

  • Higher cost to the manufacturer is one of the most common causes of a price increase, and you will see the difference passed on to you at the pharmacy.
  • Shortages can affect prices. If a popular medication isn’t widely available, the price may spike (sometimes temporarily, sometimes not). Shortages can be due to manufacturing issues, higher demand than expected or a shortage of the active ingredient, among other causes.
  • GoodRx finds prices from many different sources to provide Americans with the best savings. Occasionally, prices from these sources experience rate changes.

Unfortunately, there is no regulation requiring manufacturers, distributors or pharmacies to disclose the reason for their pricing, and there may not always be a clear-cut answer as to why a certain medication’s price has increased.

What can I do?

We never want there to be any surprises when you pick up your prescription, so we always recommend checking GoodRx every time you fill (or refill) a prescription. To make sure you still get the lowest price in your neighborhood, check prices on GoodRx.com and print out a new coupon each time you fill a prescription. If you have our mobile app, just ask your pharmacist to enter the new coupon information you find on the app.

If shopping around for the best price doesn’t work for you, GoodRx also has a program called GoodRx Gold. Because GoodRx Gold is a membership-based program, prices with GoodRx Gold are more consistent and do not update as frequently. See if GoodRx Gold is right for you and your family here. 

I found a better price at another pharmacy. How do I switch pharmacies?

If you find that a new pharmacy has a lower price, it’s easy to transfer your prescription. Simply call your new pharmacy and ask them to transfer your prescription for you. Remember to access a new coupon if it’s a discount price.

Men’s Hair Loss

Can I change my dosage on my medication? (Men’s Hair Loss)

We offer one dosage option for oral minoxidil and one dosage option for finasteride; dosage changes aren’t available.

Where is GoodRx Care Direct Available? (Men’s Hair Loss)

GoodRx Care Direct for men’s hair-loss treatment is available in all U.S. states and the District of Columbia except South Carolina.

Paying with Affirm

How do I make Affirm payments?

All payments on Affirm loans must be made by logging into Affirm.com or the Affirm mobile app. You can also select to set up automatic payments through Affirm.

  • For more Affirm-related questions, visit the Affirm help center.

What if my loan amount doesn’t match my prescription price with GoodRx?

If your loan amount is less than the price of your prescription with the GoodRx discount, you will be responsible for paying the difference when you pick up your prescription at the pharmacy.

If your loan amount ends up being more than the price of your prescription with the GoodRx discount, Affirm will automatically adjust your loan amount to match the current price of your prescription.

What is Affirm? How does Affirm financing work for GoodRx discounts?

GoodRx has partnered with Affirm, a buy-now-pay-later financing service, to offer 0% APR loans on select GoodRx-discounted medications at participating pharmacies. 

How do I get financing on my prescription through Affirm?

Follow the Affirm checkout process shown below to take out an Affirm loan* on eligible medications for the price displayed on the selected GoodRx coupon.

 

  1. From the GoodRx price page on any medication eligible for Affirm financing, select a price row for a pharmacy that displays an Affirm financing option

    • Note: In order to pick up your prescription at any given pharmacy, your prescription needs to be on file there. If it is not already, you will need to have your prescription transferred to the pharmacy you select

  2. Click Get Started to begin the Affirm checkout process

  3. Review the information on the ‘How it works’ screen, then click Continue to proceed

  4. If you have not already, you will be prompted to log in to your GoodRx account. If you do not have a GoodRx account, you will have the option to sign up for a free GoodRx account to continue your Affirm checkout.

  5. After signing in or signing up for your GoodRx account, review your prescription details and pharmacy selection and edit if necessary

    • Note: Editing the prescription details or pharmacy may impact the GoodRx discount price or Affirm loan availability

  6. Follow the steps below to complete the Affirm checkout process, then pick up your prescription **within 14 days

    **

  7. Once you have completed the purchase through Affirm, you will receive your prepaid coupon and order updates via the email address associated with your GoodRx account

    • Present your prepaid GoodRx coupon to the pharmacy where your prescription is on file to pick up your prescription within 14 days of the prescription fill date.
    • If you do not pick up your prescription with the prepaid coupon within 14 days of the fill date, the coupon will be voided and any payment you’ve made to Affirm will be refunded. 

 

*Affirm does not guarantee loan approvals. For more information on why loans may be declined, see the Affirm Help Center FAQ: Why was I declined?

Where can I find information about my Affirm orders?

To view information related to your previous Affirm orders:

  1. Click on Order History from your GoodRx account profile
  2. Click on a specific order to view order status, order summary, and details.

Why was my Affirm loan declined?

Affirm loan approval is not guaranteed. Neither GoodRx nor Affirm’s customer care team can change the outcome of any rejected loan application, provide specific details as to why your Affirm loan was declined, or suggest changes to your loan application to get it approved.

  • If your loan application is declined, you will receive an email from Affirm explaining those details.
  • For more information, see the related FAQ on Affirm’s own help center: Why was I declined?

Pharmacy Refusal: Miscellaneous

My pharmacy won’t accept GoodRx. What do I do?

In rare occurrences, a pharmacist may not be aware of the agreement they have to honor GoodRx coupons, although they are contractually obligated to accept them.

If you have an issue using a GoodRx discount at your pharmacy, please give us a call at +1 (855) 268-2822 (Monday-Sunday, between the hours of 7am and 8pm CT).  Our GoodRx Advocates are always happy to help, and can typically resolve most issues while you’re in the pharmacy.

  • Online: Placing a Gold Home Delivery Order

  2. Tap the Gold Home Delivery price 

 

  1. Review the order summary and tap Start order

 

  1. If you have a Family plan, select the member that the order is for, then tap Next

 

  1. Select whether you have an existing prescription, then tap Next

To place the Home Delivery order, you must have an active prescription on file.

               

 

  1. Review the prescription details, then tap Confirm

The order can only be delivered if the order details match exactly what’s on your prescription.

 

  1. Select whether your prescription is currently with your local pharmacy or your doctor’s office then tap Next

 

  1. Enter the Name and Phone number of the pharmacy or doctor’s office then tap Next

               

 

  1. Answer the questions on the screen, use the boxes to explain any “Yes” answers – these questions are important to ensure that we deliver your prescriptions safely. Tap Next.

 

  1. Review the order details and indicate whether you would like to enroll in Auto-refills 

 

  1. Enter your preferred mobile phone number and indicate whether you would like to speak to pharmacist about the order or receive order updates and refill reminders via SMS text

 

  1. Tap Confirm to complete the order
