Points Balance: Miscellaneous

I had a GoodRx account and earned rewards, and now have a Gold membership. Will my rewards carry over?

If you earned a referral bonus before signing up for Gold, you can still redeem your bonus using the link provided in your reward confirmation email.

Prescription Transfer Assistance: Miscellaneous

How do I Transfer My Prescription to a Different Pharmacy?

You can transfer your prescription to a different pharmacy by either:

  1. Contacting the pharmacy where you wish to transfer your prescription and providing them with your prescription details along with the contact details of the pharmacy where your prescription is currently on file
  2. Initiating a prescription transfer from a GoodRx price page (detailed below) 

 

How to Transfer your Prescription via GoodRx website

 

  1. Go to GoodRx.com

  2. Search for your medication

    • For more accurate pricing, make sure to enter your prescription details exactly as written and update your zip code to reflect your search area.

  3. Underneath any coupon on the pricing page for your prescription, click where it says Prescription not at [Pharmacy name]? Start a transfer to use this coupon **

  4. Follow the prompts to initiate a prescription transfer by selecting your preferred pharmacy location and the pharmacy where your prescription is currently on file

     

  5. When prompted, enter the patient information 

  6. Review your transfer and click Submit when ready

Pricing Discrepancy at the Pharmacy

I keep having pricing discrepancies at the pharmacy when I present a coupon. How can I avoid this?

  1. On goodrx.com, our prescription settings default to the most commonly prescribed medication which is always the generic version with frequently associated form / dosage / quantity information. To ensure that a coupon is processed correctly, make sure you adjust the drop down menu options to match the brand / form / dosage / quantity on your prescription.

  2. GoodRx drug prices are tied to the most commonly filled NDC (drug manufacturer) for the particular prescription configuration & pharmacy on the coupon. Please note that if a pharmacy does not have that particular NDC in stock, they may receive a different price when processing the coupon. 

  3. If you experience any pricing issues, we recommend calling us before you pay so we can verify the details with the pharmacy and ensure you receive the lowest price. You can reach our Patient Advocacy team at 855-268-2822. We are available Monday through Sunday, from 6 AM to 9 PM Central Time.

My pharmacy information needs to be updated

Information for pharmacies listed on GoodRx — store hours, phone number, address and services provided — are based on information from the NCPDP (National Council for Prescription Drug Programs).

In order to update the information for your pharmacy, you’ll need to provide an update to the NCPDP. They provide a portal to view or modify pharmacy data on their website here.

Once you have updated your pharmacy information with the NCPDP, GoodRx will show the updated information within 30 days.

The pharmacy accepted my coupon, but the price is wrong. What do I do?

The prices listed on GoodRx are updated constantly and are generally very accurate, but if you believe you were charged the wrong price at the pharmacy when using a GoodRx coupon, there are a few things you will want to check.

First, does the coupon you used match the information on your pharmacy receipt?

  • Does the name of the medication on your receipt match the coupon? (i.e. “metoprolol” vs. “metoprolol ER – extended release”)
  • Does the form (i.e. tablet, capsule or cream) and strength (i.e. 20mg or 1%) listed on your coupon match the form and strength on your receipt?
  • Does the quantity that was filled match the quantity on your coupon?

If your coupon does not match the prescription that was filled for you, return to goodrx.com or the mobile app and use the menu to select the right options for your prescription. You can return to the pharmacy and request they reprocess the coupon. If you have already purchased the medication, reimbursement will be at the discretion of the pharmacy.

Second, did you use the most current coupon from the website or mobile app?

GoodRx includes discounts from multiple sources to help make sure you always receive current and competitive prices. If you’re asked to pay a higher price than expected, it may be because the coupon your pharmacy has on file for you is out of date or you’re using an older discount card instead of the most current coupon.

To make sure you get current prices available, check prices on goodrx.com and print out a new coupon the next time you refill. If you have our mobile app, just ask your pharmacist to enter the new coupon information you find on the app.

Pharmacies are contractually obligated to accept GoodRx discounts under most circumstances, and issues are rare.

If you have experienced an issue with a price at your pharmacy, please give us a call at +1 (855) 268-2822 (Monday-Sunday, between the hours of 7am and 8pm CT).  Our GoodRx Advocates are always happy to help, and can typically resolve most issues while you’re in the pharmacy.

The pharmacy accepted my GoodRx Gold card, but the price is wrong. What do I do?

The prices listed on GoodRx Gold are generally very accurate, and issues are rare. Our prices are backed by contracts, meaning that each participating pharmacy has agreed to accept them.

If you believe you were charged the wrong price at the pharmacy, there are a few things you will want to check:

 

Does the drug you looked up on GoodRx Gold match the prescription you’re filling?

You may be viewing a price that isn’t exactly the same as the prescription you’re filling (this is the most common reason for price differences). First, confirm that the price you looked up matches your prescription. Be sure to check the drug name (i.e., “divalproex” vs. “divalproex ER extended release”), dosage, form and quantity.

 

Are you filling your prescription at a Gold Participating Pharmacy?

GoodRx Gold is not available at some pharmacies (click here to locate Gold participating pharmacies). If you are trying to fill your prescription at a pharmacy that does not accept Gold, you can either:

  • Transfer your prescription to a Gold participating pharmacy
  • Use a free GoodRx coupon instead

 

Are you filling your prescription at a CVS Pharmacy or Target (CVS)?

CVS may have slightly different prices than other Gold participating pharmacies. When you search for a price on GoodRx Gold, we will show you two prices: one price will be for CVS Pharmacy, and the other price will be for all other GoodRx Gold participating pharmacies.  

If you are filling your prescription at a CVS Pharmacy or Target (CVS), check the GoodRx Gold website again to make sure you were viewing the CVS Pharmacy price.

 

Still not receiving the correct price?

Our GoodRx Gold Advocates are here to help, and they can typically resolve most issues while you’re in the pharmacy. Please give us a call at +1 (855) 487-0694 (Monday-Sunday, between the hours of 7am and 8pm CT).

Privacy

California Consumer Privacy Notice

Last Updated: June 25, 2026

This California Consumer Privacy Act (“CCPA”) notice (this “CCPA Notice”) is incorporated into GoodRx’s (“GoodRx,” “we,” “us,” or “our”) general privacy policy found here (and other related policies). This CCPA Notice provides additional information solely for California “consumers” (as such term “consumers” is defined under the CCPA) (collectively, “Consumers,” “you,” or “your”) and applies solely to the personal information of such Consumers, whether collected online or offline. In the event of a conflict between this CCPA Notice and any of our other privacy policies, this CCPA Notice shall control only with respect to Consumers and their personal information.

General Information Regarding Your Personal Information

Categories of Personal Information We Collect

We collect the following categories of Personal Information via our Services (and have done so within the past 12 months when engaging with our Services): 

  • Identifiers (such as your name, home, work, billing, and/or shipping addresses, home and/or mobile phone number, email address, online identifiers, username and password for your account with us)
  • Categories of Personal Information listed in Section 1798.80(e) of the California Customer Records Act (such as name, home, work, billing, and/or shipping addresses, home and/or mobile phone number, health and medical information, credit card information, debit card number, health and medical information)
  • Characteristics of protected classifications under California or federal law (such as age, gender)
  • Commercial information (such as products or services considered or used, or the fact that someone has considered or used products or services)
  • Internet or other electronic network activity information (such as browsing history, search history, and information regarding your interaction with our website, mobile application, or advertisements)
  • Geolocation data (such as zip code, home, work, billing, and/or shipping addresses)
  • Audio, electronic, visual or similar information (such as survey responses, reviews or testimonials that you post or send us, messages you send us and other information that you type in an open text field on our websites or mobile application or communicate to us over the phone)
  • Professional or employment-related information (such as National Provider Identification numbers, medical specialty)
  • Education information (such as how many years you attended school)
  • Inferences (such as potential interest in our content or services)
  • Sensitive personal information (such as data concerning a Consumer’s health, account log-in details). 
    • We process sensitive information for purposes where a Consumer can opt out (as such opt out is described further below).

The Business or Commercial Purposes for Which We Use Your Personal Information. We use your personal information in order to provide our services (including maintaining or servicing accounts, providing customer service, processing or fulfilling orders and transactions, verifying customer information, processing payments), to directly or indirectly enable or effectuate commercial transactions, to run our business (including for internal research for technological development and demonstration, activities to verify or maintain the quality of the services or improving, upgrading or enhancing our services, debugging to identify and repair errors that impair existing intended functionality, internal business operations, analytics, and to provide, develop, change, market or optimize our services and products), to communicate and market to you, to personalize communications and marketing (whether regarding our Services or third-party services we believe you may find interesting) and to measure the effectiveness of communications and marketing (directly or via third parties), to detect security incidents, protect against malicious, deceptive, fraudulent, or illegal activity, or prosecute those responsible for that activity, debugging to identify and repair errors that impair existing intended functionality. Please see our Privacy Policy for more information about how we use your personal information.

Sources of Personal Information. As also discussed in our general Privacy Policy, we collect personal information from various sources, including directly from the Consumer and from advertising networks, internet service providers, data analytics and marketing providers, operating systems and platforms, social networks, data brokers, healthcare providers, and pharmacy benefit managers.

Categories of Personal Information We “Sell” or “Share”. The CCPA defines “sales” broadly to include not only traditional selling of data, but also many sharing arrangements where the recipient can use the personal information that they receive for the recipient’s own commercial purposes. The CCPA defines “shares” broadly to include disclosures of personal information to third parties for cross-context behavioral advertising (or “targeted advertising,” as such practice is more commonly referred to).

We make certain categories of personal information available to third parties in order to receive targeted advertising-related services, such as when we make browsing information available (e.g., through third-party tags on our digital properties) to carry out, measure, and improve our ad campaigns and reach users with more relevant ads and content. These disclosures may be considered a “sale” and “share” of personal information under the CCPA. 

More specifically, the categories of Personal Information that we may have “sold” and “shared” in the past twelve (12) months are: identifiers; categories of personal information listed in Section 1798.80(e) of the California Customer Records Act; commercial information; internet or other electronic network activity information; geolocation information; electronic or similar information; professional or employment-related information; inferences; and sensitive personal information. The categories of third parties to whom we have “sold” or “shared” such data in the past twelve (12) months: are data analytics providers, advertising networks, and social networks. We do not have actual knowledge of “selling” or “sharing” personal information of individuals under 16 years of age. 

Data Retention: We retain the categories of personal information listed above as reasonably necessary to fulfill the purposes outlined in this notice, unless a longer retention period is required or permitted by law. In many situations, we must retain all, or a portion, of your personal information to comply with legal obligations, resolve disputes, enforce our agreements, protect against fraudulent, deceptive, or illegal activity, or for another one of our business purposes.

Your Consumer Rights Under the CCPA

This section describes your various rights in relation to your personal information under the CCPA. For more information on how to effectuate such rights, please see the Submitting CCPA Requests section below.

Right to Know. You have the right to request, free of charge, that we disclose information about our collection and use of your personal information over the past twelve (12) months (or longer, unless this proves impossible or involves disproportionate effort), including:

  • The categories of personal information we collect about you;
  • The categories of sources of the personal information we collect about you;
  • The business or commercial purpose for collecting, “selling,” or “sharing” the personal information;
  • The categories of third parties to whom the business discloses personal information; and
  • The specific pieces of personal information we’ve collected about you.

Right to Request Deletion. You have the right to request the deletion of your personal information that we’ve collected from you, subject to certain exceptions. 

Right to Correct.You have the right to correct any inaccuracies in your personal information, taking into account the nature of the personal information and the purposes of the processing of the personal information. 

Right to Request Opt-Out of the “Sale” and “Share” of Your Personal Information. The CCPA allows you to opt-out of “sales” and “shares” of personal information, as such terms are further described above. 

Right to Limit Use and Disclosure of Sensitive Personal Information.The CCPA allows you to limit our uses/disclosures of “sensitive personal information” to certain reserved purposes. 

Right of Non-Discrimination. You have the right to not receive discriminatory adverse treatment  for exercising any of your CCPA rights.

Submitting CCPA Requests

Requests to Know or Delete. To exercise your rights to know, delete, or correct, you may submit a request by contacting us by phone at the toll-free number, +1 (855) 817-7639. You may also submit a request to know at goodrx.com/datarequest and a request to delete at goodrx.com/requestdeletion.  You can also have an authorized agent make a request via such links by following the instructions therein. 

Requests to Correct. To exercise your right to correct, please log into your account and edit the information therein.Please note that we may be unable to process certain requests to correct, depending on the nature of the information and the purposes of the processing of the personal information.

How to Request to Opt-Out of “Sales” and “Shares” of Your Personal Information and Limiting Our Use/Disclosure of Sensitive Personal Information.  To opt-out, please click our “Your Privacy Choices” link (found here and at the bottom of our website homepage) and follow the instructions therein.  As set forth in the link above, you may use the Global Privacy Control (GPC) browser extension for opt-outs on the browser level in relation to “cookies.” Learn more about global privacy controls at globalprivacycontrol.org.

Verification of Requests

Only you, or an individual legally authorized to act on your behalf, may make a request to know, delete, correct, or opt-out related to your personal information. You may only submit a request to know twice within a 12-month period. Your request to know, delete, or correct must:

  1. Provide sufficient information that allows us to reasonably verify you are the person about whom we collected personal information, or that the requestor is your authorized representative.
  2. Describe your request with sufficient detail that allows us to properly respond to it.

We may request information from you in order to verify your identity. Additionally, we may require that authorized agents provide proof of their authorization. We cannot respond to your request if we are unable to verify your identity, your authority to make a request on behalf of another person, or if we cannot confirm that the personal information relates to you. In general, we will verify your request and your identity by matching the information you provided with the information we have in our records.

You do not need to create an account with us to submit a request to know, correct, or delete your personal information or opt-out of “sales” or “shares” of your personal information or certain use/disclosure of sensitive personal information.

We will only use personal information provided in the request to verify the requestor’s identity or authority to make it.

Notice of Financial Incentives

We make various programs available to you that may be considered “financial incentive” programs under the CCPA.

  1. GoodRx Rewards Program and Other Account-Based Discount Offers

Through the GoodRx Rewards Program (“Rewards Program”) and other account-based discount offers (“Offers”), GoodRx offers various “financial incentives” (as set forth under the CCPA) to encourage you to register for an account and use GoodRx to fill prescriptions. The Rewards Program and Offers are only available to registered users who qualify per these Rewards Program Terms and Conditions or Offer Terms and Conditions. For more information about the Rewards Program or Offer, please see the Rewards Program Terms and Conditions and/or Offer Terms and Conditions that are available when you sign up for the Rewards Program or an Offer. 

The categories of personal information that GoodRx collects to administer the Rewards Program or Offer include: 

  1. Identifiers (such as name, date of birth or e-mail address); 
  2. Commercial information (such as records of coupons used); 
  3. Internet or other electronic network activity information (such as browsing history and information regarding your interactions with an internet website or mobile application); and
  4. Sensitive personal information (such as data concerning health, account log-in information)

As an example of an Offer, GoodRx may offer a $5 or $10 automatic savings coupon for a qualifying prescription to encourage you to register for an account and use a GoodRx coupon. Qualified participants who use the designated coupon within 30 days of qualifying using the provided coupon will automatically receive the stated amount of savings on their prescription at the pharmacy. You have the right to withdraw from the Offer at any time by not utilizing your GoodRx accounts further. You can continue to use GoodRx services without participating in the Offer.

Another example, when you initially enroll in the GoodRx Rewards Points Program, you automatically receive GoodRx Rewards Points in accordance with the GoodRx Rewards Point Value System. You then have the option to redeem the GoodRx Rewards Points accrued as a part of this Rewards Program in the form of a digital gift card. Qualified participants will receive a digital gift card via email after they redeem their GoodRx Rewards Points. You may opt-in to the Rewards Program by registering for a free account on the website and using GoodRx to fill prescriptions to accrue GoodRx Rewards Points. You have the right to withdraw from the Rewards Program at any time by not utilizing your GoodRx accounts further. You can continue to use GoodRx services without participating in the GoodRx Rewards Program.

The financial incentive GoodRx offers to you through the Rewards Program or Offer is reasonably related to the value of your data. To form the basis for offering the financial incentive, GoodRx has valued the personal information it obtains through the program based on a reasonable and good faith calculation determined by considering expenses related to the offer, provision, or imposition of any financial incentive as permitted under the CCPA. 

For the Rewards Program and the Offers, the amount of expenses associated with this Rewards Program, is calculated by measuring the proportionate IT, product, and development costs incurred for the third quarter of 2022 annualized to calculate full-year amounts, the specific details of which we maintain as a trade secret.

  1. GoodRx Gold Subscription

The GoodRx Gold Subscription Program (“GoodRx Gold”) is a monthly membership program that provides Consumers with discounts on prescription drugs and healthcare services through its website, mobile application, and membership cards issued to Consumers. The categories of personal information that GoodRx collects to administer GoodRx Gold include: (1) identifiers (such as name, e-mail address and mailing address; (2) other categories of personal information described in subdivision (e) of Civil Code Section 1798.80 (credit card number or debit card number); (3) commercial information (such as records of services purchased or obtained); (4) Internet or other electronic network activity information (such as browsing history, search history, and information regarding Consumer interactions with an internet website or mobile application); and (5) sensitive personal information (such as data concerning health, account log-in information)

For more information regarding GoodRx Gold, please see the full Terms of Service, available at support.goodrx.com.

Consumers may opt-in to this program by visiting the GoodRx Gold website at gold.goodrx.com/sign-up or downloading the GoodRx mobile application and completing the registration process. Consumers have the right to withdraw from GoodRx Gold at any time and may withdraw by canceling their GoodRx Gold memberships by logging into their GoodRx Gold account via the website or mobile application.

The financial incentive GoodRx offers to Consumers through GoodRx Gold is reasonably related to the value of the Consumers’ data. To form the basis for offering the financial incentive, GoodRx has valued the personal information it obtains through the GoodRx Gold program based on a reasonable and good faith calculation determined by considering expenses related to the offer, provision, or imposition of any financial incentive as permitted under the CCPA. The amount of expenses associated with this program is calculated by measuring the people costs and proportionate IT, product, and development costs incurred for the third quarter of 2022 annualized to calculate full-year amounts, the specific details of which we maintain as a trade secret.

California Personnel and Job Applicant Privacy Notice

Last Updated: December 23, 2025

Employee, Contractor, and Applicant Privacy Notice

This Employee, Contractor, and Applicant Privacy Notice (this “Notice”) explains how GoodRx and its affiliates (collectively, “GoodRx, “we,” “us,” or “our”) processes personal information about our California-based employees, contractors, and job applicants (collectively, “personnel” or “you” or “your”) and their family members or other individuals about whom we process personal information as a result of the relationships those individuals have with such personnel.

For purposes of this Notice, “personal information” has the meaning given in the CCPA. It does not include, and this Notice does not cover, information that is deidentified or aggregated such that it cannot reasonably be used to infer information about, or otherwise be linked to, a particular individual. Where we maintain deidentified information, we will maintain and use the information in deidentified form and not attempt to reidentify the information except as required or permitted by law.

This Notice applies to GoodRx’s processing of your personal information as an employee, contractor, or job applicant and does not cover your use of GoodRx’s products and services as a consumer or outside of your regular employment or of the job application process.

If you use the GoodRx website and services, please see the GoodRx privacy policy (goodrx.com/privacy-policy) for additional information.

Categories of Personal Information We May Collect: For each category of personal information, the list of examples is intended to be illustrative and not exhaustive. In connection with an individual’s role as a member of our personnel, we may not collect every example listed within a category nor use every example for each business purpose identified within each category. Please note, in addition to the business purposes defined below, GoodRx processes the following categories of personal information for compliance with legal and/or regulatory obligations.

  • Categories of Personal Information Collected: Examples Business Purposes

  • Identifiers :

    • Name
    • Email address
    • Home address
    • Business address
    • IP address
    • Phone number 
    • Social Security Number
    • Online identifiers
    • Personnel ID (e.g., government or state-issued identification)

     

    • HR administration, such as benefits, payroll, and other employment-related purposes.
    • Job Application Process

    • Product beta testing (voluntary opt-in collection only)

  • Protected classification information:

Sensitive Information: The following personal information elements, which are also identified above, may be classified as “sensitive” under the CCPA (“sensitive information”): Social Security number, government or state-issued identification, work-related accounts and passwords, race, national origin, ethnicity, disability or medical information, sexual orientation, and financial account information. We may use this sensitive information for the purposes set forth regarding personal information.

In addition to the business purposes set forth above, we may use these categories of personal information:

  • To fulfill or meet the reason you provided the personal information;
  • To comply with our health, safety, compliance, and fraud-prevention obligations;
  • To create anonymous, aggregated or de-identified data that we use and share to analyze our application and recruitment activities, business and for other lawful business purposes;
  • To authorize, grant, administer, monitor, and terminate access to or use of company systems, facilities, records, property, and infrastructure
  • To manage business travel (e.g., car service/rentals, flights, hotels) and personnel expenses (e.g., reimbursement, corporate credit card).
  • To facilitate personnel compensation, payroll, and benefit planning and administration (e.g., salary, tax withholding, insurance); 
  • To carry out discipline and investigations as necessary;
  • To facilitate personnel and third party safety and loss prevention;
  • To establish, exercise, or defend legal claims;
  • To comply with applicable law, court orders, subpoenas, and governmental regulations or investigations;
  • To respond to requests by public authorities, including for the purpose of meeting national security or law enforcement requirements;
  • In the event of a merger, acquisition, disposition of all or substantially all of the relevant assets of the business, bankruptcy, or similar event (including due diligence related thereto); 
  • As described to you when collecting your personal information; or
  • As otherwise authorized or required by applicable law.

We may use de-identified Personal Information in accordance with applicable law.

Disclosure of Personal Information: We may share your personal information with other parties as necessary for the purposes described above. For example, we may disclose your personal information with:

  • Affiliates. Our subsidiaries, and other affiliates for purposes consistent with this Notice or to operate shared infrastructure, systems, and technology.
  • Company service providers. Companies that provide us with services that help us manage the recruiting process and operate our business, such as job boards, recruiters, interviewing and testing, pre-employment screening, interview travel booking and expense reimbursement (where applicable), relocation (where applicable), recruitment analytics, benefit administrators, compensation, payroll, and tax providers.
  • Government authorities, law enforcement and others. Government authorities, law enforcement, courts, and others as described in the compliance, safety and fraud prevention section above.
  • Business transfers. Parties to transactions and potential transactions whereby we sell, transfer, or otherwise share some or all of our business or assets, including your personal information, such as a corporate divestiture, merger, consolidation, acquisition, reorganization or sale of assets, or in the event of bankruptcy or dissolution.
  • Professional advisors. Lawyers, immigration advisors, and other outside professional advisors.
  • Customers and business partners. Customers, other companies, and individuals with whom the Company does business or is exploring a business relationship.

We do not sell or share any information regarding employment-related personal information to third parties for monetary or other valuable consideration. However, as stated above, in the event of a merger to, acquisition by, or similar event relating to a third party, we may transfer your personal information to the new owners/entities pursuant to such event.

Finally, we may also use and share de-identified information in accordance with applicable law. Once de-identified, we will not attempt to re-identify your information except to ensure de-identification is working as expected. When we do so, we have reasonable measures in place to prevent the information from being reassociated with you. And, we obligate recipients of de-identified information to maintain and use such de-identified in accordance with applicable law.

Retention of Personal Information: We retain the categories of personal information listed above as reasonably necessary to fulfill the purposes outlined in this notice, unless a longer retention period is required or permitted by law. In many situations, we must retain all, or a portion, of your personal information to comply with legal obligations, resolve disputes, enforce our agreements, protect against fraudulent, deceptive, or illegal activity, or for another one of our business purposes.

Relatedly, we may retain application information for unsuccessful applicants beyond consideration of the immediate position for which the application is submitted as a reference in case future positions become available and are of interest. Application information for successful applicants becomes part of the applicant’s employment file and is treated accordingly. 

Sources of Personal Information: We may collect Personal Information from a variety of sources, including as follows: 

  • Directly or automatically collected from you 
  • Inferences we may derive from information we have collected 
  • References provided by you, including former and current employer 
  • Service providers (e.g., expense reimbursement services, recruiters, cookies and similar technologies related to our providers’ services) 
  • Insurance and benefits providers 
  • Travel partners (e.g., travel agents and portals, car service, and ride share companies) 
  • Background check services 
  • Online sources including social networks and recruiting sites (e.g., LinkedIn) 
  • Open government databases and other public sources 

Your Privacy Rights:

We will get consent from you for the collection, use, and disclosure of Personal Information where such consent is required by applicable law. Failure to provide necessary Personal Information may disqualify you from employment or contract or restrict participation in certain plans or programs.

For California residents, under the CCPA, you have the following rights with respect to Personal Information we process about you, subject to permitted exemptions and limitations under applicable law.

Right to Limit Use and Disclosure of Sensitive Personal Information.  Where we use or disclose Sensitive Personal Information to infer individual characteristics or for purposes other than those permitted by CCPA, you have a right to request that we limit our use and disclosure of such Sensitive Personal Information.  Note that we do not use or disclose Sensitive Personal Information to infer individual characteristics or for additional purposes.

Right to Know. You have a right to request that we disclose the Personal Information we have collected about you. You also have a right to request additional information about our collection, use, disclosure, sale, or sharing of Personal Information. Note that we have provided much of this information in this Notice.  

Right to Request Correction. You have the right to request the correction of inaccurate Personal Information.  

Right to Request Deletion. You also have a right to request that we delete Personal Information under certain circumstances, subject to lawful exceptions.  

Right to Notice. You have a right to receive notice of our Personal Information collection, use, retention, and disclosure practices at or before the collection of Personal Information.  

Right to Non-Discrimination. You have a right not to be discriminated against for exercising these rights set out in the CCPA. 

Exercising Your Rights: If you are a California resident, you can submit a GoodRx Employee Data Deletion Request form for the right to delete and the GoodRx Employee Data Access Request form for the right to know or correct. You may also contact us at for any other privacy rights requests or questions. 

Further, to process your privacy rights requests, such as to provide, correct, or delete specific pieces of Personal Information, we will need to verify your identity to the degree of certainty required by law. We will verify requests by asking you to send the request from the email address associated with your request or by requiring you to provide additional information reasonably necessary to verify your identity.

If you have any questions, please contact privacyteam@goodrx.com.

Submitting Authorized Agent Requests: In certain circumstances, you are permitted to use an authorized agent to submit requests on your behalf through the designated methods set forth above where we can verify the authorized agent’s authority to act on your behalf. In order to verify the authorized agent’s authority, we generally require evidence of either (i) a valid power of attorney or (ii) a signed letter containing your name and contact information, the name and contact information of the authorized agent, and a statement of authorization for the request. Depending on the evidence provided, we may still need to separately reach out to you to confirm the authorized agent has permission to act on your behalf and to verify your identity in connection with the request.

Changes to this Notice: We may occasionally update this Notice to reflect changes required by law or our practices or procedures. If we make material changes to this Statement, or in how we use Personal Information, we will provide notice (or obtain consent) regarding such modifications as may be required by law.

Consumer Health Data Privacy Notice

**Last Updated: September 12, 2025 Effective Date: March 31, 2024

**

Washington Residents: This Consumer Health Data Privacy Notice is incorporated into GoodRx’s (“GoodRx,” “we,” “us,” or “our”) general privacy policy found here and other related policies that link to this notice, and applies to personal data defined as “consumer health data” (“CHD”) under Washington law.

 

Consumer Health Data We Collect

The CHD we collect depends on how you interact with us, the services you use, and the choices you make. Because CHD is defined very broadly, many of the categories of data we collect are or could be considered CHD.  

Such data may also be categorized as follows:

  • Information about your health-related conditions, symptoms, status, diagnoses, testing, or treatments (including surgeries, procedures, medications, or other interventions). For example, we may collect such information through surveys or other communication with you for research studies and improving product accessibility. We may also collect information about your use of a coupon provided by GoodRx (if the corresponding pharmacy benefit manager or applicable pharmacy discloses information about the transaction to us).
  • Precise location information that could reasonably indicate your attempt to acquire or receive health services or supplies. For example, we may collect GPS location data when you request us to locate the nearest pharmacy to you.
  • Information that could identify your attempt to seek health care services or information, including services that allow you to assess, measure, improve, or learn about your or another person’s health. For example, your interactions with us could involve queries or reveal information about you concerning prescriptions, or other health-related topics. 
  • Information about your prescriptions, such as your provider and pharmacy information, to facilitate fulfillment of your prescription, or sign up for a pharmaceutical manufacturer’s copay card program.

 

  • Other information that may be used to infer or derive data related to the above or other health information.  

 

Sources of Consumer Health Data

CHD is sourced directly from you, from your interactions with our products and services (including inferences we make from your use of GoodRx Services and from your devices), and from third parties and service providers.

 

Why We Collect and Use Consumer Health Data

We collect and use CHD as reasonably necessary to provide you with the products you have requested or authorized. This may include delivering and operating the products and their features, personalization or contextualization of certain product features, communicating with you, ensuring the secure and reliable operation of the products and the systems that support them, troubleshooting and improving the products, advertising and marketing of our Services and other products or services we believe you may find interesting, as well as advertising and marketing served by third parties on our websites and mobile applications or on third-party platforms, and other essential business operations that support the provision of the products (such as analyzing our performance, meeting our legal obligations, developing our workforce, and conducting research and development). 

We may use CHD for other purposes for which we give you choices and/or obtain your consent as required by law – for example, for advertising or marketing purposes. Please review your choices in the applicable privacy policy and the “How to Exercise Your Rights” section below for more details on the controls and choices you may have.   

 

Our Sharing of Consumer Health Data

We may share each of the categories of CHD described above for the purposes described in the relevant privacy policy. In particular, we may share CHD with your consent or as reasonably necessary to complete any transaction or provide any product you have requested or authorized, as described in the previous section above.  

For example, if you make a purchase, we will share information about the transaction as necessary to process the payment, including to protect against fraud. And we may disclose data when we believe that doing so is necessary to comply with applicable law or respond to valid legal process.

 

Third Parties with Which We Share Consumer Health Data

As necessary for the purposes described above, we share CHD with the following categories of third parties: 

  • Affiliates and subsidiaries. We may share information with our subsidiaries and affiliated companies for business and operational purposes. GoodRx subsidiaries and affiliates include:
    • GoodRx Care
    • RxNxt
    • RxSaver
    • Scriptcycle

 

  • Service providers and third parties. Vendors or agents (“processors”) working on our behalf may access CHD for the purposes described above. For example, companies we’ve hired to provide customer service support or assist in protecting and securing our systems and services may need access to data to provide those functions.  
  • For corporate transactions. We may disclose CHD as part of a corporate transaction or proceeding such as a merger, financing, acquisition, bankruptcy, dissolution, or a transfer, divestiture, or sale of all or a portion of our business or assets.
  • For legal obligations/safety. We may be required to share CHD for things like compliance with a valid legal process, such as a subpoena, court order, or search warrant, or where there is a lawful request. We may also disclose your information to protect the security of our Services, servers, network systems, and databases.
  • To enforce legal rights. We may also share CHD to protect our customers, employees, or property; in emergency situations; to investigate, prevent, or take other action regarding suspected illegal activity, fraud, or other wrongdoing; and to enforce our rights under our terms of service and policies, in court or elsewhere.

 

See the sharing section of the relevant privacy policy for detailed explanations of the categories above.

 

How to Exercise Your Rights and Set Your Data Preferences

Washington law provides certain rights to Consumers, including rights to access, delete, or withdraw consent relating to such data, subject to certain exceptions. You can request to exercise such rights as described in the relevant privacy policy.  If you would like to exercise any of your rights, including a request to review and request changes to your CHD, please submit:

  • GoodRx: The “Data Access Request” form (for your right to access), fill out and submit the “Data Deletion Request” form (for your right to delete), or withdraw consent from your account settings or as otherwise instructed when providing consent.
  • Scriptcycle: The “Data Access Request” form (for your right to access), fill out and submit the “Data Deletion Request” form (for your right to delete), or withdraw consent from your account settings or as otherwise instructed when providing consent.
  • RxSaver: The “Data Access Request” form (for your right to access), fill out and submit the “Data Deletion Request” form (for your right to delete), or withdraw consent from your account settings or as otherwise instructed when providing consent.
  • RxNxt: Email Data Access Requests to  datarequest@rxnxt.com (for your right to access), email Data Deletion Requests to with the subject link ATTN: Customer Service Department—Data Deletion (for your right to delete), or withdraw consent from your account settings or as otherwise instructed when providing consent.

 

For questions, please contact us at privacy@goodrx.com. If your request to exercise a right under applicable law is denied, you may appeal that decision by contacting us at datarequest@goodrx.com. If your appeal is unsuccessful, you can raise a concern or lodge a complaint with your state Attorney General.

 

Updates to This Consumer Health Data Privacy Policy

We may update this Consumer Health Data Privacy Policy from time to time. We may modify this Privacy Policy at any time. When we do so, we will post the revised privacy policy on our website. Please check the revision date at the top of this page to determine if the policy has been modified since you last reviewed it.

Promotion Questions: Miscellaneous

Nift FAQs

Why did I receive a Nift gift card?

If you’ve received a Nift gift card, it’s a surprise thank-you gift from us for considering and using GoodRx. You’ve earned it by being a great customer. Enjoy the gift!

 

Did or will GoodRx sell my email address to Nift?

No, GoodRx does not and will not share or sell your email address or any other personal information with Nift. Any thank-you emails containing Nift gift cards are sent by Klarna. If you choose to redeem the Nift gift, you are choosing to provide your information to Nift.

 

 

How do I use my Nift gift card?

You may receive a Nift gift card via email, in app, or via push notification. To use it, tap to proceed directly to GoNift.com or visit GoNift.com and enter your gift code. Follow the prompts to tell Nift what you’re interested in and you’ll be matched with available gift options. When you select a gift, you’ll receive a Nift certificate via email that is valid for 30 days at a participating merchant.

Once you’ve picked a gift and received your Nift certificate, you’re ready to redeem it in-store or online. To redeem in person, present the Nift certificate on your phone at checkout, then tap Redeem. To redeem at a retailer’s site, add your purchases to the cart, and if needed, enter your unique promo code, featured prominently on your voucher.

Please note that Nift gift certificates are valid for 30 days, only at the selected Nift member business. Please see individual gifts for rules and restrictions. Nift gift cards and gift certificates are not redeemable for cash. Gift options may vary based on availability. By selecting a gift, you agree to Nift’s terms and conditions and may opt out at any time.

 

 

I need help with my Nift gift, what do I do?

If you have any trouble using a Nift gift card, finding a gift that suits you, or using a gift you’ve already selected, reach out to Nift’s customer success team at 

GoNift.com/happy

 

 

Where can I use my Nift gift?

Food delivery, clothing, restaurants, music, jewelry, sneakers, home goods, and more. When you use a $30 Nift gift card, you’re matched with exclusive gift options designed to help you discover and try something new—based on your interests and gift availability.

 

 

I don’t like my gift options — can I get different ones?

Of course! Nift matches you with available gift options tailored to your interests. However, if you don’t like the options you see, you may reset your card once and try again. You may also reach out to Nift’s customer success team at 

GoNift.com/happy for assistance.

 

 

Do I need to pay Nift to select a gift that’s worth more than $30?

No, you never need to pay Nift to select a gift. The brands featured on Nift fully fund all gift vouchers offered to you (some are worth more than $30). Later, depending on how you decide to apply your gift at the brand’s site, you may need to cover additional costs such as taxes and shipping.

 

How long do I have to use my Nift gift?

You’ll have 30 days to use your Nift gift card to select a gift. After selecting your gift, you’ll receive a corresponding gift certificate which must be used within 30 days. If you have any trouble using a Nift gift card, or using a gift you’ve already selected, please reach out to Nift’s customer success team at 

GoNift.com/happy.

 

Why do I only see limited gift options? Can I see all of the gifts?

The gifts available through the Nift Network are exclusive and limited in quantity. When selecting your Nift gift, you’ll be able to tell Nift what kinds of products, services, and places you’d like to try. Nift will match you with the best available gifts that suit your preferences. If you don’t like the options you see, please reach out to Nift’s customer success team at 

GoNift.com/happy and they will be be more than happy to assist.

 

Is this a $30 gift to GoodRx?

No, this is not a $30 gift to GoodRx. Nift will match you with gifts that can be used at businesses nationwide that are a part of the Nift Network.

 

Can I transfer my Nift gift to someone else?

No, you cannot transfer your Nift gift to someone else.

 

What is Nift doing with my information?

GoodRx is partnering with Nift to provide its customers with thank you gifts. In order to activate your Nift gift card you will have to sign in and enter your information. Later, you can share your gift preferences with Nift as well as other information that will allow Nift to match you with the best gifts. Nift takes privacy very seriously and does not share your information with anyone. You can ask Nift to delete your personal information from the system at any time. You can read more in their Privacy Policy here.

 

For more Nift-related FAQs, visit Nift’s FAQ page.

  2. Click the link and choose your gift card!

Redemption & Gift Card

How are gift cards delivered to me?

Your gift card will be emailed to you by Tango, our gift card partner. After you have redeemed your points, we will send you an email with a personalized link to visit the Tango gift card store, where you can select from over 200 popular options. After you’ve made your selection, Tango will send you an email containing your digital gift card.

How do I redeem points for a gift card?

You can always access your point balance in the Rewards tab of the GoodRx app. 

 

When you are ready and eligible to redeem, simply: 

  • Open the GoodRx app and select the “Rewards” tab in the bottom menu bar.
  • Click to “Redeem” your points.
  • We’ll send you an email with a personalized link to select your digital gift card.
  • Click on the link in that email to visit Tango, our gift card partner, to select a gift card to your favorite retailer.
  • You’ll receive your digital gift card from Tango via email.

How many points do I need to earn before I can redeem a gift card?

You will need a minimum of 3,000 points available in your account balance to redeem your points in exchange for a digital gift card.

I have not received information on how to select my gift card. Who can I contact?

If you have already redeemed your points in the app and don’t see an email with instructions on how to select your digital gift card, check your spam folder to ensure it has not been filtered as “junk mail”. 

If the email is not in your inbox or spam folder, contact our Patient Advocacy team at 1-855-268-2822. We’re available every day, from 7AM–8PM CT.

I have requested my digital gift card, but have not received it. Who can I contact?

Our digital gift cards are delivered to you by Tango, our gift card partner. For questions about your digital gift card, visit this support page for self-service assistance. If you need to speak with someone, you can also reach Tango’s customer support team by emailing or calling 1-877-558-2646.

My digital gift card isn’t working. What should I do?

Our digital gift cards are delivered to you by Tango, our gift card partner. For questions about your digital gift card, visit this support page for self-service assistance. If you need to speak with someone, you can also reach Tango’s customer support team by emailing or calling 1-877-558-2646.

Technical Issues

I keep getting “Service Access Denied” when I try to use GoodRx Text. What do I do?

You may be receiving a message like “Service Access Denied” when you try to use GoodRx Text or request coupons via text. This is probably because your cell phone provider (e.g. Verizon, T-mobile, ATT, etc) has blocked our 3rd party messaging provider. 

 

Usually when you text HELP/START/etc to our Text number you would be able to opt back in to our text messages (even if you previously opted-out). But if you are blocked from our service because of your cell phone provider, that troubleshooting won’t work! 

 

You will need to call your cell phone provider (e.g. Verizon, T-mobile, ATT, etc) and ask them to unblock 3rd party messaging providers.  

 

Not sure if your service provider is blocking our access?

You can try investigating by sending the standard opt-in text message (HELP / START) to the GoodRx short code line (466379). 

 

 

If they all respond with the same “Service Access Denied” message (or something similar), then your mobile service provider is automatically blocking 3rd party messaging providers and you will need to contact your service provider to remove the block.

Logging in to your GoodRx account

To log in to your existing free GoodRx account,  you will need to enter the email address (or, in some cases, phone number) connected  to your GoodRx account, then enter the verification code sent from (or via text to your phone number) when prompted. 

Note: GoodRx Gold Members and Companion members must use an email address to log in to their account 

  • Logging into your account on the GoodRx website
  • Logging into your account on the GoodRx mobile app
  • Verification code issues 

 

 

Logging into your account on the GoodRx website

  1. Go to GoodRx.com

  2. Click the Sign up / Sign In button 

  3. If you already have an account click Sign In

  • If you don’t have an account already, click Sign up for free)

  1. Enter your email address in the email address field

  2. Click Next

  3. Check your email inbox for a 6-digit verification code from GoodRx

  4. Enter the 6-digit code in the field provided

  5. Click Verify to log into your GoodRx account

  • If you encounter issues with receiving or entering the verification code, click here

 

Logging into your account on the GoodRx Mobile App

  1. Open or download the GoodRx mobile appon your mobile device

  2. Click the Sign In button

  3. Enter your email address in the email address field

  4. Click Next

  5. Check your email inbox for a 6-digit verification code from GoodRx

  6. Enter the 6-digit code in the field provided

  7. Click Verify to log into your GoodRx account

  • If you encounter issues with receiving or entering the verification code, click here

 

 

Verification Code Issues

  • Didn’t receive verification code basic troubleshooting
  • Verification code is incorrect
Didn’t receive verification code basic troubleshooting

If you didn’t receive your verification code via email, try the following:

 

  1. Confirm that you’re checking the same email address that’s connected to your GoodRx account

  2. Check your spam and junk mail folders

  3. Try checking your email on a different device

  • e.g. If you’re checking your email on your phone, try checking on your computer (or vice versa)

  1. Click Send new code

  2. If you still can’t find the email, follow the steps below

  • Didn’t receive verification code (web)
  • Didn’t receive verification code (app)

 

 

 

Didn’t receive verification code (web)

 

  1. Refresh the page

  2. Close and re-open your web browser

  3. Clear your browser’s cache/cookies/history ( see how-to links below)

  • Clear cache & cookies in Google Chrome
  • Clear cache in FIrefox
  • Clear cookies in Safari
  • Clear cache in yahoo

  1. Log out and log back in to your email inbox

  2. Try logging intoyour GoodRx accountagain

 

 

 

 

 

 

 

Didn’t receive verification code (app)

 

  1. Fully close and quit the app, then reopen

  2. Ensure your app is up-to-date

  • Check the Apple Store or Google Play Store to see if an updated version of the GoodRx app is available

  1. Uninstall and reinstall the app on your mobile device

  2. Try logging in again 

 

 

 

 

 

 

 

Verification code is incorrect

 

  1. Ensure that you entered the same 6-digit verification code that was sent to you

  2. Re-enter the verification code exactly as is and click Verify once more

  3. Click Send new code and repeat steps 1-3

  • Check the time stamps on the email to make sure you’re entering the most recently sent verification code

Agreement for Self-Payment of Services

Last Updated on October 8, 2024

HeyDoctor Medical Group, P.C., HeyDoctor Medical Group (FL), P.A., HeyDoctor Medical Group (NJ), P.C., and HeyDoctor Medical Group (KS), P.A. (collectively, the “Medical Group”), and its employed or contracted providers (together, with the “Medical Group”, the “Providers” or “we” or “our”) do not currently participate in any insurance plans, including Medicare or Medicaid, and the Providers do not accept any health insurance whatsoever. Services provided by the Providers are 100% self-pay by the Providers’ patients. By agreeing to this form, you acknowledge that: 1) you do not have any health insurance (whether through a PPO, HMO, Medicaid or Medicare or any other insurance plan); or 2) you have health insurance but have elected to self-pay as you do not want to use any insurance benefit for these services, acknowledging that the Providers do not accept any health insurance.

Your insurance policy is a contract between you and your insurance company. It is your responsibility to know your benefits, and how they will apply to your benefit payments, and we take no responsibility to understand or be bound by the terms and conditions of such insurance. By agreeing to this Agreement for Self-Payment of Services, you are electing to purchase services that may or may not be covered by your insurance if you obtained those services from a different provider. You have selected services for purchase from us on a self-pay basis. In other words, you have directed us to treat your purchase of these services as if you are an uninsured patient and you agree to be 100% responsible for full payment of the listed price of the services. There is no guarantee your insurance company will make any payment on the cost of the services you have purchased.

The Providers have provided you with the charges, in advance, for the services you have requested. You agree to pay these charges in full as a self-pay patient, electing not to use an insurance policy benefit. You have selected the services, been informed of their costs, and are willing to accept full financial responsibility for payment.

Consent to Telehealth Services

This Telehealth Consent was last updated on November 17, 2025

Telehealth involves the use of secure electronic communications, information technology, or other means to enable a healthcare provider and a patient at different locations to communicate and share individual patient health information for the purpose of rendering clinical care.  This “Telehealth Consent” informs the patient (“patient,” “you,” or “your”) concerning the treatment methods, risks, and limitations of using a telehealth platform. 

Services Provided:

Telehealth services offered by HeyDoctor Medical Group, P.C., HeyDoctor Medical Group (FL), P.A., HeyDoctor Medical Group (NJ), P.C., and HeyDoctor Medical Group (KS), P.A. (collectively, the “Medical Group”), and its employed or contracted providers including Wheel Medical, P.A. and its associated medical groups (collectively, “Engaged Providers”, and, together with the “Medical Group”, the “Providers” or your “Provider”) may include patient consultation, diagnosis, treatment recommendation, prescription, and/or a referral to in-person care, as determined to be clinically appropriate (the “Services”). 

GoodRx Care, LLC does not provide the Services; it performs administrative, payment, and other non-clinical supportive activities for the Providers.

Electronic Transmissions:

The types of electronic transmissions that may occur using the telehealth platform include, but are not limited to:

  • Appointment scheduling;

  • Completion, exchange, and review of medical intake forms and other clinically relevant information (for example: health records; images; output data from medical devices; sound and video files; diagnostic and/or lab test results) between you and your Provider via:

    • asynchronous communications;

    • two-way interactive audio in combination with store-and-forward communications; and/or

    • two-way interactive audio and video interaction;

  • Treatment recommendations by your Provider based upon such review and exchange of clinical information;

  • Delivery of a consultation report with a diagnosis, treatment and/or prescription recommendations, as deemed clinically relevant;

  • Prescription refill reminders (if applicable); and/or

  • Other electronic transmissions for the purpose of rendering clinical care to you.

Expected Benefits:

Telehealth benefits include, but are not limited to, the following:

  • Improved access to care by enabling you to remain in your preferred location while your Provider consults with you. Telehealth services rendered by Providers are available seven days a week.

  • Convenient access to follow-up care. If you need to receive non-emergent follow-up care related to your treatment, please contact your Provider by logging into your account and sending a message to your Provider through the message portal (“Message Portal”). 

  • More efficient care evaluation and management. 

Service Limitations:

  • The primary difference between telehealth and in-person service delivery is the inability to have direct, physical contact with the patient. Accordingly, some clinical needs may not be appropriate for a telehealth visit and your Provider will make that determination.

  • Providers do not address medical emergencies. If you believe you are experiencing a medical emergency, you should dial 9-1-1 and/or go to the nearest emergency room. Please do not attempt to contact GoodRx Care, LLC, or your Provider.  After receiving emergency healthcare treatment, you should visit your local primary care provider.

  • Providers are an addition to, and not a replacement for, your local primary care provider. Responsibility for your overall medical care should remain with your local primary care provider, if you have one, and we strongly encourage you to locate one if you do not.

  • Providers do not have any in-person clinic locations.

Security Measures:

The electronic communication systems we use will incorporate network and software security protocols to protect the confidentiality of patient identification and imaging data and will include measures to safeguard the data and to ensure its integrity against intentional or unintentional corruption.  All the Services delivered to you through telehealth will be delivered over a secure connection that complies with the requirements of the Health Insurance Portability and Accountability Act of 1996, as applicable.

Possible Risks: 

  • Delays in evaluation and treatment could occur due to deficiencies or failures of the equipment and technologies, or provider availability.

  • In the event of an inability to communicate as a result of a technological or equipment failure, please contact your Provider via other methods, including by calling (855) 268-2822.

  • In rare events, your Provider may determine that the transmitted information is of inadequate quality, thus necessitating a rescheduled telehealth consult or an in-person meeting with your local primary care doctor.

  • In very rare events, security protocols could fail, causing a breach of privacy of personal medical information.

Risks and Benefits of Weight Loss Treatment:

Informed consent is required before any weight loss medications or treatments are prescribed, administered, or dispensed. Where weight loss treatment is appropriate, the benefits of medical weight loss can include, among others: 

  • Lower blood pressure, reducing the risks of hypertension

  • Lower cholesterol, reducing the risks of heart and vascular disease

  • Lower blood sugar, reducing the risks of diabetes 

  • Increased energy levels

  • Better mobility

  • Improved self-esteem and mental health

While many patients benefit from medical weight loss treatment, possible side effects of weight loss medication may include:

  • Nausea, vomiting, diarrhea, or constipation

  • Headaches, fatigue, or injection site reactions

  • Low blood sugar

  • Gallbladder issues or pancreatitis

  • Increased or decreased heart rate or blood pressure

  • Rare risks, including allergic reactions, hormonal effects, or mood changes

Additional risks of medical weight loss treatment can include: 

  • Muscle loss

  • Nutritional deficiencies

  • Fatigue or dizziness from caloric restriction

You are responsible for promptly reporting any symptoms to your Provider. 

If you become pregnant, you should immediately stop all of your weight loss medications and report this to your Provider and your primary care provider, if applicable. Your diet must be changed promptly to avoid further weight loss because a restricted diet could be damaging for a developing fetus. You must take precautions to avoid becoming pregnant during the course of weight loss. 

While many patients experience weight loss, no guarantee is made regarding the amount or duration of weight loss. Results vary depending on individual adherence, metabolism, health status, and other factors. The success of the program depends significantly on your active participation, including commitment to dietary changes, physical activity, and follow-up care. Certain medications may not be available in every state. Medication access is dependent on the outcome of your consultation with your Provider and applicable regulatory restrictions. 

Long-term success in weight management requires commitment to lifestyle changes, including regular physical activity and adherence to a health diet. Obesity is a chronic condition, and there is a possibility of regaining weight over time. 

You can review the full list of side effects and warnings for Wegovy here and for Zepbound here. 

Patient Acknowledgments:

By proceeding with your visit, you acknowledge that you understand and agree to the following:

  1. As part of your telehealth visit, you have been matched with a provider based on your location, and that you have been (or will be) given your Provider’s credentials.

  2. Your Provider will take responsibility for your care only after: (i) you have created an account, answered all the required health questions and provided a photo and/or have had the requisite interaction with your Provider as required by applicable state laws, and made payment; and (ii) Provider has subsequently received your request for treatment and your responses to the aforementioned requested information, reviewed such, and then subsequently determined that you are a good candidate for the telehealth services. You understand that your Provider’s duty of care does not begin at the point of you answering questions or making payment or starting a visit but at the point at which the Provider accepts the duty of care.

  3. You understand that making a request for treatment (e.g., by completing a visit on the website and making payment, including providing photos and/or initiating communications with your Provider) or sending a message through the website does not in and of itself create a duty of care or create a practitioner-patient relationship. Your Provider reserves the right to deny care if, in the professional judgment of your Provider, the provision of the telehealth services is not medically or ethically appropriate.

  4. If you are experiencing a medical emergency, you will be directed to dial 9-1-1 immediately and your Provider is not able to connect you directly to any local emergency services.

  5. You may elect to seek services from a medical group with in-person clinics as an alternative to receiving telehealth services.

  6. You have the right to withhold or withdraw your consent to the use of telehealth in the course of your care at any time without affecting your right to future care or treatment.

  7. Federal and state law requires health care providers to protect the privacy and the security of health information. You are entitled to all confidentiality protections under applicable federal and state laws.  You understand all medical reports resulting from the telehealth visit are part of your medical record. 

  8. Your Provider will take steps to make sure that your health information is only used and disclosed as appropriate. Telehealth may involve electronic communication of your personal health information to other health practitioners who may be located in other areas, including out of state. You consent to your Provider using and disclosing your health information for purposes of your treatment and care coordination, to receive reimbursement for the services provided to you, and for your Provider’s health care operations.

  9. Dissemination of any patient identifiable images or information from your telehealth visit to researchers or other educational entities will not occur without your affirmative consent.

  10. There is a risk of technical failures during the telehealth visit beyond the control of your Provider. YOU AGREE TO HOLD HARMLESS MEDICAL GROUP AND ITS EMPLOYEES, CONTRACTORS, AGENTS, DIRECTORS, MEMBERS, MANAGERS, SHAREHOLDERS, OFFICERS, REPRESENTATIVES, ASSIGNS, PARENTS, PREDECESSORS, AND SUCCESSORS for any and all delays in evaluation or for information lost due to such technical failures.

  11. In choosing to participate in a telehealth visit, you understand that some parts of the Services involving tests (e.g., labs or bloodwork) may be conducted at another location such as a testing facility, at the direction of your Provider.

  12. Persons may be present during the telehealth visit other than your Provider in order to operate the telehealth technologies. If another person is present during the telehealth visit, you will be informed of the individual’s presence and his/her role.

  13. Your Provider will explain your diagnosis and its evidentiary basis, and the risks and benefits of various treatment options.

  14. You have the right to request a copy of your medical records. You can request to obtain or send a copy of your medical records to your primary care or other designated health care provider by contacting your Provider through the chat function, by email at care-legal@goodrx.com, or as otherwise directed by your Provider.

  15. Your Provider may charge a reasonable fee for such copy of your medical records (which fee shall include the costs of preparation, shipping and delivery of the medical record). 

  16. You must provide your Provider with complete, accurate, and current information regarding your medical history. You understand that you can log into your Message Portal at any time and send a message to access, amend, or review your health information.

  17. There is no guarantee that you will be issued a prescription and that the decision of whether a prescription is appropriate will be made in the professional judgment of your Provider. If your Provider issues a prescription, you have the right to select the pharmacy of your choice.

  18. There is no guarantee that you will be treated by a Provider. Your Provider reserves the right to deny care for potential misuse of the Services or for any other reason if, in the professional judgment of your Provider, the provision of the Services is not medically or ethically appropriate.

  19. If you are taking medications for high blood pressure, high cholesterol, diabetes or pre-diabetes, or any other condition, you agree to see your primary care provider as needed to have your need for these medications reassessed from time to time, as applicable.

Gift Items Rewards Program Terms

Promotional Item Rewards Program

GoodRx is excited to offer new GoodRx users a promotional item as selected by GoodRx for those that receive this special offer for a discount card.

How does it work?

You will receive a one-time promotional item (“Rewards Item”) when you use a GoodRx Prescription Savings Card (“Discount Card”) that you received in the mail, or by using a free GoodRx coupon from our GoodRx app.  You must be a new GoodRx user and purchase a prescription of at least $10 with the discount card or coupon at a participating pharmacy on or before 45 days after the date on which you initially sign up to participate in this Promotional Item Rewards Program. 

The Rewards Item will be mailed to the address provided by you after you have made the qualifying purchase. GoodRx reserves the right to substitute promotional items based on GoodRx’s sole discretion, including based on availability. Any third-party product names, logos, brands, and trademarks shown in relation to the Promotional Item Rewards Program are the property of their respective owners. These companies are not affiliated with GoodRx and do not sponsor or endorse any GoodRx services, websites, mobile applications or content, including this Promotional Item Rewards Program. 

I received this special offer in the mail. How do I get the promotional item?

  1. Use the discount card or a GoodRx coupon for at least a $10 prescription purchase within a set time period described in your special offer (likely a month).
  2. We will mail you a GoodRx Savings Prescription Card with instructions and Terms & Conditions
  3. Once we verify the qualifying purchase of a prescription, we will mail you a Rewards Item (see specific promo for how you will receive your Rewards Item).

GoodRx Rewards Program Terms and Conditions

These GoodRx Rewards Program Terms and Conditions (these “Terms and Conditions”) apply to the GoodRx Promotional Item Rewards Program (this “Program”).

  • This Program is open to new GoodRx users only.
  • When you use your GoodRx Prescription Drug Savings Card (“Discount Card”) or a free GoodRx coupon to purchase a prescription of at least $10 dollars at a participating pharmacy on or before the date listed in the promotional materials you received, you will receive a promotional item or, as applicable, a $5 gift card from select retailers (“Rewards Item”). Please visit goodrx.com for a current list of participating pharmacies.
  • Participation is subject to the GoodRx Terms of Use and Privacy Policy.
  • Limit one Rewards Item per Discount Card or GoodRx coupon. You will not receive additional Rewards Items for multiple uses of the same Discount Card or GoodRx coupon.
  • The Rewards Item will be mailed to the address associated with your GoodRx account, or emailed to the email address associated with your GoodRx account.
  • The Rewards Item may not be traded, sold or otherwise transferred to others.
  • Rewards Items are offered at the sole discretion of GoodRx and are subject to change at any time without notice. As part of this Promo, you consent to the use of a third party vendor of GoodRx’s choosing to deliver Rewards Items.
  • Fulfillment times may vary.  For certain physical Rewards Items, please allow at least eight (8) weeks for fulfillment.
  • If we believe that a participant has violated any of the terms or conditions of GoodRx, a Discount Card or  this Program, including, but not limited to where we believe a Discount Card is being used without proper authorization, fraudulently, inappropriately or unlawfully, then we may terminate that participant’s Discount Card without notice.
  • We reserve the right to cancel, terminate, suspend, or modify any services, a Discount Card or this Program at any time in our sole discretion and without notice.
  • Any third-party product names, logos, brands, and trademarks shown in relation to the Promotional Item Rewards Program are the property of their respective owners. These companies are not affiliated with GoodRx and do not sponsor or endorse any GoodRx services, websites, mobile applications or content, including this Promotional Item Rewards Program.
  • Use of the Discount Card or a GoodRx coupon constitutes acceptance of these Terms and Conditions and all applicable terms and conditions on our website or provided to you at any time. Please keep a copy of these Terms and Conditions for your records.
  • Through the GoodRx Promotional Item Rewards Program, GoodRx offers a promotional item to encourage consumers to register for an account and use a Discount Card. The categories of personal information that GoodRx collects to administer the GoodRx Promotional Item Rewards Program include: (1) identifiers (such as name or e-mail address); (2) commercial information (such as records of coupons used); and (3) Internet or other electronic network activity information (such as browsing history and information regarding consumer interactions with an internet website or mobile application).  Consumers may opt-in by opting in to a GoodRx Promotional Item Rewards Program offer, selecting a coupon, redeeming it at a participating pharmacy by the date listed on the promotional material. Consumers have the right to withdraw from the GoodRx Promotional Item Rewards Program at any time by not utilizing their Discount Card and/or GoodRx accounts further. If you request that we delete any of your personal information that is essential to providing you with the program, we will not be able to provide you with the benefits of that program (e.g., if you ask us to delete your GoodRx account, we will no longer be able to maintain your loyalty account). While we do not assign a monetary value to the personal information we collect from program participants, the financial incentive GoodRx offers to consumers through the GoodRx Promotional Item Rewards Program is reasonably related to the value of the consumers’ data and increased engagement. This value will vary by consumer depending on purchases made, where those purchases are made, which offers a consumer takes advantage of, and many other factors. GoodRx requires your consent to process sensitive data to participate in the program in accordance with applicable law to be able to administer the program to you (e.g., calculating your rewards and benefits). To form the basis for offering the financial incentive, GoodRx values the personal information it obtains through the program based on a reasonable and good faith calculation determined by considering the revenue generated from activities related to the collection and retention of program participants’ personal information less expenses related to the offer, provision, or imposition of any financial incentive or price or service difference as permitted under applicable law. If you delete your GoodRx personal information, GoodRx can no longer provide you with the Rewards Item.

Mandatory Informal Dispute Resolution

If a dispute arises, GoodRx is committed to working with you to reach a reasonable resolution.
