Arbitration Agreement

By agreeing to the Terms, you agree that you are required to resolve any claim that you may have against GoodRx on an individual basis in arbitration, as set forth in this Arbitration Agreement. This will preclude you from bringing any class, collective, or representative action against GoodRx, and also preclude you from participating in or recovering relief under any current or future class, collective, consolidated, or representative action brought against GoodRx by someone else.

A. Agreement to Binding Arbitration Between You and GoodRx.

You and GoodRx agree that any dispute, claim or controversy arising out of or relating to (a) these Terms or the existence, breach, termination, enforcement, interpretation or validity thereof, or (b) your access to or use of the HCP Services at any time, whether before or after the date you agreed to the Terms, will be settled by binding arbitration between you and GoodRx, and not in a court of law. For the avoidance of doubt, the scope of arbitration includes federal and state statutory and common law claims, including under the consumer protection laws and principles.

You acknowledge and agree that you and GoodRx are each waiving the right to a trial by jury or to participate as a plaintiff or class member in any purported class action or representative proceeding. Unless both you and GoodRx otherwise agree in writing, any arbitration will be conducted only on an individual basis and not in a class, collective, consolidated, or representative proceeding. However, you and GoodRx each retain the right to bring an individual action in small claims court and the right to seek injunctive or other equitable relief in a court of competent jurisdiction to prevent the actual or threatened infringement, misappropriation or violation of a party’s copyrights, trademarks, trade secrets, patents or other intellectual property rights.

B. Rules and Governing Law.

To facilitate informal dispute resolution, neither you nor GoodRx may start an arbitration or small claims proceeding against the other for at least thirty (30) calendar days after a party notifies the other of a dispute. The notification shall include the nature of the dispute, the relief sought, and the party’s contact information (phone, address, email). Notification of a dispute pursuant to this paragraph to GoodRx shall be emailed to legal@goodrx.com with the subject line “User Dispute.”

In the event the parties are unable to resolve the dispute via informal dispute resolution within thirty (30) days of notification of a dispute, the parties may proceed with arbitration. The arbitration will be administered by the American Arbitration Association (“AAA”) in accordance with the AAA’s Consumer Arbitration Rules and the Supplementary Procedures for Consumer Related Disputes (the “AAA Rules”) then in effect, except as modified by this Arbitration Agreement. The AAA Rules are available at www.adr.org.

The parties agree that the arbitrator (“Arbitrator”), and not any federal, state, or local court or agency, shall have exclusive authority to resolve any disputes relating to the interpretation, applicability, enforceability or formation of this Arbitration Agreement, including any claim that all or any part of this Arbitration Agreement is void or voidable. The Arbitrator shall also be responsible for determining all threshold arbitrability issues, including issues relating to whether the Terms are unconscionable or illusory and any defense to arbitration, including waiver, delay, laches, or estoppel.

Notwithstanding any choice of law or other provision in the Terms, the parties agree and acknowledge that this Arbitration Agreement evidences a transaction involving interstate commerce and that the Federal Arbitration Act, 9 U.S.C. § 1 et seq. (“FAA”), will govern its interpretation and enforcement and proceedings pursuant thereto. It is the intent of the parties that the FAA and AAA Rules shall preempt all state laws to the fullest extent permitted by law. If the FAA and AAA Rules are found to not apply to any issue that arises under this Arbitration Agreement or the enforcement thereof, then that issue shall be resolved under the laws of the state of California.

C. Process.

A party who desires to initiate arbitration must provide the other party with a written Demand for Arbitration as specified in the AAA Rules. (The AAA provides a form Demand for Arbitration - Consumer Arbitration Rules at www.adr.org). Additionally, the Demand for Arbitration shall set forth and allege sufficient facts in support of a legally-recognized cause of action, along with the amount of recovery sought in connection with each associated cause of action. In the event the Demand for Arbitration fails to adequately plead a cause of action or associated recovery as set forth herein, the Demand for Arbitration and/or cause of action shall be subject to dismissal by the Arbitrator. The Arbitrator will be either (1) a retired judge or (2) an attorney specifically licensed to practice law in the state of California and will be selected by the parties from the AAA’s roster of consumer dispute arbitrators. If the parties are unable to agree upon an Arbitrator within seven (7) days of delivery of the Demand for Arbitration, then the AAA will appoint the Arbitrator in accordance with the AAA Rules.

D. Location and Procedure.

Unless you and GoodRx otherwise agree, the arbitration will be conducted in the county where you reside. If your claim does not exceed $10,000, then the arbitration will be conducted solely on the basis of documents you and GoodRx submit to the Arbitrator, unless you request a hearing or the Arbitrator determines that a hearing is necessary. If your claim exceeds $10,000, your right to a hearing will be determined by the AAA Rules. Subject to the AAA Rules, the Arbitrator will have the discretion to direct a reasonable exchange of information by the parties, consistent with the expedited nature of the arbitration.

E. Arbitrator’s Decision.

The Arbitrator will render an award within the time frame specified in the AAA Rules. Judgment on the arbitration award may be entered in any court having competent jurisdiction to do so. The Arbitrator may award declaratory or injunctive relief only in favor of the claimant and only to the extent necessary to provide relief warranted by the claimant’s individual claim. An Arbitrator’s decision shall be final and binding on all parties. If you prevail in arbitration you will be entitled to an award of attorneys’ fees and expenses, to the extent provided under applicable law. GoodRx will not seek, and hereby waives all rights GoodRx may have under applicable law to recover attorneys’ fees and expenses if GoodRx prevails in arbitration.

F. Fees.

Your responsibility to pay any AAA filing, administrative and arbitrator fees will be solely as set forth in the AAA Rules. However, if your claim for damages does not exceed $75,000, GoodRx will pay all such fees, unless the Arbitrator finds that either the substance of your claim or the relief sought in your Demand for Arbitration was frivolous or was brought for an improper purpose (as measured by the standards set forth in Federal Rule of Civil Procedure 11(b)).

G. Severability and Survival.

If any portion of this Arbitration Agreement is found to be unenforceable or unlawful for any reason, (1) the unenforceable or unlawful provision shall be severed from these Terms; (2) severance of the unenforceable or unlawful provision shall have no impact whatsoever on the remainder of the Arbitration Agreement or the parties’ ability to compel arbitration of any remaining claims on an individual basis pursuant to the Arbitration Agreement; and (3) to the extent that any claims must therefore proceed on a class, collective, consolidated, or representative basis, such claims must be litigated in a civil court of competent jurisdiction and not in arbitration, and the parties agree that litigation of those claims shall be stayed pending the outcome of any individual claims in arbitration.

 

About HCP Mode

HCP Mode offers prescription discounts and coupons and prescription pricing information that are free to access for healthcare providers (the “HCP Services”). As part of the HCP Services, you can also send coupons directly to patients via text or email. By accessing or using the HCP Services, you confirm your agreement to be bound by these Terms.

GoodRx cannot be combined with federal or state-funded programs like Medicare or Medicaid. GoodRx prescription discounts and coupons are NOT health insurance. GoodRx prescription discounts and coupons can be used instead of, but not in conjunction with, health insurance.  GoodRx prescription discounts and coupons are not intended as a substitute for health insurance. The range of the discounts will vary depending on the prescription drug and the participating pharmacy. GoodRx only provides discounts from participating pharmacies. Patients are required to pay for all prescription drugs at the time of purchase. GOODRX PRESCRIPTION DISCOUNTS AND COUPONS DO NOT MEET MINIMUM CREDITABLE COVERAGE REQUIREMENTS UNDER STATE HEALTHCARE COVERAGE REGULATIONS.

Please note that cash payments that patients make for medications using GoodRx prescription discounts and coupons may not count toward the patient’s prescription drug coverage out of pocket costs, but patient can confirm that by contacting the patient’s own coverage provider. If the patient is a Medicare Part D beneficiary, cash payments made using GoodRx prescription discounts and coupons will not count toward the patient’s Medicare Part D cost-sharing obligation. GoodRx cannot be combined with federal or state-funded programs like Medicare or Medicaid.

GoodRx shares personal data (but not sensitive data) with this processor so that the processor knows how many GoodRx Rewards Points you can redeem and can send you an email to redeem GoodRx Rewards Points on the vendor platform. The vendor processes the information GoodRx provides to it on behalf of GoodRx. However, when you interact directly with the vendor and its digital gift card providers, you are subject to the terms and privacy policy of the vendor and digital gift card providers.

This Program offers GoodRx Rewards Points based on certain actions you take, including using certain GoodRx coupons and in relation to managing prescription-related reminders and actions. If you delete your personal data, we will be unable to provide the Program because we will not have any of the information required to provide these Program benefits.

Notice of Privacy Practices

This Notice was last updated on October 8, 2024

 

THIS NOTICE DESCRIBES HOW MEDICAL INFORMATION ABOUT YOU MAY BE USED AND DISCLOSED AND HOW YOU CAN GET ACCESS TO THIS INFORMATION. PLEASE REVIEW IT CAREFULLY.

This Notice of Privacy Practices (the “Notice”) describes how HeyDoctor Medical Group, P.C., HeyDoctor Medical Group (FL), P.A., HeyDoctor Medical Group (NJ), P.C., and HeyDoctor Medical Group (KS), P.A. (collectively, the “Medical Group”), and its employed or contracted providers (together, with the “Medical Group”, the “Providers” or “we” or “our”), may use and disclose your Protected Health Information. This Notice also describes your rights to access and control your Protected Health Information. The Providers will use and disclose your Protected Health Information as permitted by the Health Insurance Portability and Accountability Act (“HIPAA”) and this Notice. “Protected Health Information” or “PHI” is information about you, including demographic information, that may identify you and that relates to your past, present or future physical health or condition, treatment or payment for health care services.

 

USES AND DISCLOSURES OF PHI

We typically use or share your health information for the following purposes:

  • Treatment: We may use and disclose your Protected Health Information to provide, coordinate, or manage your health care and any related services. This includes the coordination or management of your health care with a third party. For example, your Protected Health Information may be provided to a health care provider to whom you have been referred to ensure the necessary information is accessible to diagnose or treat you.
  • Payment: Your Protected Health Information may be used to bill or obtain payment for your health care services, including transmission of certain Protected Health Information to third party payment processors for the purpose of processing your payments for health care services. This may include certain activities that your health insurance plan may undertake before it approves or pays for your services, such as: making a determination of eligibility or coverage for insurance benefits and reviewing services provided to you for medical necessity.
  • Health Care Operations: We may use and disclose your Protected Health Information in order to support our business activities. These activities may include, but are not limited to, conducting quality assessment and improvement activities, business planning and development, and conducting audits and compliance programs, including fraud, waste and abuse investigations.

We are also allowed or required to share your information, without your authorization, in other circumstances, as permitted under HIPAA.  Certain conditions must be satisfied before your information may be used for these purposes.  Generally, this includes use of your information for:

  • Help with Public Health and Safety Issues: We can disclose your Protected Health Information for certain situations such as preventing disease, helping with product recalls, reporting adverse reactions to medications, reporting suspected abuse, neglect, or domestic violence, and preventing or reducing a serious threat to anyone’s health or safety.
  • Research: We can use or disclose your Protected Health Information for health research.
  • Compliance with Law: We will disclose information about you if state or federal laws require it, including with the Department of Health and Human Services.
  • Responses to Organ and Tissue Donation Requests: We can disclose your Protected Health Information to organ procurement organizations.
  • Responses for Matters Related to Workers’ Compensation, Law Enforcement, and Other Government Requests: We can use or disclose your Protected Health Information for workers’ compensation claims, for law enforcement purposes or with a law enforcement official, for health oversight agencies for activities authorized by law, and for special government functions such as military, national security, and presidential protective services.
  • Work with a Medical Examiner or a Funeral Director: We can share your Protected Health Information with a coroner, medical examiner, or funeral director.
  • Responses to Lawsuits and Legal Actions: We can disclose your Protected Health Information in response to a court or administrative order, or in response to a subpoena. 

Other uses and disclosures (such as use of your Protected Health Information for marketing purposes) will be made only with your consent, authorization or opportunity to object unless permitted or required by law. We will not use or disclose your Protected Health Information as prohibited by HIPAA, including to conduct a criminal, civil, or administrative investigation based upon the act of seeking, obtaining, providing, or facilitating reproductive health care. If you provide us with an authorization for certain uses and disclosures of your information, you may revoke such authorization, at any time, in writing, except to the extent that we have taken an action in reliance on the use or disclosure indicated in the authorization.

 

YOUR RIGHTS WITH RESPECT TO YOUR PROTECTED HEALTH INFORMATION:

You have certain rights to your Protected Health Information, including the following:

  • You have the right to inspect and obtain a copy of your medical record.
  • You have the right to request a restriction on the use or disclosure of your Protected Health Information. Your request must be in writing and state the specific restriction requested and to whom you want the restriction to apply. We are not required to agree to a restriction that you may request, except if the requested restriction is on a disclosure to a health plan for a payment or health care operations purpose regarding a service that has been paid in full out-of-pocket.
  • You have the right to request to receive confidential communications from us by alternative means or at an alternate location, to the extent applicable. We will comply with all reasonable requests submitted in writing which specify how or where you wish to receive these communications.
  • You have the right to request an amendment of your Protected Health Information. If we deny your request for amendment, you have the right to file a statement of disagreement with us. We may prepare a rebuttal to our statement and we will provide you with a copy of any such rebuttal.
  • You have the right to receive an accounting of certain disclosures of your Protected Health Information that we have made, paper or electronic. We will include all disclosures except for those about treatment, payment, and health care operations, and certain other disclosures (such as those made pursuant to an authorization).
  • You have the right to obtain a paper copy of this Notice, upon request, even if you have previously requested its receipt electronically by email.

OUR RESPONSIBILITIES

  • We are required by law to maintain the privacy and security of your Protected Health Information.
  • We will let you know promptly if a breach occurs that may have compromised the privacy or security of your information.
  • We must follow the duties and privacy practices described in this Notice. 

If you have any questions about this Notice, please contact us by emailing us at care-legal@goodrx.com.

 

REVISIONS TO THIS NOTICE

We reserve the right to revise this Notice and to make the revised Notice effective for Protected Health Information we already have about you as well as any information we receive in the future. You are entitled to a copy of the Notice currently in effect. Any significant changes to this Notice will be posted on our website. 

 

COMPLAINTS

Complaints about this Notice or how we handle your Protected Health Information should be directed to our HIPAA Privacy Officer. If you are not satisfied with the manner in which a complaint is handled you may submit a formal complaint to the Department of Health and Human Services, Office for Civil Rights by sending a letter to 200 Independence Avenue, S.W., Washington, D.C. 20201, calling 1-877-696-6775, or visiting www.hhs.gov/ocr/privacy/hipaa/complaints/. We will not retaliate against you for filing a complaint.

A.   Agreement to Binding Arbitration Between You and GoodRx.

You and GoodRx agree that any dispute, claim or controversy arising out of or relating to (a) these Terms or the existence, breach, termination, enforcement, interpretation or validity thereof, or (b) your access to or use of Pharmacy Mode at any time, whether before or after the date you agreed to the Terms, will be settled by binding arbitration between you and GoodRx, and not in a court of law. For the avoidance of doubt, the scope of arbitration includes federal and state statutory and common law claims, including under the consumer protection laws and principles.

You acknowledge and agree that you and GoodRx are each waiving the right to a trial by jury or to participate as a plaintiff or class member in any purported class action or representative proceeding. Unless both you and GoodRx otherwise agree in writing, any arbitration will be conducted only on an individual basis and not in a class, collective, consolidated, or representative proceeding. However, you and GoodRx each retain the right to bring an individual action in small claims court and the right to seek injunctive or other equitable relief in a court of competent jurisdiction to prevent the actual or threatened infringement, misappropriation or violation of a party’s copyrights, trademarks, trade secrets, patents or other intellectual property rights.

B.   Rules and Governing Law.

The arbitration will be administered by the American Arbitration Association (“AAA”) in accordance with the AAA’s Consumer Arbitration Rules and the Supplementary Procedures for Consumer Related Disputes (the “AAA Rules”) then in effect, except as modified by this Arbitration Agreement. The AAA Rules are available at www.adr.org.

The parties agree that the arbitrator (“Arbitrator”), and not any federal, state, or local court or agency, shall have exclusive authority to resolve any disputes relating to the interpretation, applicability, enforceability or formation of this Arbitration Agreement, including any claim that all or any part of this Arbitration Agreement is void or voidable. The Arbitrator shall also be responsible for determining all threshold arbitrability issues, including issues relating to whether the Terms are unconscionable or illusory and any defense to arbitration, including waiver, delay, laches, or estoppel.

Notwithstanding any choice of law or other provision in the Terms, the parties agree and acknowledge that this Arbitration Agreement evidences a transaction involving interstate commerce and that the Federal Arbitration Act, 9 U.S.C. § 1 et seq. (“FAA”), will govern its interpretation and enforcement and proceedings pursuant thereto. It is the intent of the parties that the FAA and AAA Rules shall preempt all state laws to the fullest extent permitted by law. If the FAA and AAA Rules are found to not apply to any issue that arises under this Arbitration Agreement or the enforcement thereof, then that issue shall be resolved under the laws of the state of California.

C.   Process.

A party who desires to initiate arbitration must provide the other party with a written Demand for Arbitration as specified in the AAA Rules. (The AAA provides a form Demand for Arbitration - Consumer Arbitration Rules at www.adr.org). The Arbitrator will be either (1) a retired judge or (2) an attorney specifically licensed to practice law in the state of California and will be selected by the parties from the AAA’s roster of consumer dispute arbitrators. If the parties are unable to agree upon an Arbitrator within seven (7) days of delivery of the Demand for Arbitration, then the AAA will appoint the Arbitrator in accordance with the AAA Rules.

D.   Location and Procedure.

Unless you and GoodRx otherwise agree, the arbitration will be conducted in the county where you reside. If your claim does not exceed $10,000, then the arbitration will be conducted solely on the basis of documents you and GoodRx submit to the Arbitrator, unless you request a hearing or the Arbitrator determines that a hearing is necessary. If your claim exceeds $10,000, your right to a hearing will be determined by the AAA Rules. Subject to the AAA Rules, the Arbitrator will have the discretion to direct a reasonable exchange of information by the parties, consistent with the expedited nature of the arbitration.

E.   Arbitrator’s Decision.

The Arbitrator will render an award within the time frame specified in the AAA Rules. Judgment on the arbitration award may be entered in any court having competent jurisdiction to do so. The Arbitrator may award declaratory or injunctive relief only in favor of the claimant and only to the extent necessary to provide relief warranted by the claimant’s individual claim. An Arbitrator’s decision shall be final and binding on all parties. If you prevail in arbitration you will be entitled to an award of attorneys’ fees and expenses, to the extent provided under applicable law. GoodRx will not seek, and hereby waives all rights GoodRx may have under applicable law to recover attorneys’ fees and expenses if GoodRx prevails in arbitration.

F.   Fees.

Your responsibility to pay any AAA filing, administrative and arbitrator fees will be solely as set forth in the AAA Rules. However, if your claim for damages does not exceed $75,000, GoodRx will pay all such fees, unless the Arbitrator finds that either the substance of your claim or the relief sought in your Demand for Arbitration was frivolous or was brought for an improper purpose (as measured by the standards set forth in Federal Rule of Civil Procedure 11(b)).

G.   Severability and Survival.

If any portion of this Arbitration Agreement is found to be unenforceable or unlawful for any reason, (1) the unenforceable or unlawful provision shall be severed from these Terms; (2) severance of the unenforceable or unlawful provision shall have no impact whatsoever on the remainder of the Arbitration Agreement or the parties’ ability to compel arbitration of any remaining claims on an individual basis pursuant to the Arbitration Agreement; and (3) to the extent that any claims must therefore proceed on a class, collective, consolidated, or representative basis, such claims must be litigated in a civil court of competent jurisdiction and not in arbitration, and the parties agree that litigation of those claims shall be stayed pending the outcome of any individual claims in arbitration.

About GoodRx Pharmacy Mode

GoodRx Pharmacy Mode offers prescription discounts and coupons and prescription pricing information that are free to access for healthcare providers (“Pharmacy Mode”). As part of Pharmacy Mode, you can also send coupons directly to patients via text or email. By accessing or using Pharmacy Mode, you confirm your agreement to be bound by these Terms.

GoodRx cannot be combined with federal or state-funded programs like Medicare or Medicaid. GoodRx prescription discounts and coupons are NOT health insurance. GoodRx prescription discounts and coupons can be used instead of, but not in conjunction with, health insurance.  GoodRx prescription discounts and coupons are not intended as a substitute for health insurance. The range of the discounts will vary depending on the prescription drug and the participating pharmacy. GoodRx only provides discounts from participating pharmacies. Patients are required to pay for all prescription drugs at the time of purchase. GOODRX PRESCRIPTION DISCOUNTS AND COUPONS DO NOT MEET MINIMUM CREDITABLE COVERAGE REQUIREMENTS UNDER STATE HEALTHCARE COVERAGE REGULATIONS.

Please note that cash payments that patients make for medications using GoodRx prescription discounts and coupons may not count toward the patient’s prescription drug coverage out of pocket costs, but patients can confirm that by contacting the patient’s own coverage provider. If the patient is a Medicare Part D beneficiary, cash payments made using GoodRx prescription discounts and coupons will not count toward the patient’s Medicare Part D cost-sharing obligation. GoodRx cannot be combined with federal or state-funded programs like Medicare or Medicaid.

COLORADO:

By participating in the GoodRx Rewards Program (the “Program”), you agree to the collection, use, processing, and sharing of your sensitive data to provide the Program described in our Loyalty Program Privacy Notice. 

 This Program offers GoodRx Rewards Points based on certain actions you take, including using certain GoodRx coupons and managing prescription-related reminders and actions. To provide this Program, we will need to process your prescription information, which is considered “sensitive data” under the Colorado Privacy Act to the extent it reveals a mental/physical health condition or diagnosis. We process your sensitive data to provide this Program and in accordance with our Privacy Policy. We require sensitive data to provide the Program benefits, such as rewarding you with GoodRx Reward Points for your prescriptions. You may withdraw from the Program anytime by deleting your account within your account settings.

 

VIRGINIA:

If you choose to use products or services offered by GoodRx, Inc. and its parents, subsidiaries, and affiliates (collectively, “GoodRx,” “we,” “our,” or “us”) that may involve sexual or reproductive health data as defined under applicable law, your use of those services constitutes affirmative consent for us to process your sexual or reproductive health data in accordance with the Privacy Policy.

We process this data as necessary to provide or improve the product or service you have requested. For example, we may process your sexual or reproductive health data to:

  • Fulfill your request (such as applying a coupon or delivering a prescription),
  • Respond to inquiries or provide you with requested customer support,
  • Improve or troubleshoot product performance,
  • Maintain the security and integrity of our systems, or
  • Satisfy audit, legal, or compliance obligations.

We may also share this data in limited circumstances, such as:

  • In connection with legal obligations or audits,
  • In the event of a corporate transaction (such as a merger or acquisition), or
  • To protect the rights, safety, or integrity of GoodRx, our users, or others.

For more information on how we use and disclose personal data, please review our full Privacy Policy. We do not use your sexual or reproductive health data for advertising or marketing uses without your separate, explicit consent. 

This consent applies to GoodRx products or services that include or incorporate this consent language and involve the processing of sexual or reproductive health data. These may include, but are not limited to:

  • Coupons: Your use, saving, or sharing of a coupon related to sexual or reproductive health constitutes your consent for us to process such data to provide you with that coupon and facilitate any related claims or transactions. You can choose to remove a saved coupon at any time via your account settings. 
  • Gold Member Card Coupon: When you use a Gold member card coupon at the pharmacy for a sexual or reproductive health purpose, you consent to the processing of sexual or reproductive health data that may be involved in managing your membership and fulfilling your request and facilitating Gold member prescription discounts and claims. You may opt out of Gold by cancelling your subscription via your account settings. 
  • Rewards: Participation in our Rewards program, including the accrual and redemption of points, may involve processing of sexual or reproductive health data when such rewards relate to eligible transactions involving sexual or reproductive health products. You may opt out of Rewards at any time via your account settings. 
  • Gold Home Delivery: If you request home delivery of medications that may involve sexual or reproductive health, you consent to our use of that data for order fulfillment and delivery.

Your choices to opt out are described above. Because many of these services are fulfilled immediately and on a one-time basis (e.g., coupon use), withdrawal of consent applies only to future uses. How to Register for a Free GoodRx Account

Creating a GoodRx account is free and easy. Learn how to create a free GoodRx account to manage your prescriptions, earn Rewards points, and more!

Registering for a GoodRx account on the GoodRx mobile app (iOS or Android)

  1. Download the GoodRx mobile app for free (if you haven’t already)

  2. Open the app

  3. Tap the ‘Account’ icon

  4. Tap ‘Create your free account’

  5. Enter your email address and tap ‘Next’

    • Leave the ‘Earn rewards with GoodRx’ box checked to enroll in GoodRx Rewards

  6. Enter the 6-digit  verification code sent to your email address

  7. Enter the required information to complete your profile and then hit ‘Finish’

    • First name

    • Last name

    • Email address

    • **Birth Date

      **

  8. Congratulations! You now have a free GoodRx account

  4. Congratulations! You now have a free GoodRx account

What is GoodRx Gold? How does it work?

    • Plan types & pricing

What is GoodRx Gold?

GoodRx Gold is a membership program which offers prescription savings of up to 90% along with discounted telehealth visits from GoodRx Care, a free home delivery option, and more! 

GoodRx Gold offers two different plan types and billing cycle options to best fit your needs

Plan Types & Pricing
  • Individual Plan 
    •  $9.99 per month or $89.99 per year with an annual membership
  • Family Plan - Add up to five additional members (family, friends, pets)
    • $19.99 per month or $179.99 per year with an annual membership

For more information on GoodRx Gold plans, visit the Free GoodRx vs Gold Comparison Guide 

How does GoodRx Gold Work?

As a Gold member, just provide the pharmacy with your Gold Card information when you go to purchase your prescription from a Gold-participating pharmacy in order to take advantage of your membership savings.  

What Pharmacies Accept GoodRx Gold discounts?

GoodRx Gold is accepted at over 38,000 pharmacy locations nationwide. To find a Gold-participating pharmacy near you, visit our Gold Pharmacy Directory

Sign up for a free 30-day trial of Gold today.

Unsubscribe

How do I unsubscribe from emails?

There are multiple ways that you can unsubscribe from GoodRx emails. 

 

Method 1: If you don’t have a GoodRx account and would like to unsubscribe from marketing emails, please click the “unsubscribe” link at the bottom of the email you received from GoodRx.

 

Method 2: Otherwise from your Account Settings page, you can choose which emails (along with other notification types) you want to receive.

  1.  Log in and click the Profile icon located at the top right of the page.

  2. Select Account from the dropdown menu

  3. Select Communications Preferences

  4. Under Email preferences, check the box next to each type of email they wish to receive and click Save Changes  

    • To unsubscribe from all emails, click the Unsubscribe from All button at the bottom

I want to opt out of GoodRx mobile push notifications

Push notifications are messages that the GoodRx app sends to your mobile device. You may receive notifications for the following:

  • Refill Reminders
  • Price Alerts
  • Savings & News
  • General Notifications

You can control which push notifications you receive from your notification settings directly in the app.

  1. Open the GoodRx app
  2. Tap “Settings” on the bottom right of the screen
  3. Under the Alerts section, switch the toggle next to each option you’d like to receive notifications for.

I want to opt out of receiving text messages

To opt out of reminder and survey notifications, text PASS in response to the message.

To opt out of receiving all other texts, including coupons from GoodRx, text STOP to 466379 (GOODRX).

I’m a healthcare practitioner and I want to opt out of GoodRx mailings, how do I do that?

To no longer receive packages of bulk pharmacy discount cards or Patient Savings Kits from GoodRx, please click here to fill out a short form with the information on the shipping label.

If you are a physician, please enter your NPI number in the designated area of the form to ensure you are properly removed from receiving any future mailings. 

If you are an individual who has received a discount card or envelope in the mail, please Do Not fill out this form.Other Mailings

If you would like to no longer receive envelopes or postcard mailing from GoodRx, please click here to fill out a short form with the information on the shipping label.

Our deliveries take up to 4 weeks to arrive, so if any shipments were sent prior to your request, you may still receive them. Once you submit your information, no more shipments will be sent to you.

Updating Account Information

Can I change my billing date?

At this time, Gold members are unable to change their billing date as it is tied to the date you registered for Gold. If you would like to adjust your billing date, you must cancel and resubscribe.

How can I add more members to my Gold Family plan?

  1.  Log in to your account on the GoodRx app.
  2. Once logged in, tap GoodRx Gold Family in your Account Settings.                                                                                                             
  3. Select ‘Add a Member’ and enter the member’s name and birthdate.

How can I switch my plan type?

There are two ways to switch your plan type – on the GoodRx mobile app and on the GoodRx Gold website. 

Switching Plans on the GoodRx app

  1. Log in to the GoodRx app and tap Account.
  2. Next to GoodRx Gold, click Manage                                   
  3. Under Your current plan, tap Change >                         
  4. Under Select a new plan, choose Billed monthly or Billed annually and Individual or Family
  5. Click Next
  6. Review your plan and tap Confirm to finalize your plan change

 

Switching Plans on the GoodRx website

  1. Log in and click the Profile icon located at the top right of the page. 

  2. Select ’Account’ from the dropdown menu

  3. Select ‘Plan Information’ and click ’Change’ to update your plan type.

  4. From this menu you can update your plan type (Individual or Family) and adjust your billing cycle (monthly or annual). 

  5. Click ‘Next’ to proceed and review the updated information for your membership. 

  6. Review your updated Gold membership information and select ‘Confirm’ to proceed with these changes.

How do I cancel my Gold membership?

You can cancel your account at any time by following these quick steps: 

  • Canceling your account in the app
  • Canceling your account online

Canceling Your Gold Account in the GoodRx Mobile App

  1. Log in to the GoodRx Mobile App and tap the Account tab.  
  2. Next, click Gold Membership  
  3. Click Cancel my GoodRx Gold membership  
  4. Confirm that you want to cancel your Gold membership by clicking Continue cancellation   

   

Canceling Your Gold Account on the GoodRx Gold Website

  1. Log in to your Gold account at goodrx.com/gold                                       
  2.  Click on the profile icon in the upper right corner   
  3. Click the Account option                                     
  4. Select GoldMembership   
  5. Click Cancel my Gold Membership  
  6. If you’re sure you want to cancel your Gold membership, click ‘Yes, cancel anyway’  

Done!

If you’ve signed up for a GoodRx account, you can update your name or birthdate at any time by logging in to your GoodRx account online at GoodRx.com or via the GoodRx mobile app.  

Please note: The email address connected to your GoodRx account cannot be updated. 

  • If you need to use a different email address to access your GoodRx account, sign up for a new account using your preferred email address. 

 

How to update your name or date of birth on GoodRx.com

  1. Log into your GoodRx account at GoodRx.com
  2. Click the account / menu icon in the upper right corner
  3. Click Account
  4. Click Personal Information
  5. Click Edit to change your name or date of birth
    • Note: Your name and/or date of birth should match your prescription information
  6. Click Update to save any changes

How to update your name or date of birth on the GoodRx mobile app

  1. Open the GoodRx app
  2. Tap Account
  3. Tap Edit Profile
  4. Update your name and/or birthdate
    • Note: Your name and/or date of birth should match your prescription information
  5. Click Save to save any changes

 

For instructions on updating information for Gold family plan members, see How do I update a member’s name or birthdate?

Website & App Navigation

How do I access my GoodRx Gold card?

Your GoodRx Gold card is available digitally through the web or the mobile app. 

  • GoodRx Gold card on the web
  • GoodRx Gold card on the app

GoodRx Gold Card on the web Step 1: Log in and click the “Profile” icon located at the top right of the page.  

Step 2: Select ‘Account’ or ‘Dashboard’ from the dropdown menu.

Step 3: Select ‘Gold Card’ at the top right of the page

Step 4: If you’re at a CVS or Kroger pharmacy select the ‘For CVS or Kroger Pharmacy’ view, otherwise select the ‘For all other pharmacies view’ 

 

GoodRx Gold on the appStep 1: Click the ‘Home’ tab at the bottom of the app

Step 2: Press ‘Gold Card’ at the top right of the page

Step 3: If you’re at a CVS or Kroger pharmacy select the ‘For CVS or Kroger Pharmacy’ view, otherwise select the ‘For all other pharmacies view’

Logging in to your GoodRx account

To log in to your existing free GoodRx account,  you will need to enter the email address (or, in some cases, phone number) connected  to your GoodRx account, then enter the verification code sent from (or via text to your phone number) when prompted. 

Note: GoodRx Gold Members and Companion members must use an email address to log in to their account 

  • Logging into your account on the GoodRx website
  • Logging into your account on the GoodRx mobile app
  • Verification code issues 

 

 

Logging into your account on the GoodRx website

  1. Go to GoodRx.com

  2. Click the Sign up / Sign In button 

  3. If you already have an account click Sign In

  • If you don’t have an account already, click Sign up for free)

  1. Enter your email address in the email address field

  2. Click Next

  3. Check your email inbox for a 6-digit verification code from GoodRx

  4. Enter the 6-digit code in the field provided

  5. Click Verify to log into your GoodRx account

  • If you encounter issues with receiving or entering the verification code, click here

 

Logging into your account on the GoodRx Mobile App

  1. Open or download the GoodRx mobile appon your mobile device

  2. Click the Sign In button

  3. Enter your email address in the email address field

  4. Click Next

  5. Check your email inbox for a 6-digit verification code from GoodRx

  6. Enter the 6-digit code in the field provided

  7. Click Verify to log into your GoodRx account

  • If you encounter issues with receiving or entering the verification code, click here

 

 

Verification Code Issues

  • Didn’t receive verification code basic troubleshooting
  • Verification code is incorrect
Didn’t receive verification code basic troubleshooting

If you didn’t receive your verification code via email, try the following:

 

  1. Confirm that you’re checking the same email address that’s connected to your GoodRx account

  2. Check your spam and junk mail folders

  3. Try checking your email on a different device

  • e.g. If you’re checking your email on your phone, try checking on your computer (or vice versa)

  1. Click Send new code

  2. If you still can’t find the email, follow the steps below

  • Didn’t receive verification code (web)
  • Didn’t receive verification code (app)

 

 

 

Didn’t receive verification code (web)

 

  1. Refresh the page

  2. Close and re-open your web browser

  3. Clear your browser’s cache/cookies/history ( see how-to links below)

  • Clear cache & cookies in Google Chrome
  • Clear cache in FIrefox
  • Clear cookies in Safari
  • Clear cache in yahoo

  1. Log out and log back in to your email inbox

  2. Try logging intoyour GoodRx accountagain

 

 

 

 

 

 

 

Didn’t receive verification code (app)

 

  1. Fully close and quit the app, then reopen

  2. Ensure your app is up-to-date

  • Check the Apple Store or Google Play Store to see if an updated version of the GoodRx app is available

  1. Uninstall and reinstall the app on your mobile device

  2. Try logging in again 

 

 

 

 

 

 

 

Verification code is incorrect

 

  1. Ensure that you entered the same 6-digit verification code that was sent to you

  2. Re-enter the verification code exactly as is and click Verify once more

  3. Click Send new code and repeat steps 1-3

  • Check the time stamps on the email to make sure you’re entering the most recently sent verification code

Medicine Cabinet

With Medicine Cabinet, you can save and manage your prescriptions all from the Home tab on the GoodRx app! With Medicine Cabinet you can feel confident. 

  • Save and manage prescriptions
  • Set reminders to take your medication on time
  • Receive alerts for your refills
  • Earn rewards points

Saving & Managing Prescriptions

You can use Medicine Cabinet to keep track of your medications! You can manually add prescriptions from the Home tab or when searching from coupons. Additionally, any coupons that you use that we’re able to link to your account will also be added.

 

Adding medications from the Dashboard (Home) tab of the app
  1. Click the Add button next to the Medicine cabinet section on the Home screen of the app
  2. Search for your prescription and review prescription details
  3. Select the image that matches your medication
  4. Medication added to Medicine Cabinet

 

Saving a prescription when searching for coupons
Managing prescriptions

If you need to delete, edit, or change a medication to ‘inactive’ you can do so within Medicine Cabinet:

  1. Tap the 3 dots next to the medication name and dosage
  2. Select the action you want to take 
  3. [If editing prescription details] Modify the details of your prescription on the ‘Edit prescription details’ screen
  4. Tap ‘Update my medicine cabinet’

Daily Reminders

You can also use Medicine Cabinet to set up reminders to take your medication at specific times:

  1. On the prescription details page, click ‘Schedule a reminder’
  2. Select the time of day or custom reminder
  3. Add reminder details such as weekly schedule, reminder time, and any additional saved medications to be reminded of on the same schedule 

 

Refill Reminders

We’ve got your back for critical reminders like remembering to refill your prescriptions!

  1. Select the medication in the medicine cabinet
  2. Tap ‘Get refill reminders’
  3. Make sure the ‘Reminders’ toggle is on
  4. Select the next pickup date for your medication
  5. Select the refill frequency

 

GoodRx Rewards

Users with free GoodRx accounts can earn points for healthy habits–like using a GoodRx coupon when picking up their prescription at the pharmacy.

 

You can redeem points for gift cards or discounts on eligible prescriptions. Learn more about GoodRx Rewards here! 

 

 

Download the GoodRx app now so you can get started today!

Why are brand name drugs expensive with Gold?

Since GoodRx Gold is not insurance, prices may still be high for brand-name prescriptions. Our discounted prices will be lower than if you just paid cash, but they can still be expensive. 

Don’t worry—there are other ways for you to save! If you don’t find a reasonable price using GoodRx Gold, we encourage you to search for your prescription on GoodRx.com. Scroll down and see information about manufacturer coupons, patient assistance programs, and other ways to  significantly reduce the cost of brand-name prescriptions.

Weight Loss

Can I change my dosage on my medication? (Weight Loss)

Until you reach a steady state dosage, your clinician will decide during your monthly followup appointments whether it’s time to increase your dosage. If the clinician approves an increased dosage, your prescription will be updated accordingly.

Can I change the date on my next order? (Weight Loss)

If you want to track or make any changes to your GLP-1 shipments, you can contact your pharmacy directly. For Zepbound Vials, call (855) 723-4672. They can help you update your shipping address, your billing information and track the status of your order.

Can I transfer my Care Direct prescription? (Weight Loss)

If you are taking Zepbound vials from Lilly Direct, that Rx cannot be transferred to a retail pharmacy. If you are taking Wegovy or Zepbound pens and would like to transfer from one pharmacy to another, call your pharmacy to make the transfer, or call our support team to help facilitate the transfer.

How do I make changes to my upcoming shipments? (Weight Loss)

If you want to track or make any changes to your GLP-1 shipments, you can contact your pharmacy directly.For Zepbound Vials, call (855) 723-4672. They can help you update your shipping address, your billing information and track the status of your order.

How do I renew my prescription? (Weight Loss)

You will get a new prescription every 28 days during your monthly follow-up visits. Once you reach a steady state dosage, these visits will be every 90 days. These visits are required to get a new prescription and avoid any lapse in refills.

How do I request a refill for my prescription? (Weight Loss)

Every month, you’ll receive a prompt when you log-in at GoodRx.com. You will be guided to complete a telehealth visit and message a clinician. Once your clinician reviews and approves your refill, your pharmacy will receive an updated prescription. If you are filling by mail, the pharmacy will reach out to you to complete payment for your refill. If you are filling at your local pharmacy, you’ll receive a notification from the store when your refill is ready for pickup. You can also call your local pharmacy for status updates.

How do I update my shipping address? (Weight Loss)

If you want to track or make any changes to your GLP-1 shipments, you can contact your pharmacy directly. For Zepbound Vials, call (855) 723-4672. They can help you update your shipping address, your billing information and track the status of your order.

How does shipping work? (Weight Loss)

Your mail order pharmacy will reach out to you when they receive your prescription each month. Once you provide your payment information, they will ship out your order within 2-3 business days. If you are using Zepbound Vials, your pharmacy is LillyDirect.

When is my next delivery? (Weight Loss)

If you want to track or make any changes to your GLP-1 shipments, you can contact your pharmacy directly. For Zepbound Vials, call (855) 723-4672. They can help you update your shipping address, your billing information and track the status of your order.

When will I be charged? (Weight Loss)

  • Initial Charge: You’ll be charged only after your clinician approves your prescription and your first fill is shipped. Once you complete your initial onboarding (i.e. enter your payment information).
  • Future Charges: After that, you’ll be billed every 30 days in advance of your telehealth visit. You will pay for your GLP-1 medication separately, either through a mail order pharmacy or at your local retailer. These charges are independent of your monthly telehealth charge.

Where is GoodRx Care Direct Available? (Weight Loss)

GoodRx Care Direct weight loss treatment is available in all U.S. states except Alabama, and Louisiana.

Where is my order? (Weight Loss)

If you want to track or make any changes to your GLP-1 shipments, you can contact your pharmacy directly.. For Zepbound VIals, call (855) 723-4672. They can help you update your shipping address, your billing information and track the status of your order.

What is Home Delivery?

Do I need a prescription to use GoodRx Gold Home Delivery?

Yes, all GoodRx Gold Home Delivery orders require a valid prescription. Please note, prescriptions obtained through GoodRx Care are not currently eligible for Gold Home Delivery.

Do you deliver medications that require climate-controlled packaging?

Unfortunately, we currently cannot deliver any medications that require climate-controlled packaging, such as insulin.

Does GoodRx deliver my prescriptions?

No. Our mail delivery pharmacy partner, Fuze Health, is a trusted American pharmacy that delivers high-quality prescription medications. 

Fuze Health is fully accredited by URAC and NABP, and legally bound to protect patient confidentiality.

How does GoodRx Gold Home Delivery work?

With GoodRx Gold Home Delivery, you can order your prescriptions from the comfort of your home and get them delivered with free shipping!

If delivery is available for your prescription, you will see “Gold Mail Delivery” as an option on the medication’s price page in the GoodRx app, or our website.

Is GoodRx Gold Home Delivery available where I live?

GoodRx Gold Home Delivery is available in 49 states and the District of Columbia, excluding Washington state.

What medications are available for GoodRx Gold Home Delivery?

Nearly 1,000 common medications are available to order through GoodRx Gold Home Delivery. 

These include medications that are used to treat high blood pressure, high cholesterol, anxiety, depression, erectile dysfunction, and many more.

Some of the top medications offered through Gold Home Delivery include:

  • atorvastatin
  • rosuvastatin
  • losartan
  • lisinopril
  • amlodipine
  • escitalopram
  • bupropion XL
  • sildenafil
  • tadalafil
  • omeprazole

Where are my medications coming from?

GoodRx only works with licensed American pharmacies to deliver the same high-quality medications provided by your local pharmacy. We work tirelessly with our pharmacy network to bring you low prices and free shipping.
